## CONTEXT

A plan was developed to refactor a target that suffers from a specific type of code smell. The plan consists of blocks, each of which is formed by a list of refactoring operations. The blocks were designed to perform the refactoring in steps, such that after the execution of each block, it will have a compilable sub-solution with functional tests. For this, both the source code mentioned in the plan and the related tests may need to be rewritten.

## TASK

Apply the staged refactoring block to the provided Java source files.

Preserving behavior does not require preserving the original target-class API when the plan explicitly moves methods or responsibilities to another class.

## CONSTRAINTS

- Modify only files listed in allowed_files field.
- Return the full new content for every modified file.
- Do not return partial patches.
- Do not edit unrelated tests.
- Do not change unrelated code.
- Do not undo previous refactorings.
- If the block cannot be safely applied, return an empty files_to_write list and explain the reason in "notes".
- Return only files that actually changed.
- Do not include unchanged files in files_to_write.
- Implement the refactoring operations requested in the current block. Do not replace the requested refactoring with a more conservative alternative.

          "org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequestLocationProximity"
        ],
        "details": "Create RouteRequestLocationProximity in the same package and move the geographic-envelope responsibility currently implemented by the private fromEnvelope and toEnvelope fields and getEnvelope(Coordinate, int). The new class should provide an operation that accepts the existing GenericLocation start and end values and a Vertex, returns the same boolean result as isCloseToStartOrEnd(Vertex), and internally uses Coordinate, Envelope, and SphericalDistanceLibrary. Update RouteRequest.isCloseToStartOrEnd(Vertex) to delegate to this helper while retaining its existing public signature and null checks. Remove fromEnvelope, toEnvelope, getEnvelope(Coordinate, int), and all direct Coordinate, Envelope, and SphericalDistanceLibrary references from RouteRequest. RouteRequest will introduce only the new RouteRequestLocationProximity dependency; GenericLocation and Vertex must remain because they are still used by the public API and other target methods. The expected distinct outgoing dependencies removed from RouteRequest are Coordinate, Envelope, and SphericalDistanceLibrary; the new helper dependency reduces the estimated Fan-Out by two net dependencies.",
        "risk": "medium"
      },

org.opentripplanner.util.time.DateUtils;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\n/**\n * A trip planning request. Some parameters may not be honored by the trip planner for some or all\n * itineraries.\n * <p>\n * All defaults should be specified here in the RoutingRequest, NOT as annotations on query\n * parameters in web services that create RoutingRequests. This establishes a priority chain for\n * default values: RoutingRequest field initializers, then JSON router config, then query\n * parameters.\n *\n * @Deprecated tag is added to all parameters that are not currently functional in either the Raptor\n * router or other non-transit routing (walk, bike, car etc.)\n * <p>\n * TODO OTP2 Many fields are deprecated in this class, the reason is documented in the\n *           RoutingResource class, not here. Eventually the field will be removed from this\n *           class, but we want to keep it in the RoutingResource as long as we support the\n *           REST API.\n */\npublic class RouteRequest implements Cloneable, Serializable {\n\n  private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(RouteRequest.class);\n\n  private static final long NOW_THRESHOLD_SEC = durationInSeconds(\"15h\");\n\n  /* FIELDS UNIQUELY IDENTIFYING AN SPT REQUEST */\n  /**\n   * How close to do you have to be to the start or end to be considered \"close\".\n   *\n   * @see RouteRequest#isCloseToStartOrEnd(Vertex)\n   * @see DominanceFunction#betterOrEqualAndComparable(State, State)\n   */\n  private static final int MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS = 500;\n  /** The complete list of incoming query parameters. */\n\n  public final HashMap<String, String> parameters = new HashMap<>();\n\n  /** Configure the transfer optimization */\n  public final TransferOptimizationParameters transferOptimization = new TransferOptimizationRequest();\n  /**\n   * Transit reluctance per mode. Use this to add a advantage(<1.0) to specific modes, or to add a\n   * penalty to other modes (> 1.0). The type used here it the internal model {@link TransitMode}\n   * make sure to create a mapping for this before using it on the API.\n   * <p>\n   * If set, the alight-slack-for-mode override the default value {@code 1.0}.\n   * <p>\n   * This is a scalar multiplied with the time in second on board the transit vehicle. Default value\n   * is not-set(empty map).\n   */\n  private final Map<TransitMode, Double> transitReluctanceForMode = new HashMap<>();\n\n  private GenericLocation from;\n\n  private GenericLocation to;\n\n  /**\n   * An ordered list of intermediate locations to be visited.\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 - Regression. Not currently working in OTP2. Must be re-implemented\n   * - using raptor.\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public List<GenericLocation> intermediatePlaces;\n\n  /**\n   * This is the maximum duration for a direct street search. This is a performance limit and should\n   * therefore be set high. Results close to the limit are not guaranteed to be optimal.\n   * Use filters to limit what is presented to the client.\n   *\n   * @see ItineraryListFilter\n   */\n  public Duration maxDirectStreetDuration = Duration.ofHours(4);\n\n  /**\n   * Override the settings in maxDirectStreetDuration for specific street modes. This is done\n   * because some street modes searches are much more resource intensive than others.\n   */\n  public Map<StreetMode, Duration> maxDirectStreetDurationForMode = new HashMap<>();\n\n  /**\n   * This is the maximum duration for access/egress street searches. This is a performance limit and\n   * should therefore be set high. Results close to the limit are not guaranteed to be optimal.\n   * Use filters to limit what is presented to the client.\n   *\n   * @see ItineraryListFilter\n   */\n  public Duration maxAccessEgressDuration = Duration.ofMinutes(45);\n  /**\n   * Override the settings in maxAccessEgressDuration for specific street modes. This is done\n   * because some street modes searches are much more resource intensive than others.\n   */\n  public Map<StreetMode, Duration> maxAccessEgressDurationForMode = new HashMap<>();\n  /**\n   * The access/egress/direct/transit modes allowed for this main request. The parameter\n   * \"streetSubRequestModes\" below is used for a single A Star sub request.\n   * <p>\n   * // TODO OTP2 Street routing requests should eventually be split into its own request class.\n   */\n  public RequestModes modes = RequestModes.defaultRequestModes();\n  /**\n   * The set of TraverseModes allowed when doing creating sub requests and doing street routing. //\n   * TODO OTP2 Street routing requests should eventually be split into its own request class.\n   */\n  public TraverseModeSet streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK); // defaults in constructor overwrite this\n  /**\n   * The set of characteristics that the user wants to optimize for -- defaults to SAFE.\n   */\n  public BicycleOptimizeType bicycleOptimizeType = BicycleOptimizeType.SAFE;\n\n  private Instant dateTime = Instant.now();\n\n  private Duration searchWindow;\n\n  /**\n   * The expected maximum time a journey can last across all possible journeys for the current\n   * deployment. Normally you would just do an estimate and add enough slack, so you are sure that\n   * there is no journeys that falls outside this window. The parameter is used find all possible\n   * dates for the journey and then search only the services which run on those dates. The duration\n   * must include access, egress, wait-time and transit time for the whole journey. It should also\n   * take low frequency days/periods like holidays into account. In other words, pick the two points\n   * within your area that has the worst connection and then try to travel on the worst possible\n   * day, and find the maximum journey duration. Using a value that is too high has the effect of\n   * including more patterns in the search, hence, making it a bit slower. Recommended values would\n   * be from 12 hours(small town/city), 1 day (region) to 2 days (country like Norway).\n   */\n  public Duration maxJourneyDuration = Duration.ofHours(24);\n\n  private PageCursor pageCursor;\n\n  private boolean timetableView = true;\n\n  private boolean arriveBy = false;\n\n  /**\n   * Whether the trip must be wheelchair-accessible and how strictly this should be interpreted.\n   */\n  @Nonnull\n  public WheelchairAccessibilityRequest wheelchairAccessibility =\n    WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.DEFAULT;\n\n  private int numItineraries = 50;\n\n  /**\n   * Human walk speed along streets, in meters per second.\n   * <p>\n   * Default: 1.33 m/s ~ 3mph, <a href=\"http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walking\">avg. human walk\n   * speed</a>\n   */\n  public double walkSpeed = 1.33;\n  /**\n   * Default: 5 m/s, ~11 mph, a random bicycling speed\n   */\n  public double bikeSpeed = 5;\n  /**\n   * Default: 1.33 m/s ~ Same as walkSpeed\n   */\n  public double bikeWalkingSpeed = 1.33;\n  /**\n   * Max car speed along streets, in meters per second.\n   * <p>\n   * Default: 40 m/s, 144 km/h, above the maximum (finite) driving speed limit worldwide.\n   */\n  public double carSpeed = 40.0;\n\n  private Locale locale = new Locale(\"en\", \"US\");\n\n  /**\n   * An extra penalty added on transfers (i.e. all boardings except the first one). Not to be\n   * confused with bikeBoardCost and walkBoardCost, which are the cost of boarding a vehicle with\n   * and without a bicycle. The boardCosts are used to model the 'usual' perceived cost of using a\n   * transit vehicle, and the transferCost is used when a user requests even less transfers. In the\n   * latter case, we don't actually optimize for fewest transfers, as this can lead to absurd\n   * results. Consider a trip in New York from Grand Army Plaza (the one in Brooklyn) to Kalustyan's\n   * at noon. The true lowest transfers route is to wait until midnight, when the 4 train runs local\n   * the whole way. The actual fastest route is the 2/3 to the 4/5 at Nevins to the 6 at Union\n   * Square, which takes half an hour. Even someone optimizing for fewest transfers doesn't want to\n   * wait until midnight. Maybe they would be willing to walk to 7th Ave and take the Q to Union\n   * Square, then transfer to the 6. If this takes less than optimize_transfer_penalty seconds, then\n   * that's what we'll return.\n   */\n  public int transferCost = 0;\n\n  /**\n   * Penalty for using a non-preferred transfer\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Regression. Not currently working in OTP2. We might not implement the\n   * old functionality the same way, but we will try to map this parameter\n   * so it does work similar as before.\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public int nonpreferredTransferCost = 180;\n\n  /**\n   * A multiplier for how bad walking is, compared to being in transit for equal\n   * lengths of time. Empirically, values between 2 and 4 seem to correspond\n   * well to the concept of not wanting to walk too much without asking for\n   * totally ridiculous itineraries, but this observation should in no way be\n   * taken as scientific or definitive. Your mileage may vary. See\n   * https://github.com/opentripplanner/OpenTripPlanner/issues/4090 for impact on\n   * performance with high values. Default value: 2.0\n   */\n  public double walkReluctance = 2.0;\n  public double bikeWalkingReluctance = 5.0;\n  public double bikeReluctance = 2.0;\n  public double carReluctance = 2.0;\n  /**\n   * How much more time does it take to walk a flight of stairs compared to walking a similar\n   * horizontal length\n   * <p>\n   * Default value is based on: Fujiyama, T., & Tyler, N. (2010). Predicting the walking speed of\n   * pedestrians on stairs. Transportation Planning and Technology, 33(2), 177–202.\n   */\n  public double stairsTimeFactor = 3.0;\n  /** Used instead of walk reluctance for stairs */\n  public double stairsReluctance = 2.0;\n  /** Multiplicative factor on expected turning time. */\n  public double turnReluctance = 1.0;\n  /**\n   * How long does it take to get an elevator, on average (actually, it probably should be a bit\n   * *more* than average, to prevent optimistic trips)? Setting it to \"seems like forever,\" while\n   * accurate, will probably prevent OTP from working correctly.\n   */\n  // TODO: how long does it /really/ take to get an elevator?\n  public int elevatorBoardTime = 90;\n  /** What is the cost of boarding an elevator? */\n  public int elevatorBoardCost = 90;\n  /** How long does it take to advance one floor on an elevator? */\n  public int elevatorHopTime = 20;\n\n  // it is assumed that getting off an elevator is completely free\n  /** What is the cost of travelling one floor on an elevator? */\n  public int elevatorHopCost = 20;\n  /** Time to get on and off your own bike */\n  public int bikeSwitchTime;\n  /** Cost of getting on and off your own bike */\n  public int bikeSwitchCost;\n  /** Time to rent a vehicle */\n  public int vehicleRentalPickupTime = 60;\n  /**\n   * Cost of renting a vehicle. The cost is a bit more than actual time to model the associated cost\n   * and trouble.\n   */\n  public int vehicleRentalPickupCost = 120;\n  /** Time to drop-off a rented vehicle */\n  public int vehicleRentalDropoffTime = 30;\n  /** Cost of dropping-off a rented vehicle */\n  public int vehicleRentalDropoffCost = 30;\n  /** The vehicle rental networks which may be used. If empty all networks may be used. */\n  public Set<String> allowedVehicleRentalNetworks = Set.of();\n  /** The vehicle rental networks which may not be used. If empty, no networks are banned. */\n  public Set<String> bannedVehicleRentalNetworks = Set.of();\n  /** Time to park a bike */\n  public int bikeParkTime = 60;\n  /** Cost of parking a bike. */\n  public int bikeParkCost = 120;\n  /** Time to park a car */\n  public int carParkTime = 60;\n  /** Cost of parking a car. */\n  public int carParkCost = 120;\n  /** Tags which are required to use a vehicle parking. If empty, no tags are required. */\n  public Set<String> requiredVehicleParkingTags = Set.of();\n  /** Tags with which a vehicle parking will not be used. If empty, no tags are banned. */\n  public Set<String> bannedVehicleParkingTags = Set.of();\n  /**\n   * Time to park a car in a park and ride, w/o taking into account driving and walking cost (time\n   * to park, switch off, pick your stuff, lock the car, etc...)\n   */\n  public int carDropoffTime = 120;\n  /** Time of getting in/out of a carPickup (taxi) */\n  public int carPickupTime = 60;\n  /** Cost of getting in/out of a carPickup (taxi) */\n  public int carPickupCost = 120;\n  /**\n   * How much worse is waiting for a transit vehicle than being on a transit vehicle, as a\n   * multiplier. The default value treats wait and on-vehicle time as the same.\n   * <p>\n   * It may be tempting to set this higher than walkReluctance (as studies often find this kind of\n   * preferences among riders) but the planner will take this literally and walk down a transit line\n   * to avoid waiting at a stop. This used to be set less than 1 (0.95) which would make waiting\n   * offboard preferable to waiting onboard in an interlined trip. That is also undesirable.\n   * <p>\n   * If we only tried the shortest possible transfer at each stop to neighboring stop patterns, this\n   * problem could disappear.\n   */\n  public double waitReluctance = 1.0;\n\n  /**\n   * How much less bad is waiting at the beginning of the trip (replaces waitReluctance on the first\n   * boarding)\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Probably a regression, but I'm not sure it worked correctly in OTP 1.X\n   * either. It could be a part of itinerary-filtering after a Raptor search.\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public double waitAtBeginningFactor = 0.4;\n\n  /**\n   * This prevents unnecessary transfers by adding a cost for boarding a vehicle. This is in\n   * addition to the cost of the transfer(walking) and waiting-time. It is also in addition to the\n   * {@link #transferCost}.\n   */\n  public int walkBoardCost = 60 * 10;\n  /**\n   * Separate cost for boarding a vehicle with a bicycle, which is more difficult than on foot. This\n   * is in addition to the cost of the transfer(biking) and waiting-time. It is also in addition to\n   * the {@link #transferCost}.\n   */\n  public int bikeBoardCost = 60 * 10;\n  /**\n   * Factor for how much the walk safety is considered in routing. Value should be between 0 and 1.\n   * If the value is set to be 0, safety is ignored.\n   */\n  public double walkSafetyFactor = 1.0;\n  /**\n   * Do not use certain named agencies\n   */\n  private Set<FeedScopedId> bannedAgencies = Set.of();\n  /**\n   * Only use certain named agencies\n   */\n  private Set<FeedScopedId> whiteListedAgencies = Set.of();\n\n  /**\n   * Set of preferred agencies by user.\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  private Set<FeedScopedId> preferredAgencies = Set.of();\n\n  /**\n   * Set of unpreferred agencies for given user.\n   */\n  private Set<FeedScopedId> unpreferredAgencies = Set.of();\n\n  /**\n   * Do not use certain named routes. The paramter format is: feedId_routeId,feedId_routeId,feedId_routeId\n   * This parameter format is completely nonstandard and should be revised for the 2.0 API, see\n   * issue #1671.\n   */\n  private RouteMatcher bannedRoutes = RouteMatcher.emptyMatcher();\n  /**\n   * Only use certain named routes\n   */\n  private RouteMatcher whiteListedRoutes = RouteMatcher.emptyMatcher();\n\n  /**\n   * Set of preferred routes by user and configuration.\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Needs to be implemented\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public List<FeedScopedId> preferredRoutes = List.of();\n\n  /**\n   * Penalty added for using every route that is not preferred if user set any route as preferred.\n   * We return number of seconds that we are willing to wait for preferred route.\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Needs to be implemented\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public int otherThanPreferredRoutesPenalty = 300;\n\n  /**\n   * Set of unpreferred routes for given user and configuration.\n   */\n  private Set<FeedScopedId> unpreferredRoutes = Set.of();\n\n  /**\n   * A cost function used to calculate penalty for an unpreferred route or agency. Function should\n   * return number of seconds that we are willing to wait for preferred route.\n   */\n  public DoubleFunction<Double> unpreferredCost = RequestFunctions.createLinearFunction(\n    0.0,\n    DEFAULT_ROUTE_RELUCTANCE\n  );\n\n  /**\n   * Do not use certain trips\n   */\n  public Set<FeedScopedId> bannedTrips = Set.of();\n  /**\n   * A global minimum transfer time (in seconds) that specifies the minimum amount of time that must\n   * pass between exiting one transit vehicle and boarding another. This time is in addition to time\n   * it might take to walk between transit stops, the {@link #alightSlack}, and the {@link\n   * #boardSlack}. This time should also be overridden by specific transfer timing information in\n   * transfers.txt\n   * <p>\n   * This only apply to transfers between two trips, it does not apply when boarding the first\n   * transit.\n   * <p>\n   * Unit is seconds. Default value is 2 minutes.\n   */\n  public int transferSlack = 120;\n  /**\n   * The number of seconds to add before boarding a transit leg. It is recommended to use the\n   * `boardTimes` in the `router-config.json` to set this for each mode.\n   * <p>\n   * Unit is seconds. Default value is 0.\n   */\n  public int boardSlack = 0;\n  /**\n   * Has information how much time boarding a vehicle takes. Can be significant eg in airplanes or\n   * ferries.\n   * <p>\n   * If set, the board-slack-for-mode override the more general {@link #boardSlack}. This enables\n   * configuring the board-slack for airplane boarding to be 30 minutes and a slack for bus of 2\n   * minutes.\n   * <p>\n   * Unit is seconds. Default value is not-set(empty map).\n   */\n  public Map<TransitMode, Integer> boardSlackForMode = new EnumMap<>(TransitMode.class);\n  /**\n   * The number of seconds to add after alighting a transit leg. It is recommended to use the\n   * `alightTimes` in the `router-config.json` to set this for each mode.\n   * <p>\n   * Unit is seconds. Default value is 0.\n   */\n  public int alightSlack = 0;\n  /**\n   * Has information how much time alighting a vehicle takes. Can be significant eg in airplanes or\n   * ferries.\n   * <p>\n   * If set, the alight-slack-for-mode override the more general {@link #alightSlack}. This enables\n   * configuring the alight-slack for train alighting to be 4 minutes and a bus alight slack to be 0\n   * minutes.\n   * <p>\n   * Unit is seconds. Default value is not-set(empty map).\n   */\n  public Map<TransitMode, Integer> alightSlackForMode = new EnumMap<>(TransitMode.class);\n  /**\n   * Ideally maxTransfers should be set in the router config, not here. Instead the client should be\n   * able to pass in a parameter for the max number of additional/extra transfers relative to the\n   * best trip (with the fewest possible transfers) within constraint of the other search\n   * parameters(TODO OTP2 Expose {@link org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.api.request.SearchParams#numberOfAdditionalTransfers()}\n   * in APIs). This might be to complicated to explain to the customer, so we might stick to the old\n   * limit, but that have side-effects that you might not find any trips on a day where a critical\n   * part of the trip is not available, because of some real-time disruption.\n   * <p>\n   * See https://github.com/opentripplanner/OpenTripPlanner/issues/2886\n   */\n  public Integer maxTransfers = 12;\n  /**\n   * For the bike triangle, how important time is. triangleTimeFactor+triangleSlopeFactor+triangleSafetyFactor\n   * == 1\n   */\n  public double bikeTriangleTimeFactor;\n  /** For the bike triangle, how important slope is */\n  public double bikeTriangleSlopeFactor;\n  /** For the bike triangle, how important safety is */\n  public double bikeTriangleSafetyFactor;\n  /**\n   * Whether or not vehicle rental availability information will be used to plan vehicle rental\n   * trips\n   */\n  public boolean useVehicleRentalAvailabilityInformation = false;\n  /**\n   * Whether arriving at the destination with a rented (station) bicycle is allowed without dropping\n   * it off.\n   *\n   * @see RouteRequest#keepingRentedVehicleAtDestinationCost\n   * @see VehicleRentalStation#isKeepingVehicleRentalAtDestinationAllowed\n   */\n  public boolean allowKeepingRentedVehicleAtDestination = false;\n  /**\n   * The cost of arriving at the destination with the rented bicycle, to discourage doing so.\n   *\n   * @see RouteRequest#allowKeepingRentedVehicleAtDestination\n   */\n  public double keepingRentedVehicleAtDestinationCost = 0;\n  /**\n   * The deceleration speed of an automobile, in meters per second per second.\n   */\n  // 2.9 m/s/s: 65 mph - 0 mph in 10 seconds\n  public double carDecelerationSpeed = 2.9;\n  /**\n   * The acceleration speed of an automobile, in meters per second per second.\n   */\n  // 2.9 m/s/s: 0 mph to 65 mph in 10 seconds\n  public double carAccelerationSpeed = 2.9;\n  /**\n   * When true, realtime updates are ignored during this search.\n   */\n  public boolean ignoreRealtimeUpdates = false;\n  /**\n   * When true, trips cancelled in scheduled data are included in this search.\n   */\n  public boolean includePlannedCancellations = false;\n\n  /**\n   * A transit stop that this trip must start from\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Is this in use, what is is used for. It seems to overlap with\n   * the fromPlace parameter. Is is used for onBoard routing only?\n   */\n  @Deprecated\n  public FeedScopedId startingTransitStopId;\n\n  /**\n   * A trip where this trip must start from (depart-onboard routing)\n   *\n   * @deprecated TODO OTP2 Regression. Not currently working in OTP2. set of allowed modes.\n   */\n  public void addMode(TraverseMode mode) {\n    streetSubRequestModes.setMode(mode, true);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * The epoch date/time in seconds that the trip should depart (or arrive, for requests where\n   * arriveBy is true)\n   * <p>\n   * The search time for the current request. If the client have moved to the next page then this is\n   * the adjusted search time - the dateTime passed in is ignored and replaced with by a time from\n   * the pageToken.\n   */\n  public Instant dateTime() {\n    return dateTime;\n  }\n\n  public void setDateTime(Instant dateTime) {\n    this.dateTime = dateTime;\n  }\n\n  public void setDateTime(String date, String time, ZoneId tz) {\n    ZonedDateTime dateObject = DateUtils.toZonedDateTime(date, time, tz);\n    setDateTime(dateObject == null ? Instant.now() : dateObject.toInstant());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Is the trip originally planned withing the previous/next 15h?\n   */\n  public boolean isTripPlannedForNow() {\n    return Duration.between(dateTime, Instant.now()).abs().toSeconds() < NOW_THRESHOLD_SEC;\n  }\n\n  public SortOrder getItinerariesSortOrder() {\n    if (pageCursor != null) {\n      return pageCursor.originalSortOrder;\n    }\n    return arriveBy ? SortOrder.STREET_AND_DEPARTURE_TIME : SortOrder.STREET_AND_ARRIVAL_TIME;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Adjust the 'dateTime' if the page cursor is set to \"goto next/previous page\". The date-time is\n   * used for many things, for example finding the days to search, but the transit search is using\n   * the cursor[if exist], not the date-time.\n   */\n  public void applyPageCursor() {\n    if (pageCursor != null) {\n      // We switch to \"depart-after\" search when paging next(lat==null). pageCursor.originalSortOrder.isSortedByArrivalTimeAcceding() == previousPage;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Related to {@link #maxNumberOfItinerariesCropHead()}, but is {@code true} if we should crop the\n   * this.intermediatePlaces = new ArrayList<>();\n    }\n    this.intermediatePlaces.add(location);\n  }\n\n  public RouteRequest getStreetSearchRequest(StreetMode streetMode) {\n    RouteRequest streetRequest = this.clone();\n    streetRequest.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet();\n\n    if (streetMode != null) {\n      switch (streetMode) {\n        case WALK:\n        case TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR, TraverseMode.WALK)\n          );\n          streetRequest.parkAndRide = true;\n          break;\n        case CAR_PICKUP:\n          streetRequest.setStreetSubRequestModes(\n            new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR, TraverseMode.WALK)\n          );\n          streetRequest.carPickup = true;\n          break;\n        case CAR_RENTAL:\n          streetRequest.setStreetSubRequestModes(\n            new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR, TraverseMode.WALK)\n          );\n          streetRequest.vehicleRental = true;\n          streetRequest.allowedRentalFormFactors.add(FormFactor.CAR);\n      }\n    }\n\n    return streetRequest;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * This method is used to clone the default message, and insert a current \" + mode);\n    };\n  }\n\n  /** @return The highest speed for all possible road-modes. */\n  public double getStreetSpeedUpperBound() {\n    // Assume carSpeed > bikeSpeed > walkSpeed\n    if return unpreferredAgencies;\n  }\n\n  public Set<FeedScopedId> getUnpreferredRoutes() {\n    return unpreferredRoutes;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Sets the bicycle triangle routing parameters -- the relative importance of safety, flatness,\n   * and speed. These three fields of the RoutingRequest should have values between 0 and 1, and\n   * should add up to 1. This setter function accepts any three numbers and will normalize them to\n   * add up to 1.\n   */\n  public void setTriangleNormalized(double safe, double slope, double time) {\n    if (safe == 0 && slope == 0 && time == 0) {\n      var oneThird = 1f / 3;\n      safe = oneThird;\n      slope = oneThird;\n      time = oneThird;\n    }\n    safe = setMinValue(safe);\n    slope = setMinValue(slope);\n    time = setMinValue(time);\n\n    double total = safe + slope + time;\n    if (total != 1) {\n      LOG.warn(\n        \"Bicycle triangle factors don't add up to 1. Values will be scaled proportionally to each other.\"\n      );\n    }\n\n    safe /= total;\n    slope /= total;\n    time /= total;\n    this.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor = safe;\n    this.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor = slope;\n    this.bikeTriangleTimeFactor = time;\n  }\n\n  public Comparator<GraphPath> getPathComparator(boolean compareStartTimes) {\n    if (\"duration\".equals(pathComparator)) {\n      return new DurationComparator();\n    }\n    return new PathComparator(compareStartTimes);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Returns if the vertex is considered \"close\" to the start or end point of the request. This is\n   * useful if you want to allow loops in car routes under certain conditions.\n   * <p>\n   * Note: If you are doing Raptor access/egress searches this method does not take the possible\n   * intermediate points (stations) into account. This means that stations might be skipped because\n   * a car route to it cannot be found and a suboptimal route to another station is returned\n   * instead.\n   * <p>\n   * If you encounter a case of this, you can adjust this code to take this into account.\n   *\n   * @see RouteRequest#MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS\n   * @see DominanceFunction#betterOrEqualAndComparable(State, State)\n   */\n  public boolean isCloseToStartOrEnd(Vertex vertex) {\n    if (from == null || to == null || from.getCoordinate() == null || to.getCoordinate() == null) {\n      return false;\n    }\n    if (fromEnvelope == null) {\n      fromEnvelope = getEnvelope(from.getCoordinate(), MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS);\n    }\n    if (toEnvelope == null) {\n      toEnvelope = getEnvelope(to.getCoordinate(), MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS);\n    }\n    return (\n      fromEnvelope.intersects(vertex.getCoordinate()) ||\n      toEnvelope.intersects(vertex.getCoordinate())\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** The start location */\n  public GenericLocation from() {\n    return from;\n  }\n\n  public void setFrom(GenericLocation from) {\n    this.from = from;\n  }\n\n  /** The end location */\n  public GenericLocation to() {\n    return to;\n  }\n\n  public void setTo(GenericLocation to) {\n    this.to = to;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * This is the time/duration in seconds from the earliest-departure-time(EDT) to\n   * latest-departure-time(LDT). In case of a reverse search it will be the time from earliest to\n   * latest arrival time (LAT - EAT).\n   * <p>\n   * All optimal travels that depart within the search window is guarantied to be found.\n   * <p>\n   * This is sometimes referred to as the Range Raptor Search Window - but could be used in a none\n   * Transit search as well; Hence this is named search-window and not raptor-search-window. Do not\n   * confuse this with the travel-window, which is the time between EDT to LAT.\n   * <p>\n   * Use {@code null} to unset, and {@link Duration#ZERO} to do one Raptor iteration. The value is\n   * dynamically  assigned a suitable value, if not set. In a small to medium size operation you may\n   * use a fixed value, like 60 minutes. If you have a mixture of high frequency cities routes and\n   * infrequent long distant journeys, the best option is normally to use the dynamic auto\n   * assignment.\n   * <p>\n   * There is no need to set this when going to the next/previous page any more.\n   */\n  public Duration searchWindow() {\n    return searchWindow;\n  }\n\n  public void setSearchWindow(Duration searchWindow) {\n    this.searchWindow = searchWindow;\n  }\n\n  public Locale locale() {\n    return locale;\n  }\n\n  public void setLocale(Locale locale) {\n    this.locale = locale;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Use the cursor to go to the next or previous \"page\" of trips. You should pass in the original\n   * request as is.\n   * <p>\n   * The next page of itineraries will depart after the current results and the previous page of\n   * itineraries will depart before the current results.\n   * <p>\n   * The paging does not support timeTableView=false and arriveBy=true, this will result in none\n   * pareto-optimal results.\n   */\n  public PageCursor pageCursor() {\n    return pageCursor;\n  }\n\n  public void setPageCursorFromEncoded(String pageCursor) {\n    this.pageCursor = PageCursor.decode(pageCursor);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Search for the best trip options within a time window. If {@code true} two itineraries are\n   * considered optimal if one is better on arrival time(earliest wins) and the other is better on\n   * departure time(latest wins).\n   * <p>\n   * In combination with {@code arriveBy} this parameter cover the following 3 use cases:\n   * <ul>\n   *   <li>\n   *     The traveler want to find the best alternative within a time window. Set\n   *     {@code timetableView=true} and {@code arriveBy=false}. This is the default, and if the\n   *     intention of the traveler is unknown, this gives the best result. This use-case includes\n   *     all itineraries in the two next use-cases. This option also work well with paging.\n   * <p>\n   *     Setting the {@code arriveBy=false}, covers the same use-case, but the input time is\n   *     interpreted as latest-arrival-time, and not earliest-departure-time.\n   *   </li>\n   *   <li>\n   *     The traveler want to find the best alternative with departure after a specific time.\n   *     For example: I am at the station now and want to get home as quickly as possible.\n   *     Set {@code timetableView=true} and {@code arriveBy=false}. Do not support paging.\n   *   </li>\n   *   <li>\n   *     Traveler want to find the best alternative with arrival before specific time. For\n   *     example going to a meeting. Set {@code timetableView=true} and {@code arriveBy=false}.\n   *     Do not support paging.\n   *   </li>\n   * </ul>\n   * Default: true\n   */\n  public boolean timetableView() {\n    return timetableView;\n  }\n\n  public void setTimetableView(boolean timetableView) {\n    this.timetableView = timetableView;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Whether the trip should depart at dateTime (false, the default), or arrive at dateTime.\n   */\n  public boolean arriveBy() {\n    return arriveBy;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * The maximum number of itineraries to return. In OTP1 this parameter terminates the search, but\n   * in OTP2 it crops the list of itineraries AFTER the search is complete. This parameter is a post\n   * search filter function. A side effect from reducing the result is that OTP2 cannot guarantee to\n   * find all pareto-optimal itineraries when paging. Also, a large search-window and a small {@code\n   * numItineraries} waste computer CPU calculation time.\n   * <p>\n   * The default value is 50. This is a reasonably high threshold to prevent large amount of data to\n   * be returned. Consider tuning the search-window instead of setting this to a small value.\n   */\n  public int numItineraries() {\n    return numItineraries;\n  }\n\n  public void setNumItineraries(int numItineraries) {\n    this.numItineraries = numItineraries;\n  }\n\n  private static Envelope getEnvelope(Coordinate c, int meters) {\n    double lat = SphericalDistanceLibrary.metersToDegrees(meters);\n    double lon = SphericalDistanceLibrary.metersToLonDegrees(meters, c.y);\n\n    Envelope env = new Envelope(c);\n    env.expandBy(lon, lat);\n\n    return env;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Checks if the route is banned. Also, if whitelisting is used, the route (or its agency) has to\n   * be whitelisted in order to not count as banned.\n   *\n   * @return True if the route is banned\n   */\n  private boolean routeIsBanned(Route route) {\n    /* check if agency is banned for this plan */\n    if (!bannedAgencies.isEmpty()) {\n      if (bannedAgencies.contains(route.getAgency().getId())) {\n        return true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    /* check if route banned for this plan */\n    if (!bannedRoutes.isEmpty()) {\n      if (bannedRoutes.matches(route)) {\n        return true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    boolean whiteListed = false;\n    boolean whiteListInUse = false;\n\n    /* check if agency is whitelisted for this plan */\n    if (!whiteListedAgencies.isEmpty()) {\n      whiteListInUse = true;\n      if (whiteListedAgencies.contains(route.getAgency().getId())) {\n        whiteListed = true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    /* check if route is whitelisted for this plan */\n    if (!whiteListedRoutes.isEmpty()) {\n      whiteListInUse = true;\n      if (whiteListedRoutes.matches(route)) {\n        whiteListed = true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (whiteListInUse && !whiteListed) {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  private double setMinValue(double value) {\n    return Math.max(0, value);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/api/request/RouteRequestLocationProximity.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request;\n\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Envelope;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.geometry.SphericalDistanceLibrary;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\n\n/**\n * Calculates whether a vertex is close to the start or end locations of a route request.\n */\npublic class RouteRequestLocationProximity {\n\n  private static final int MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS = 500;\n\n  private final GenericLocation from;\n  private final GenericLocation to;\n\n  private Envelope fromEnvelope;\n  private Envelope toEnvelope;\n\n  public RouteRequestLocationProximity(GenericLocation from, GenericLocation to) {\n    this.from = from;\n    this.to = to;\n  }\n\n  public boolean isCloseToStartOrEnd(Vertex vertex) {\n    if (from == null || to == null || from.getCoordinate() == null || to.getCoordinate() == null) {\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    if (fromEnvelope == null) {\n      fromEnvelope = getEnvelope(from.getCoordinate(), MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS);\n    }\n    if (toEnvelope == null) {\n      toEnvelope = getEnvelope(to.getCoordinate(), MAX_CLOSENESS_METERS);\n    }\n\n    return (\n      fromEnvelope.intersects(vertex.getCoordinate()) ||\n      toEnvelope.intersects(vertex.getCoordinate())\n    );\n  }\n\n  private static Envelope getEnvelope(Coordinate coordinate, int meters) {\n    double latitude = SphericalDistanceLibrary.metersToDegrees(meters);\n    double longitude = SphericalDistanceLibrary.metersToLonDegrees(meters, coordinate.y);\n\n    Envelope envelope = new Envelope(coordinate);\n    envelope.expandBy(longitude, latitude);\n    return envelope;\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package org.opentripplanner;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.updater.stoptime.BackwardsDelayPropagationType.REQUIRED_NO_DATA;\n\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.FeedEntity;\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.FeedMessage;\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.TripUpdate;\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.io.FileInputStream;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.opentripplanner.api.common.LocationStringParser;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.model.GtfsBundle;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.GtfsFeedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.GtfsModule;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.calendar.ServiceDateInterval;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.Itinerary;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.Leg;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.response.RoutingResponse;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.TransitAlertServiceImpl;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.api.OtpServerRequestContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.updater.TimetableSnapshotSourceParameters;\nimport org.opentripplanner.updater.alerts.AlertsUpdateHandler;\nimport org.opentripplanner.updater.stoptime.TimetableSnapshotSource;\n\n/** Common base class for many test classes which need to load a GTFS feed in preparation for tests. */\npublic abstract class GtfsTest {\n\n  public Graph graph;\n  public TransitModel transitModel;\n\n  AlertsUpdateHandler alertsUpdateHandler;\n  TimetableSnapshotSource timetableSnapshotSource;\n  TransitAlertServiceImpl alertPatchServiceImpl;\n  public OtpServerRequestContext serverContext;\n  public GtfsFeedId feedId;\n\n  public abstract String getFeedName();\n\n  public Itinerary plan(\n    long dateTime,\n    String fromVertex,\n    String toVertex,\n    String onTripId,\n    boolean wheelchairAccessible,\n    boolean preferLeastTransfers,\n    TraverseMode preferredMode,\n    String excludedRoute,\n    String excludedStop,\n    int legCount\n  ) {\n    final TraverseMode mode = preferredMode != null ? preferredMode : TraverseMode.TRANSIT;\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    routingRequest.setNumItineraries(1);\n\n    routingRequest.setArriveBy(dateTime < 0);\n    routingRequest.setDateTime(Instant.ofEpochSecond(Math.abs(dateTime)));\n    if (fromVertex != null && !fromVertex.isEmpty()) {\n      routingRequest.setFrom(\n        LocationStringParser.getGenericLocation(null, feedId.getId() + \":\" + fromVertex)\n      );\n    }\n    if (toVertex != null && !toVertex.isEmpty()) {\n      routingRequest.setTo(\n        LocationStringParser.getGenericLocation(null, feedId.getId() + \":\" + toVertex)\n      );\n    }\n    if (onTripId != null && !onTripId.isEmpty()) {\n      routingRequest.startingTransitTripId = (new FeedScopedId(feedId.getId(), onTripId));\n    }\n    routingRequest.setWheelchairAccessible(wheelchairAccessible);\n    routingRequest.transferCost = (preferLeastTransfers ? 300 : 0);\n    routingRequest.setStreetSubRequestModes(new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, mode));\n    if (excludedRoute != null && !excludedRoute.isEmpty()) {\n      routingRequest.setBannedRoutes(List.of(new FeedScopedId(feedId.getId(), excludedRoute)));\n    }\n    if (excludedStop != null && !excludedStop.isEmpty()) {\n      throw new UnsupportedOperationException(\"Stop banning is not yet implemented in OTP2\");\n    }\n    routingRequest.setOtherThanPreferredRoutesPenalty(0);\n    // The walk board cost is set low because it interferes with test 2c1.\n    // As long as boarding has a very low cost, waiting should not be \"better\" than riding\n    // since this makes interlining _worse_ than alighting and re-boarding the same line.\n    // TODO rethink whether it makes sense to weight waiting to board _less_ than 1.\n    routingRequest.setWaitReluctance(1);\n    routingRequest.setWalkBoardCost(30);\n    routingRequest.transferSlack = 0;\n\n    RoutingResponse res = serverContext.routingService().route(routingRequest);\n    List<Itinerary> itineraries = res.getTripPlan().itineraries;\n    // Stored in instance field for use in individual tests\n    Itinerary itinerary = itineraries.get(0);\n\n    assertEquals(legCount, itinerary.getLegs().size());\n\n    return itinerary;\n  }\n\n  public void validateLeg(\n    Leg leg,\n    long startTime,\n    long endTime,\n    String toStopId,\n    String fromStopId,\n    String alert\n  ) {\n    assertEquals(startTime, leg.getStartTime().toInstant().toEpochMilli());\n    assertEquals(endTime, leg.getEndTime().toInstant().toEpochMilli());\n    assertEquals(toStopId, leg.getTo().stop.getId().getId());\n    assertEquals(feedId.getId(), leg.getTo().stop.getId().getFeedId());\n    if (fromStopId != null) {\n      assertEquals(feedId.getId(), leg.getFrom().stop.getId().getFeedId());\n      assertEquals(fromStopId, leg.getFrom().stop.getId().getId());\n    } else {\n      assertNull(leg.getFrom().stop.getId());\n    }\n    if (alert != null) {\n      assertNotNull(leg.getStreetNotes());\n      assertEquals(1, leg.getStreetNotes().size());\n      assertEquals(alert, leg.getStreetNotes().iterator().next().note.toString());\n    } else {\n      assertNull(leg.getStreetNotes());\n    }\n  }\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  protected void setUp() throws Exception {\n    File gtfs = new File(\"src/test/resources/\" + getFeedName());\n    File gtfsRealTime = new File(\"src/test/resources/\" + getFeedName() + \".pb\");\n    GtfsBundle gtfsBundle = new GtfsBundle(gtfs);\n    feedId = new GtfsFeedId.Builder().id(\"FEED\").build();\n    gtfsBundle.setFeedId(feedId);\n    List<GtfsBundle> gtfsBundleList = Collections.singletonList(gtfsBundle);\n\n    alertsUpdateHandler = new AlertsUpdateHandler();\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    transitModel = new TransitModel(new StopModel(), deduplicator);\n\n    GtfsModule gtfsGraphBuilderImpl = new GtfsModule(\n      gtfsBundleList,\n      transitModel,\n      graph,\n      ServiceDateInterval.unbounded()\n    );\n\n    gtfsGraphBuilderImpl.buildGraph();\n    transitModel.index();\n    graph.index(transitModel.getStopModel());\n    serverContext = TestServerContext.createServerContext(graph, transitModel);\n    timetableSnapshotSource =\n      new TimetableSnapshotSource(\n        TimetableSnapshotSourceParameters.DEFAULT.withPurgeExpiredData(false),\n        transitModel\n      );\n    alertPatchServiceImpl = new TransitAlertServiceImpl(transitModel);\n    alertsUpdateHandler.setTransitAlertService(alertPatchServiceImpl);\n    alertsUpdateHandler.setFeedId(feedId.getId());\n\n    try {\n      final boolean fullDataset = false;\n      InputStream inputStream = new FileInputStream(gtfsRealTime);\n      FeedMessage feedMessage = FeedMessage.PARSER.parseFrom(inputStream);\n      List<FeedEntity> feedEntityList = feedMessage.getEntityList();\n      List<TripUpdate> updates = new ArrayList<>(feedEntityList.size());\n      for (FeedEntity feedEntity : feedEntityList) {\n        updates.add(feedEntity.getTripUpdate());\n      }\n      timetableSnapshotSource.applyTripUpdates(\n        null,\n        REQUIRED_NO_DATA,\n        fullDataset,\n        updates,\n        feedId.getId()\n      );\n      alertsUpdateHandler.update(feedMessage);\n    } catch (Exception exception) {}\n  }\n}\n"

static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.fail;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.PathTransfer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.GraphRoutingTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.StopPattern;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.TripPattern;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.StopLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.lang.ToStringBuilder;\n\n/**\n * This creates a graph with trip patterns\n <pre>\n  S0 -  V0 ------------\n        |     \\       |\n S11 - V11 --------> V21 - S21\n        |      \\      |\n S12 - V12 --------> V22 - V22\n        |             |\n S13 - V13 --------> V23 - V23\n </pre>\n */\nclass DirectTransferGeneratorTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  private static public void testDirectTransfersWithoutPatterns() {\n    var otpModel = model(false);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n    var transferRequests = transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(transitModel.getAllPathTransfers());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testDirectTransfersWithPatterns() {\n    var otpModel = model(true);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = false;\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n    var transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build())\n    );\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(\n      transitModel.getAllPathTransfers(),\n      tr(S0, 556, S11),\n      tr(S0, 935, S21),\n      tr(S11, 751, S21),\n      tr(S12, 751, S22),\n      tr(S13, 2224, S12),\n      tr(S13, 2347, S22),\n      tr(S21, 751, S11),\n      tr(S22, 751, S12),\n      tr(S23, 2347, S12),\n      tr(S23, 2224, S22)\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testDirectTransfersWithRestrictedPatterns() {\n    var otpModel = model(true, true);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = false;\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n    var transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build())\n    );\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(\n      transitModel.getAllPathTransfers(),\n      tr(S0, 2780, S12),\n      tr(S0, 935, S21),\n      tr(S11, 2224, S12),\n      tr(S11, 751, S21),\n      tr(S12, 751, S22),\n      tr(S13, 2224, S12),\n      tr(S13, 2347, S22),\n      tr(S21, 2347, S12),\n      tr(S22, 751, S12),\n      tr(S23, 2347, S12),\n      tr(S23, 2224, S22)\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testSingleRequestWithoutPatterns() {\n    var transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build())\n    );\n\n    var otpModel = model(false);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(transitModel.getAllPathTransfers());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testSingleRequestWithPatterns() {\n    List<RouteRequest> transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build())\n    );\n\n    var otpModel = model(true);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(\n      transitModel.getAllPathTransfers(),\n      tr(S0, 100, List.of(V0, V11), S11),\n      tr(S0, 100, List.of(V0, V21), S21),\n      tr(S11, 100, List.of(V11, V21), S21)\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testMultipleRequestsWithoutPatterns() {\n    var transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build()),\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.BIKE).build())\n    );\n\n    var otpModel = model(false);\n    var graph = otpModel.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    var transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(transitModel.getAllPathTransfers());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testMultipleRequestsWithPatterns() {\n    var transferRequests = List.of(\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK).build()),\n      new RouteRequest(RequestModes.of().withTransferMode(StreetMode.BIKE).build())\n    );\n\n    TestOtpModel model = model(true);\n    var graph = model.graph();\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    var transitModel = model.transitModel();\n\n    new DirectTransferGenerator(\n      graph,\n      transitModel,\n      noopIssueStore(),\n      MAX_TRANSFER_DURATION,\n      transferRequests\n    )\n      .buildGraph();\n\n    assertTransfers(\n      transitModel.getAllPathTransfers(),\n      tr(S0, 100, List.of(V0, V11), S11),\n      tr(S0, 100, List.of(V0, V21), S21),\n      tr(S11, 100, List.of(V11, V21), S21),\n      tr(S11, 110, List.of(V11, V22), S22)\n    );\n  }\n\n  private TestOtpModel model(boolean addPatterns) {\n    return model(addPatterns, false);\n  }\n\n  private TestOtpModel model(boolean addPatterns, boolean withBoardingConstraint) {\n    return modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          S0 = stop(\"S0\", 47.495, 19.001);\n          S11 = stop(\"S11\", 47.500, 19.001);\n          S12 = stop(\"S12\", 47.520, 19.001);\n          S13 = stop(\"S13\", 47.540, 19.001);\n          S21 = stop(\"S21\", 47.500, 19.011);\n          S22 = stop(\"S22\", 47.520, 19.011);\n          S23 = stop(\"S23\", 47.540, 19.011);\n\n          V0 = intersection(\"V0\", 47.495, 19.000);\n          V11 = intersection(\"V11\", 47.500, 19.000);\n          V12 = intersection(\"V12\", 47.510, 19.000);\n          V13 = intersection(\"V13\", 47.520, 19.000);\n          V21 = intersection(\"V21\", 47.500, 19.010);\n          V22 = intersection(\"V22\", 47.510, 19.010);\n          V23 = intersection(\"V23\", 47.520, 19.010);\n\n          biLink(V0, S0);\n          biLink(V11, S11);\n          biLink(V12, S12);\n          biLink(V13, S13);\n          biLink(V21, S21);\n          biLink(V22, S22);\n          biLink(V23, S23);\n\n          street(V0, V11, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n          street(V0, V12, 200, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n          street(V0, V21, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n          street(V0, V22, 200, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n\n          street(V11, V12, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(V12, V13, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(V21, V22, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(V22, V23, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(V11, V21, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(V11, V22, 110, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n\n          if (addPatterns) {\n            var agency = TransitModelForTest.agency(\"Agency\");\n\n            tripPattern(\n              TripPattern\n                .of(TransitModelForTest.id(\"TP1\"))\n                .withRoute(route(\"R1\", TransitMode.BUS, agency))\n                .withStopPattern(\n                  new StopPattern(List.of(st(S11, !withBoardingConstraint, true), st(S12), st(S13)))\n                )\n                .build()\n            );\n\n            tripPattern(\n              TripPattern\n                .of(TransitModelForTest.id(\"TP2\"))\n                .withRoute(route(\"R2\", TransitMode.BUS, agency))\n                .withStopPattern(new StopPattern(List.of(st(S21), st(S22), st(S23))))\n                .build()\n            );\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertTransfers(\n    Collection<PathTransfer> allPathTransfers,\n    TransferDescriptor... transfers\n  ) {\n    var matchedTransfers = new HashSet<PathTransfer>();\n    var assertions = Stream.concat(\n      Arrays.stream(transfers).map(td -> td.matcher(allPathTransfers, matchedTransfers)),\n      Stream.of(allTransfersMatched(allPathTransfers, matchedTransfers))\n    );\n\n    assertAll(assertions);\n  }\n\n  private Executable allTransfersMatched(\n    Collection<PathTransfer> transfersByStop,\n    Set<PathTransfer> matchedTransfers\n  ) {\n    return () -> {\n      var missingTransfers = new HashSet<>(transfersByStop);\n      missingTransfers.removeAll(matchedTransfers);\n\n      assertEquals(Set.of(), missingTransfers, \"All transfers matched\");\n    };\n  }\n\n  private TransferDescriptor tr(TransitStopVertex from, double distance, TransitStopVertex to) {\n    return new TransferDescriptor(from, distance, to);\n  }\n\n  private TransferDescriptor tr(\n    TransitStopVertex from,\n    double distance,\n    List<StreetVertex> vertices,\n    TransitStopVertex to\n  ) {\n    return new TransferDescriptor(from, distance, vertices, to);\n  }\n\n  private static class TransferDescriptor {\n\n    private final StopLocation from;\n    private final StopLocation to;\n    private final Double distanceMeters;\n    private final List<StreetVertex> vertices;\n\n    public TransferDescriptor(TransitStopVertex from, Double distanceMeters, TransitStopVertex to) {\n      this.from = from.getStop();\n      this.distanceMeters = distanceMeters;\n      this.vertices = null;\n      this.to = to.getStop();\n    }\n\n    public TransferDescriptor(\n      TransitStopVertex from,\n      Double distanceMeters,\n      List<StreetVertex> vertices,\n      TransitStopVertex to\n    ) {\n      this.from = from.getStop();\n      this.distanceMeters = distanceMeters;\n      this.vertices = vertices;\n      this.to = to.getStop();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n      return ToStringBuilder\n        .of(getClass())\n        .addObj(\"from\", from)\n        .addObj(\"to\", to)\n        .addNum(\"distanceMeters\", distanceMeters)\n        .addCol(\"vertices\", vertices)\n        .toString();\n    }\n\n    boolean matches(PathTransfer transfer) {\n      if (!Objects.equals(from, transfer.from) || !Objects.equals(to, transfer.to)) {\n        return false;\n      }\n\n      if (vertices == null) {\n        return distanceMeters == transfer.getDistanceMeters() && transfer.getEdges() == null;\n      } else {\n        var transferVertices = transfer\n          .getEdges()\n          .stream()\n          .map(Edge::getToVertex)\n          .filter(StreetVertex.class::isInstance)\n          .toList();\n\n        return (\n          distanceMeters == transfer.getDistanceMeters() &&\n          Objects.equals(vertices, transferVertices)\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    private Executable matcher(\n      Collection<PathTransfer> transfersByStop,\n      Set<PathTransfer> matchedTransfers\n    ) {\n      return () -> {\n        var matched = transfersByStop.stream().filter(this::matches).findFirst();\n\n        if (matched.isPresent()) {\n          assertTrue(true, \"Found transfer for \" + this);\n          matchedTransfers.add(matched.get());\n        } else {\n          fail(\"Missing transfer for \" + this);\n        }\n      };\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.osm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.fail;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.DataImportIssueStore.noopIssueStore;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.osm.WayPropertiesBuilder.withModes;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission.ALL;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN;\n\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.net.URLDecoder;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.model.P2;\nimport org.opentripplanner.openstreetmap.OpenStreetMapProvider;\nimport org.opentripplanner.openstreetmap.model.OSMWay;\nimport org.opentripplanner.openstreetmap.model.OSMWithTags;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Edge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.LocalizedString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.NonLocalizedString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\npublic class OpenStreetMapModuleTest {\n\n  @Test\n  public void testGraphBuilder() {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    var gg = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    var transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n\n    File file = new File(\n      URLDecoder.decode(getClass().getResource(\"map.osm.pbf\").getFile(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)\n    );\n\n    OpenStreetMapProvider provider = new OpenStreetMapProvider(file, true);\n\n    OpenStreetMapModule osmModule = new OpenStreetMapModule(\n      List.of(provider),\n      Set.of(),\n      gg,\n      transitModel.getTimeZone(),\n      noopIssueStore()\n    );\n    osmModule.setDefaultWayPropertySetSource(new DefaultWayPropertySetSource());\n\n    osmModule.buildGraph();\n\n    // Kamiennogorska at south end of segment\n    Vertex v1 = gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:280592578\");\n\n    // Kamiennogorska at Mariana Smoluchowskiego\n    Vertex v2 = gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:288969929\");\n\n    // Mariana Smoluchowskiego, north end\n    Vertex v3 = gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:280107802\");\n\n    // Mariana Smoluchowskiego, south end (of segment connected to v2)\n    Vertex v4 = gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:288970952\");\n\n    assertNotNull(v1);\n    assertNotNull(v2);\n    assertNotNull(v3);\n    assertNotNull(v4);\n\n    Edge e1 = null, e2 = null, e3 = null;\n    for (Edge e : v2.getOutgoing()) {\n      if (e.getToVertex() == v1) {\n        e1 = e;\n      } else if (e.getToVertex() == v3) {\n        e2 = e;\n      } else if (e.getToVertex() == v4) {\n        e3 = e;\n      }\n    }\n\n    assertNotNull(e1);\n    assertNotNull(e2);\n    assertNotNull(e3);\n\n    assertTrue(\n      e1.getDefaultName().contains(\"Kamiennog\\u00F3rska\"),\n      \"name of e1 must be like \\\"Kamiennog\\u00F3rska\\\"; was \" + e1.getDefaultName()\n    );\n    assertTrue(\n      e2.getDefaultName().contains(\"Mariana Smoluchowskiego\"),\n      \"name of e2 must be like \\\"Mariana Smoluchowskiego\\\"; was \" + e2.getDefaultName()\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Detailed testing of OSM graph building using a very small chunk of NYC (SOHO-ish).\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void testBuildGraphDetailed() throws Exception {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    var gg = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    var transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n\n    File file = new File(\n      URLDecoder.decode(\n        getClass().getResource(\"NYC_small.osm.pbf\").getFile(),\n        StandardCharsets.UTF_8\n      )\n    );\n    OpenStreetMapProvider provider = new OpenStreetMapProvider(file, true);\n    OpenStreetMapModule osmModule = new OpenStreetMapModule(\n      List.of(provider),\n      Set.of(),\n      gg,\n      transitModel.getTimeZone(),\n      noopIssueStore()\n    );\n    osmModule.setDefaultWayPropertySetSource(new DefaultWayPropertySetSource());\n\n    osmModule.buildGraph();\n\n    // These vertices are labeled in the OSM file as having traffic lights.\n    IntersectionVertex iv1 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:1919595918\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv2 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42442273\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv3 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:1919595927\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv4 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42452026\");\n    assertTrue(iv1.trafficLight);\n    assertTrue(iv2.trafficLight);\n    assertTrue(iv3.trafficLight);\n    assertTrue(iv4.trafficLight);\n\n    // These are not.\n    IntersectionVertex iv5 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42435485\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv6 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42439335\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv7 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42436761\");\n    IntersectionVertex iv8 = (IntersectionVertex) gg.getVertex(\"osm:node:42442291\");\n    assertFalse(iv5.trafficLight);\n    assertFalse(iv6.trafficLight);\n    assertFalse(iv7.trafficLight);\n    assertFalse(iv8.trafficLight);\n\n    Set<P2<Vertex>> edgeEndpoints = new HashSet<>();\n    for (StreetEdge se : gg.getStreetEdges()) {\n      P2<Vertex> endpoints = new P2<>(se.getFromVertex(), se.getToVertex());\n      // Check that we don't get any duplicate edges on this small graph.\n      if (edgeEndpoints.contains(endpoints)) {\n        fail();\n      }\n      edgeEndpoints.add(endpoints);\n    }\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBuildAreaWithoutVisibility() throws Exception {\n    testBuildingAreas(true);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBuildAreaWithVisibility() throws Exception {\n    testBuildingAreas(false);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testWayDataSet() {\n    OSMWithTags way = new OSMWay();\n    way.addTag(\"highway\", \"footway\");\n    way.addTag(\"cycleway\", \"lane\");\n    way.addTag(\"access\", \"no\");\n    way.addTag(\"surface\", \"gravel\");\n\n    WayPropertySet wayPropertySet = new WayPropertySet();\n\n    // where there are no way specifiers, the default is used\n    assertEquals(wayPropertySet.getDataForWay(way), wayPropertySet.defaultProperties);\n\n    // add two equal matches: lane only...\n    OSMSpecifier lane_only = new OSMSpecifier(\"cycleway=lane\");\n\n    WayProperties lane_is_safer = withModes(ALL).bicycleSafety(1.5).build();\n\n    wayPropertySet.addProperties(lane_only, lane_is_safer);\n\n    // and footway only\n    OSMSpecifier footway_only = new OSMSpecifier(\"highway=footway\");\n\n    WayProperties footways_allow_peds = new WayPropertiesBuilder(PEDESTRIAN).build();\n\n    wayPropertySet.addProperties(footway_only, footways_allow_peds);\n\n    WayProperties dataForWay = wayPropertySet.getDataForWay(way);\n    // the first one is found\n    assertEquals(dataForWay, lane_is_safer);\n\n    // add a better match\n    OSMSpecifier lane_and_footway = new OSMSpecifier(\"cycleway=lane;highway=footway\");\n\n    WayProperties safer_and_peds = new WayPropertiesBuilder(PEDESTRIAN).bicycleSafety(0.75).build();\n\n    wayPropertySet.addProperties(lane_and_footway, safer_and_peds);\n    dataForWay = wayPropertySet.getDataForWay(way);\n    assertEquals(dataForWay, safer_and_peds);\n\n    // add a mixin\n    OSMSpecifier gravel = new OSMSpecifier(\"surface=gravel\");\n    WayProperties gravel_is_dangerous = new WayPropertiesBuilder(ALL).bicycleSafety(2).build();\n    wayPropertySet.addProperties(gravel, gravel_is_dangerous, true);\n\n    dataForWay = wayPropertySet.getDataForWay(way);\n    assertEquals(dataForWay.getBicycleSafetyFeatures().first, 1.5);\n\n    // test a left-right distinction\n    way = new OSMWay();\n    way.addTag(\"highway\", \"footway\");\n    way.addTag(\"cycleway\", \"lane\");\n    way.addTag(\"cycleway:right\", \"track\");\n\n    OSMSpecifier track_only = new OSMSpecifier(\"highway=footway;cycleway=track\");\n    WayProperties track_is_safest = new WayPropertiesBuilder(ALL).bicycleSafety(0.25).build();\n\n    wayPropertySet.addProperties(track_only, track_is_safest);\n    dataForWay = wayPropertySet.getDataForWay(way);\n    assertEquals(0.25, dataForWay.getBicycleSafetyFeatures().first); // right (with traffic) comes\n    // from track\n    assertEquals(0.75, dataForWay.getBicycleSafetyFeatures().second); // left comes from lane\n\n    way = new OSMWay();\n    way.addTag(\"highway\", \"footway\");\n    way.addTag(\"footway\", \"sidewalk\");\n    way.addTag(\"RLIS:reviewed\", \"no\");\n    WayPropertySet propset = new WayPropertySet();\n    CreativeNamer namer = new CreativeNamer(\"platform\");\n    propset.addCreativeNamer(\n      new OSMSpecifier(\"railway=platform;highway=footway;footway=sidewalk\"),\n      namer\n    );\n    namer = new CreativeNamer(\"sidewalk\");\n    propset.addCreativeNamer(new OSMSpecifier(\"highway=footway;footway=sidewalk\"), namer);\n    assertEquals(\"sidewalk\", propset.getCreativeNameForWay(way).toString());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCreativeNaming() {\n    OSMWithTags way = new OSMWay();\n    way.addTag(\"highway\", \"footway\");\n    way.addTag(\"cycleway\", \"lane\");\n    way.addTag(\"access\", \"no\");\n\n    CreativeNamer namer = new CreativeNamer();\n    namer.setCreativeNamePattern(\n      \"Highway with cycleway {cycleway} and access {access} and morx {morx}\"\n    );\n    assertEquals(\n      \"Highway with cycleway lane and access no and morx \",\n      namer.generateCreativeName(way).toString()\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testLocalizedString() {\n    LocalizedString localizedString = new LocalizedString(\n      \"corner\",\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"first\"),\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"second\")\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(\"Kreuzung first mit second\", localizedString.toString(new Locale(\"de\")));\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * This reads test file with area and tests if it can be routed if visibility is used and if it\n   * isn't\n   * <p>\n   * Routing needs to be successful in both options since without visibility calculation area rings\n   * are used.\n   *\n   * @param skipVisibility if true visibility calculations are skipped\n   */\n  private void testBuildingAreas(boolean skipVisibility) {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    var graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    var transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n\n    File file = new File(\n      URLDecoder.decode(\n        getClass().getResource(\"usf_area.osm.pbf\").getFile(),\n        StandardCharsets.UTF_8\n      )\n    );\n    OpenStreetMapProvider provider = new OpenStreetMapProvider(file, false);\n\n    OpenStreetMapModule loader = new OpenStreetMapModule(\n      List.of(provider),\n      Set.of(),\n      graph,\n      transitModel.getTimeZone(),\n      noopIssueStore()\n    );\n    loader.skipVisibility = skipVisibility;\n    loader.setDefaultWayPropertySetSource(new DefaultWayPropertySetSource());\n\n    loader.buildGraph();\n\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest(new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK));\n\n    //This are vertices that can be connected only over edges on area (with correct permissions)\n    //It tests if it is possible to route over area without visibility calculations\n    Vertex bottomV = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:580290955\");\n    Vertex topV = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:559271124\");\n\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, bottomV, topV);\n\n    GraphPathFinder graphPathFinder = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(3));\n    List<GraphPath> pathList = graphPathFinder.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n    assertNotNull(pathList);\n    assertFalse(pathList.isEmpty());\n    for (GraphPath path : pathList) {\n      assertFalse(path.states.isEmpty());\n    }\n  }\n  // disabled pending discussion with author (AMB)\n  // @Test\n  // public void testMultipolygon() throws Exception {\n  // Graph gg = new Graph();\n  // OpenStreetMapGraphBuilderImpl loader = new OpenStreetMapGraphBuilderImpl();\n  //\n  // FileBasedOpenStreetMapProviderImpl pr = new FileBasedOpenStreetMapProviderImpl();\n  // pr.setPath(new File(getClass().getResource(\"otp-multipolygon-test.osm\").getPath()));\n  // loader.setProvider(pr);\n  //\n  // loader.buildGraph(gg, extra);\n  //\n  // assertNotNull(gg.getVertex(\"way -3535 from 4\"));\n  // }\n}\n"

org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Disabled;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\n/**\n * Tests for planning with intermediate places\n * TODO OTP2 - Test is too close to the implementation and will need to be reimplemented.\n */\n@Disabled\npublic class TestIntermediatePlaces {\n\n  /**\n   * The spatial deviation that we allow in degrees\n   */\n  public static final double DELTA = 0.005;\n\n  private static ZoneId timeZone;\n\n  private static GraphPathFinder graphPathFinder;\n\n  private static Graph graph;\n\n  private static GraphPathToItineraryMapper graphPathToItineraryMapper;\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void setUp() {\n    try {\n      TestOtpModel model = FakeGraph.buildGraphNoTransit();\n      graph = model.graph();\n      TransitModel transitModel = model.transitModel();\n      FakeGraph.addPerpendicularRoutes(graph, transitModel);\n      FakeGraph.link(graph, transitModel);\n      model.index();\n      TestIntermediatePlaces.graphPathFinder = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(3));\n      timeZone = transitModel.getTimeZone();\n\n      graphPathToItineraryMapper =\n        new GraphPathToItineraryMapper(\n          timeZone,\n          graph.streetNotesService,\n          graph.ellipsoidToGeoidDifference\n        );\n    } catch (Exception e) {\n      e.printStackTrace();\n      assert false : \"Could not add transit data: \" + e.toString();\n    }\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testWithoutIntermediatePlaces() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.93080, -82.98522);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.96383, -82.96291);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = {};\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  @Disabled\n  public void testOneIntermediatePlace() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.93080, -82.98522);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.96383, -82.96291);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = { new GenericLocation(39.92099, -82.95570) };\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  @Disabled\n  public void testTwoIntermediatePlaces() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.93080, -82.98522);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.96383, -82.96291);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = new GenericLocation[2];\n    intermediateLocations[0] = new GenericLocation(39.92099, -82.95570);\n    intermediateLocations[1] = new GenericLocation(39.96146, -82.99552);\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTransitWithoutIntermediatePlaces() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9308, -83.0118);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9998, -83.0198);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = {};\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testThreeBusStopPlaces() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9058, -83.1341);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9058, -82.8841);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = { new GenericLocation(39.9058, -82.9841) };\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void
