GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = { new GenericLocation(39.9948, -83.0148) };\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTransitTwoIntermediatePlaces() {\n    GenericLocation fromLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9908, -83.0118);\n    GenericLocation toLocation = new GenericLocation(39.9998, -83.0198);\n    GenericLocation[] intermediateLocations = new GenericLocation[2];\n    intermediateLocations[0] = new GenericLocation(40.0000, -82.900);\n    intermediateLocations[1] = new GenericLocation(39.9100, -83.100);\n\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      false\n    );\n    handleRequest(\n      fromLocation,\n      toLocation,\n      intermediateLocations,\n      new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT, TraverseMode.WALK),\n      true\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void handleRequest(\n    GenericLocation from,\n    GenericLocation to,\n    GenericLocation[] via,\n    TraverseModeSet modes,\n    boolean arriveBy\n  ) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest(modes);\n    request.setDateTime(\"2016-04-20\", \"13:00\", timeZone);\n    request.setArriveBy(arriveBy);\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n    for (GenericLocation intermediateLocation : via) {\n      request.addIntermediatePlace(intermediateLocation);\n    }\n    try (var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, request)) {\n      var routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, temporaryVertices);\n      List<GraphPath> paths = graphPathFinder.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n      assertNotNull(paths);\n      assertFalse(paths.isEmpty());\n      List<Itinerary> itineraries = graphPathToItineraryMapper.mapItineraries(paths);\n      TripPlan plan = TripPlanMapper.mapTripPlan(request, itineraries);\n      assertLocationIsVeryCloseToPlace(from, plan.from);\n      assertLocationIsVeryCloseToPlace(to, plan.to);\n      assertTrue(1 <= plan.itineraries.size());\n      for (Itinerary itinerary : plan.itineraries) {\n        validateIntermediatePlacesVisited(itinerary, via);\n        assertTrue(via.length < itinerary.getLegs().size());\n        validateLegsTemporally(request, itinerary);\n        validateLegsSpatially(plan, itinerary);\n        if (modes.contains(TraverseMode.TRANSIT)) {\n          assertTrue(itinerary.getTransitDuration().toSeconds() > 0);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Check that every via location is visited in the right order\n  private void validateIntermediatePlacesVisited(Itinerary itinerary, GenericLocation[] via) {\n    int legIndex = 0;\n\n    for (GenericLocation location : via) {\n      Leg leg;\n      do {\n        assertTrue(\n          legIndex < itinerary.getLegs().size(),\n          \"Intermediate location was not an endpoint of any leg\"\n        );\n        leg = itinerary.getLegs().get(legIndex);\n        legIndex++;\n      } while (\n        Math.abs(leg.getTo().coordinate.latitude() - location.lat) > DELTA ||\n        Math.abs(leg.getTo().coordinate.longitude() - location.lng) > DELTA\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Check that the end point of a leg is also the start point of the next leg\n  private void validateLegsSpatially(TripPlan plan, Itinerary itinerary) {\n    Place place = plan.from;\n    for (Leg leg : itinerary.getLegs()) {\n      assertEquals(place.coordinate, leg.getFrom().coordinate);\n      place = leg.getTo();\n    }\n    assertEquals(place.coordinate, plan.to.coordinate);\n  }\n\n  // Check that the start time and end time of each leg are consistent\n  private void validateLegsTemporally(RouteRequest request, Itinerary itinerary) {\n    Instant departTime;\n    Instant arriveTime;\n    if (request.arriveBy()) {\n      departTime = itinerary.getLegs().get(0).getStartTime().toInstant();\n      arriveTime = request.dateTime();\n    } else {\n      departTime = request.dateTime();\n      arriveTime = itinerary.getLegs().get(itinerary.getLegs().size() - 1).getEndTime().toInstant();\n    }\n    long sumOfDuration = 0;\n    for (Leg leg : itinerary.getLegs()) {\n      assertFalse(departTime.isAfter(leg.getStartTime().toInstant()));\n      assertFalse(leg.getStartTime().isAfter(leg.getEndTime()));\n\n      departTime = leg.getEndTime().toInstant();\n      sumOfDuration += leg.getDuration().toSeconds();\n    }\n    sumOfDuration += itinerary.getWaitingDuration().toSeconds();\n\n    assertFalse(departTime.isAfter(arriveTime));\n\n    // Check the total duration of the legs,\n    int accuracy = itinerary.getLegs().size(); // allow 1 second per leg for rounding errors\n    assertEquals(sumOfDuration, itinerary.getDuration().toSeconds(), accuracy);\n  }\n\n  private void assertLocationIsVeryCloseToPlace(GenericLocation location, Place place) {\n    assertEquals(location.lat, place.coordinate.latitude(), DELTA);\n    assertEquals(location.lng, place.coordinate.longitude(), DELTA);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/graph_builder/module/osm/TriangleInequalityTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.osm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.DataImportIssueStore.noopIssueStore;\n\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.net.URLDecoder;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.datastore.api.FileType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.datastore.file.FileDataSource;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.ConfiguredDataSource;\nimport org.opentripplanner.openstreetmap.OpenStreetMapProvider;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.intersection_model.ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Edge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.DominanceFunction;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.feed.OsmDefaultsConfig;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.feed.OsmExtractConfig;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.feed.OsmExtractConfigBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\npublic class TriangleInequalityTest {\n\n  private static Graph graph;\n  private static TransitModel transitModel;\n\n  private Vertex start;\n  private Vertex end;\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void onlyOnce() {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n\n    File file = new File(\n      URLDecoder.decode(\n        TriangleInequalityTest.class.getResource(\"NYC_small.osm.pbf\").getFile(),\n        StandardCharsets.UTF_8\n      )\n    );\n    FileDataSource dataSource = new FileDataSource(file, FileType.OSM);\n    ConfiguredDataSource<OsmExtractConfig> source = new ConfiguredDataSource<>(\n      dataSource,\n      new OsmExtractConfigBuilder().withSource(dataSource.uri()).build()\n    );\n    OpenStreetMapProvider provider = new OpenStreetMapProvider(\n      source,\n      new OsmDefaultsConfig(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    OpenStreetMapModule osmModule = new OpenStreetMapModule(\n      List.of(provider),\n      Set.of(),\n      graph,\n      transitModel.getTimeZone(),\n      noopIssueStore()\n    );\n    osmModule.setDefaultWayPropertySetSource(new DefaultWayPropertySetSource());\n    osmModule.buildGraph();\n  }\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void before() {\n    start = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:1919595913\");\n    end = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:42448554\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDefaultModes() {\n    checkTriangleInequality();\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkingOnly() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDrivingOnly() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkTransit() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.TRANSIT);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkBike() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDefaultModesBasicSPT() {\n    checkTriangleInequality(null);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkingOnlyBasicSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDrivingOnlyBasicSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkTransitBasicSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.TRANSIT);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkBikeBasicSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDefaultModesMultiSPT() {\n    checkTriangleInequality(null);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkingOnlyMultiSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityDrivingOnlyMultiSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkTransitMultiSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.TRANSIT);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangleInequalityWalkBikeMultiSPT() {\n    TraverseModeSet modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    checkTriangleInequality(modes);\n  }\n\n  private GraphPath getPath(RouteRequest options, Edge startBackEdge, Vertex u, Vertex v) {\n    return AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setOriginBackEdge(startBackEdge)\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, u, v))\n      .getShortestPathTree()\n      .getPath(v);\n  }\n\n  private void checkTriangleInequality() {\n    checkTriangleInequality(null);\n  }\n\n  private void checkTriangleInequality(TraverseModeSet traverseModes) {\n    assertNotNull(start);\n    assertNotNull(end);\n\n    RouteRequest prototypeOptions = new RouteRequest();\n\n    // All reluctance terms are 1.0 so that duration is monotonically increasing in weight.\n    prototypeOptions.stairsReluctance = (1.0);\n    prototypeOptions.setNonTransitReluctance(1.0);\n    prototypeOptions.turnReluctance = (1.0);\n    prototypeOptions.carSpeed = 1.0;\n    prototypeOptions.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    prototypeOptions.bikeSpeed = 1.0;\n\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel(10.0));\n\n    if (traverseModes != null) {\n      prototypeOptions.setStreetSubRequestModes(traverseModes);\n    }\n\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setDominanceFunction(new DominanceFunction.EarliestArrival())\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(prototypeOptions, graph, start, end))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(end);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    double startEndWeight = path.getWeight();\n    int startEndDuration = path.getDuration();\n    assertTrue(startEndWeight > 0);\n    assertEquals(startEndWeight, startEndDuration, 1.0 * path.edges.size());\n\n    // Try every vertex in the graph as an intermediate.\n    boolean violated = false;\n    for (Vertex intermediate : graph.getVertices()) {\n      if (intermediate == start || intermediate == end) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      GraphPath startIntermediatePath = getPath(prototypeOptions, null, start, intermediate);\n      if (startIntermediatePath == null) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      Edge back = startIntermediatePath.states.getLast().getBackEdge();\n      GraphPath intermediateEndPath = getPath(prototypeOptions, back, intermediate, end);\n      if (intermediateEndPath == null) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      double startIntermediateWeight = startIntermediatePath.getWeight();\n      int startIntermediateDuration = startIntermediatePath.getDuration();\n      double intermediateEndWeight = intermediateEndPath.getWeight();\n      int intermediateEndDuration = intermediateEndPath.getDuration();\n\n      // TODO(flamholz): fix traversal so that there's no rounding at the second resolution.\n      assertEquals(\n        startIntermediateWeight,\n        startIntermediateDuration,\n        1.0 * startIntermediatePath.edges.size()\n      );\n      assertEquals(\n        intermediateEndWeight,\n        intermediateEndDuration,\n        1.0 * intermediateEndPath.edges.size()\n      );\n\n      double diff = startIntermediateWeight + intermediateEndWeight - startEndWeight;\n      if (diff < -0.01) {\n        System.out.println(\"Triangle inequality violated - diff = \" + diff);\n        violated = true;\n      }\n      //assertTrue(startIntermediateDuration + intermediateEndDuration >=\n      //        startEndDuration);\n    }\n\n    assertFalse(violated);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.osm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.DataImportIssueStore.noopIssueStore;\n\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.net.URL;\nimport java.net.URLDecoder;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.openstreetmap.OpenStreetMapProvider;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Edge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\n/**\n * Verify that OSM ways that represent proposed or as yet unbuilt roads are not used for routing.\n * This tests functionality in or around the method isWayRoutable() in the OSM graph builder\n * module.\n *\n * @author abyrd\n */\npublic class UnroutableTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void setUp() throws Exception {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    TransitModel transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n\n    URL osmDataUrl = getClass().getResource(\"bridge_construction.osm.pbf\");\n    File osmDataFile = new File(URLDecoder.decode(osmDataUrl.getFile(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n    OpenStreetMapProvider provider = new OpenStreetMapProvider(osmDataFile, true);\n    OpenStreetMapModule osmBuilder = new OpenStreetMapModule(\n      List.of(provider),\n      Set.of(),\n      graph,\n      transitModel.getTimeZone(),\n      noopIssueStore()\n    );\n    osmBuilder.setDefaultWayPropertySetSource(new DefaultWayPropertySetSource());\n    osmBuilder.buildGraph();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Search for a path across the Willamette river. This OSM data includes a bridge that is not yet\n   * built and is therefore tagged highway=construction.\n   * TODO also test unbuilt, proposed, raceways etc.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void testOnBoardRouting() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n\n    Vertex from = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:2003617278\");\n    Vertex to = graph.getVertex(\"osm:node:40446276\");\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, from, to))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = spt.getPath(to);\n    // At the time of writing this test, the router simply doesn't find a path at all when highway=construction\n    // is filtered out, thus the null check.\n    if (path != null) {\n      for (Edge edge : path.edges) {\n        assertNotEquals(\n          \"Path should not use the as-yet unbuilt Tilikum Crossing bridge.\",\n          \"Tilikum Crossing\",\n          edge.getDefaultName()\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/model/TimetableTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.model;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.util.TestUtils.AUGUST;\n\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.TripDescriptor;\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.TripUpdate;\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.TripUpdate.StopTimeEvent;\nimport com.google.transit.realtime.GtfsRealtime.TripUpdate.StopTimeUpdate;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.LocalDate;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.TripPattern;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.timetable.RealTimeState;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.updater.stoptime.BackwardsDelayPropagationType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.TestUtils;\n\npublic class TimetableTest {\n\n  private static final ZoneId timeZone = ZoneId.of(\"America/New_York\");\n  private static final LocalDate serviceDate = LocalDate.of(2009, 8, 7);\n  private static Graph graph;\n  private static TransitModel transitModel;\n  private static Map<FeedScopedId, TripPattern> patternIndex;\n  private static TripPattern pattern;\n  private static Timetable timetable;\n  private static String feedId;\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void setUp() throws Exception {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildGtfsGraph(ConstantsForTests.FAKE_GTFS);\n    graph = model.graph();\n    transitModel = model.transitModel();\n\n    feedId = transitModel.getFeedIds().stream().findFirst().get();\n    patternIndex = new HashMap<>();\n\n    for (TripPattern pattern : transitModel.getAllTripPatterns()) {\n      pattern.scheduledTripsAsStream().forEach(trip -> patternIndex.put(trip.getId(), pattern));\n    }\n\n    pattern = patternIndex.get(new FeedScopedId(feedId, \"1.1\"));\n    timetable = pattern.getScheduledTimetable();\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testUpdate() {\n    TripUpdate tripUpdate;\n    TripUpdate.Builder tripUpdateBuilder;\n    TripDescriptor.Builder tripDescriptorBuilder;\n    StopTimeUpdate.Builder stopTimeUpdateBuilder;\n    StopTimeEvent.Builder stopTimeEventBuilder;\n\n    int trip_1_1_index = timetable.getTripIndex(new FeedScopedId(feedId, \"1.1\"));\n    Vertex stop_a = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":A\");\n    Vertex stop_c = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":C\");\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n\n    ShortestPathTree spt;\n    GraphPath path;\n\n    // non-existing trip\n    tripDescriptorBuilder = TripDescriptor.newBuilder();\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setTripId(\"b\");\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(TripDescriptor.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    tripUpdateBuilder = TripUpdate.newBuilder();\n    tripUpdateBuilder.setTrip(tripDescriptorBuilder);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder = tripUpdateBuilder.addStopTimeUpdateBuilder(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setStopSequence(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(StopTimeUpdate.ScheduleRelationship.NO_DATA);\n    tripUpdate = tripUpdateBuilder.build();\n    var patch = timetable.createUpdatedTripTimes(\n      tripUpdate,\n      timeZone,\n      serviceDate,\n      BackwardsDelayPropagationType.REQUIRED_NO_DATA\n    );\n    assertNull(patch);\n\n    // update trip with bad data\n    tripDescriptorBuilder = TripDescriptor.newBuilder();\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setTripId(\"1.1\");\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(TripDescriptor.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    tripUpdateBuilder = TripUpdate.newBuilder();\n    tripUpdateBuilder.setTrip(tripDescriptorBuilder);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder = tripUpdateBuilder.addStopTimeUpdateBuilder(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setStopSequence(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(StopTimeUpdate.ScheduleRelationship.SKIPPED);\n    tripUpdate = tripUpdateBuilder.build();\n    patch =\n      timetable.createUpdatedTripTimes(\n        tripUpdate,\n        timeZone,\n        serviceDate,\n        BackwardsDelayPropagationType.REQUIRED_NO_DATA\n      );\n    assertNull(patch);\n\n    // update trip with non-increasing data\n    tripDescriptorBuilder = TripDescriptor.newBuilder();\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setTripId(\"1.1\");\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(TripDescriptor.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    tripUpdateBuilder = TripUpdate.newBuilder();\n    tripUpdateBuilder.setTrip(tripDescriptorBuilder);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder = tripUpdateBuilder.addStopTimeUpdateBuilder(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setStopSequence(2);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(StopTimeUpdate.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    stopTimeEventBuilder = stopTimeUpdateBuilder.getArrivalBuilder();\n    stopTimeEventBuilder.setTime(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, AUGUST, 7, 0, 10, 1)\n    );\n    stopTimeEventBuilder = stopTimeUpdateBuilder.getDepartureBuilder();\n    stopTimeEventBuilder.setTime(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, AUGUST, 7, 0, 10, 0)\n    );\n    tripUpdate = tripUpdateBuilder.build();\n    patch =\n      timetable.createUpdatedTripTimes(\n        tripUpdate,\n        timeZone,\n        serviceDate,\n        BackwardsDelayPropagationType.REQUIRED_NO_DATA\n      );\n    assertNull(patch);\n\n    //---\n    long startTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, AUGUST, 7, 0, 0, 0);\n    options.setDateTime(Instant.ofEpochSecond(startTime));\n\n    // update trip\n    tripDescriptorBuilder = TripDescriptor.newBuilder();\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setTripId(\"1.1\");\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(TripDescriptor.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    tripUpdateBuilder = TripUpdate.newBuilder();\n    tripUpdateBuilder.setTrip(tripDescriptorBuilder);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder = tripUpdateBuilder.addStopTimeUpdateBuilder(0);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setStopSequence(1);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(StopTimeUpdate.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    stopTimeEventBuilder = stopTimeUpdateBuilder.getArrivalBuilder();\n    stopTimeEventBuilder.setTime(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, AUGUST, 7, 0, 2, 0)\n    );\n    stopTimeEventBuilder = stopTimeUpdateBuilder.getDepartureBuilder();\n    stopTimeEventBuilder.setTime(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, AUGUST, 7, 0, 2, 0)\n    );\n    tripUpdate = tripUpdateBuilder.build();\n    assertEquals(20 * 60, timetable.getTripTimes(trip_1_1_index).getArrivalTime(2));\n    patch =\n      timetable.createUpdatedTripTimes(\n        tripUpdate,\n        timeZone,\n        serviceDate,\n        BackwardsDelayPropagationType.REQUIRED_NO_DATA\n      );\n    var updatedTripTimes = patch.getTripTimes();\n    assertNotNull(updatedTripTimes);\n    timetable.setTripTimes(trip_1_1_index, updatedTripTimes);\n    assertEquals(20 * 60 + 120, timetable.getTripTimes(trip_1_1_index).getArrivalTime(2));\n\n    // update trip arrival time incorrectly\n    tripDescriptorBuilder = TripDescriptor.newBuilder();\n    tripDescriptorBuilder.setTripId(\"1.1\");\n than to departure time on a stop, we should not try to fix it by default because the spec\n    // only allows you to drop all estimates for a stop when it's passed according to schedule\n stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setStopSequence(3);\n    stopTimeUpdateBuilder.setScheduleRelationship(StopTimeUpdate.ScheduleRelationship.SCHEDULED);\n    StopTimeEvent.Builder stopTimeEventBuilder = stopTimeUpdateBuilder.getArrivalBuilder();\n    stopTimeEventBuilder.setDelay(-700);\n    TripUpdate tripUpdate = tripUpdateBuilder.build();\n    var timetable = this.timetable;\n    var patch = bl = new IntersectionVertex(graph, \"bl\", -74.01, 40.0);\n    br = new IntersectionVertex(graph, \"br\", -74.00, 40.0);\n\n    top =\n      new StreetEdge(\n        tl,\n        tr,\n transitModel.mergeStopModels(StopModel.of().withRegularStop(s1).withRegularStop(s2).build());\n\n    station1 = new TransitStopVertexBuilder().withGraph(graph).withStop(s1).build();\n    station2 = new TransitStopVertexBuilder().withGraph(graph).withStop(s2).build();\n    station1.addMode(TransitMode.RAIL);\n    station2.addMode(TransitMode.RAIL);\n\n    //Linkers aren't run otherwise in endTurns.add(right);\n    endTurns.add(rightBack);\n\n    TemporaryStreetLocation end = StreetVertexIndex.createTemporaryStreetLocationForTest(\n      \"end\",\n      new assertTrue(start.getX() < end.getX());\n    assertTrue(start.getY() < end.getY());\n\n    Collection<Edge> edges = end.getIncoming();\n\n    assertEquals(2, edges.size());\n\n    long startTime = the FreeEdge from the StreetLocation to the new IntersectionVertex\n    RouteRequest req = new RouteRequest();\n    State traversedOne = new State(start, req, null);\n    State currentState;\n    for graph.streetNotesService.removeStaticNotes(leftBack);\n    graph.streetNotesService.addStaticNote(\n      left,\n      wheelchairAlert,\n      StreetNotesService.WHEELCHAIR_MATCHER\n    );\n    graph.streetNotesService.addStaticNote(\n      leftBack,\n      wheelchairAlert,\n      StreetNotesService.WHEELCHAIR_MATCHER\n    );\n\n    req.setWheelchairAccessible(true);\n\n    start =\n e.traverse(traversedOne);\n      if (currentState != null) {\n        traversedOne = currentState;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    assertEquals(wheelchairAlerts, tempEdges.disposeEdges();\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testStreetLocationFinder() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    StreetVertexIndex finder = graph.getStreetIndex();\n some = (TemporaryStreetLocation) finder.getVertexForLocationForTest(\n      new GenericLocation(40.00, -74.00),\n      options,\n      true,\n      tempEdges\n    );\n    assertNotNull(some);\n\n (splitting)\"\n    );\n\n    Collection<Edge> edges = start.getOutgoing();\n    assertEquals(2, edges.size());\n\n    RouteRequest biking = new RouteRequest(new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.BICYCLE));\n    TemporaryStreetLocation end = (TemporaryStreetLocation) finder.getVertexForLocationForTest(\n      new GenericLocation(40.008, -74.0),\n      biking,\n }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTemporaryVerticesContainer() {\n    // test that it is possible to travel between two splits on the same street\n    RouteRequest walking = new end is set in the setRoutingContext call\n      ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(walking, graph, container))\n   }\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testNetworkLinker() {\n    int numVerticesBefore = graph.getVertices().size();\n    StreetLinkerModule.linkStreetsForTestOnly(graph, transitModel);\n    int vertex(\"market_leary\", 47.668669, -122.384392);\n    vertex(\"market_russell\", 47.668655, -122.382997);\n    vertex(\"market_20th\", 47.668684, -122.382117);\n\n    vertex(\"shilshole_24th\", edges(\"market_24th\", \"shilshole_24th\", \"shilshole_22nd\", \"shilshole_vernon\", \"shilshole_20th\");\n    edges(\"market_ballard\", \"ballard_turn\", \"ballard_22nd\", \"ballard_vernon\", \"ballard_20th\");\n    edges(\"market_leary\", \"leary_vernon\", \"leary_20th\");\n    edges(\"market_russell\", \"russell_20th\");\n\n    edges(\"market_22nd\", \"ballard_22nd\", \"shilshole_22nd\");\n    edges(\"leary_vernon\", \"ballard_vernon\", \"shilshole_vernon\");\n    edges(\"market_20th\", \"russell_20th\", \"leary_20th\", \"ballard_20th\", \"shilshole_20th\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForward() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    Vertex from = graph.getVertex(\"56th_24th\");\n    Vertex to = assertEquals(\"market_24th\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_ballard\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_22nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_leary\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"leary_vernon\", states.get(5).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"leary_20th\", states.get(6).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBack() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    options.setArriveBy(true);\n    Vertex from = graph.getVertex(\"56th_24th\");\n    Vertex to = graph.getVertex(\"leary_20th\");\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, from, to))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(from);\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n\n    assertTrue(states.size() == 6 || states.size() == 7);\n\n    assertEquals(\"56th_24th\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n\n    int n;\n    // we could go either way around the block formed by 56th, 22nd, market, and 24th.\n    if (states.size() == 7) {\n      assertEquals(\"market_24th\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n      assertEquals(\"market_ballard\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n      n = 0;\n    } else {\n      assertEquals(\"56th_22nd\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n      n = -1;\n    }\n\n    assertEquals(\"market_22nd\", states.get(n + 3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_leary\", states.get(n + 4).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"leary_vernon\", states.get(n + 5).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"leary_20th\", states.get(n + 6).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardExtraEdges() {\n    RouteRequest options = new new NonLocalizedString(\"near_shilshole_22nd\"),\n      false\n    );\n    new TemporaryConcreteEdge(from, graph.getVertex(\"shilshole_22nd\"));\n\n    TemporaryStreetLocation to = new TemporaryStreetLocation(\n      \"near_56th_20th\",\n      new Coordinate(-122.382347, 47.669518),\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"near_56th_20th\"),\n      true\n    );\n    new TemporaryConcreteEdge(graph.getVertex(\"56th_20th\"), to);\n\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, from, to))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(to);\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n\n    assertEquals(9, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"near_shilshole_22nd\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"shilshole_22nd\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"ballard_22nd\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_22nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_leary\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_russell\", states.get(5).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_20th\", states.get(6).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"56th_20th\", states.get(7).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"near_56th_20th\", states.get(8).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBackExtraEdges() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.walkSpeed = .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(from);\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n\n    assertEquals(9, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"near_shilshole_22nd\", assertEquals(\"market_22nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_leary\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"market_russell\", assertEquals(\"near_56th_20th\", states.get(8).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testMultipleTargets() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n\n    Set<Vertex> targets = new HashSet<>();\n    targets.add(graph.getVertex(\"shilshole_22nd\"));\n    targets.add(graph.getVertex(\"market_russell\"));\n    targets.add(graph.getVertex(\"56th_20th\"));\n    targets.add(graph.getVertex(\"leary_20th\"));\n\n    SearchTerminationStrategy strategy = new MultiTargetTerminationStrategy(targets);\n\n    Vertex v1 = graph.getVertex(\"56th_24th\");\n    Vertex v2 = graph.getVertex(\"leary_20th\");\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setTerminationStrategy(strategy)\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, v1, v2))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    for (Vertex v : targets) {\n      GraphPath path = tree.getPath(v);\n      assertNotNull(path, \"No path found for target \" + v.getLabel());\n    }\n  }\n\n  /****\n   * Private Methods\n   ****/\n\n  private SimpleConcreteVertex vertex(String label, double lat, double lon) {\n    return new SimpleConcreteVertex(graph, label, lat, lon);\n  }\n\n  private void edges(String... vLabels) {\n    for (int i = 0; i < vLabels.length - 1; i++) {\n      Vertex vA = graph.getVertex(vLabels[i]);\n      Vertex vB = graph.getVertex(vLabels[i + 1]);\n\n      new SimpleConcreteEdge(vA, vB);\n      new SimpleConcreteEdge(vB, vA);\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },

All tests use the same graph structure, but a part of\n * the graph is changed before running each test. The setup method runs for each test, so this is\n * not a problem.\n */\npublic class BikeRentalTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  private final String NON_NETWORK = \"non network\";\n  private Graph graph;\n  private TransitStopVertex S1;\n  private TemporaryStreetLocation T1, T2;\n  private TransitEntranceVertex E1;\n  private StreetVertex A, B, C, D;\n  private VehicleRentalPlaceVertex B1, B2;\n  private StreetEdge SE1, SE2, SE3;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void setUp() {\n    // Generate a very simple graph\n    //\n    //   A <-> B <-> C <-> D\n    //   A <-> S1\n    //   B <-> B1\n    //   C <-> B2\n    //   D <-> E1\n    //   D <-> T2\n\n    var otpModel = modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          S1 = stop(\"S1\", 47.500, 19.001);\n          A = intersection(\"A\", 47.500, 19.000);\n          B = intersection(\"B\", 47.510, 19.000);\n          C = intersection(\"C\", 47.520, 19.000);\n          D = intersection(\"D\", 47.530, 19.000);\n          E1 = entrance(\"E1\", 47.530, 19.001);\n\n          T1 = streetLocation(\"T1\", 47.500, 18.999, false);\n          T2 = streetLocation(\"T1\", 47.530, 18.999, true);\n\n          B1 = vehicleRentalStation(\"B1\", 47.510, 19.001);\n          B2 = vehicleRentalStation(\"B2\", 47.520, 19.001);\n\n          biLink(A, S1);\n          biLink(D, E1);\n\n          biLink(B, B1);\n          biLink(C, B2);\n\n          link(T1, A);\n          link(D, T2);\n\n          SE1 = street(A, B, 50, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n          SE2 = street(B, C, 1000, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n          SE3 = street(C, D, 50, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = otpModel.graph();\n  }\n\n  // This tests exists to test if the cost of walking with a bike changes\n  @Test\n  public void testWalkBikeOnly() {\n    SE2.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    var descriptor = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      B,\n      C,\n      false,\n      new RouteRequest(),\n      StreetMode.BIKE\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(List.of(\"WALK - null - BC street (3,759.40, 752)\"), descriptor);\n  }\n\n  // This tests exists to test if the cost of biking changes\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeOnly() {\n    var descriptor = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      B,\n      C,\n      false,\n      new RouteRequest(),\n      StreetMode.BIKE\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(List.of(\"BICYCLE - null - BC street (400.00, 200)\"), descriptor);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeRentalFromStation() {\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testFallBackToWalking() {\n    SE2.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      true,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - BEFORE_RENTING - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testNoBikesAvailable() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B1.getStation()).vehiclesAvailable = 0;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      true,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - BEFORE_RENTING - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testNoSpacesAvailable() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B2.getStation()).spacesAvailable = 0;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      true,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - BEFORE_RENTING - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - BC street (1,579.95, 790)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (1,655.14, 828)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (1,656.14, 828)\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testIgnoreAvailabilityNoBikesAvailable() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B1.getStation()).vehiclesAvailable = 0;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      false,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testIgnoreAvailabilityNoSpacesAvailable() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B2.getStation()).spacesAvailable = 0;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      false,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testFloatingBike() {\n    VehicleRentalPlace station = B1.getStation();\n    VehicleRentalVehicle vehicle = new VehicleRentalVehicle();\n    vehicle.latitude = station.getLatitude();\n    vehicle.longitude = station.getLongitude();\n    vehicle.id = station.getId();\n    vehicle.name = station.getName();\n    vehicle.vehicleType = RentalVehicleType.getDefaultType(station.getId().getFeedId());\n    B1.setStation(vehicle);\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FLOATING - E1 (561.19, 290)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      T2,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FLOATING - null (561.19, 290)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      T1,\n      E1,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FLOATING - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FLOATING - E1 (561.19, 290)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeRentalFromStationWantToKeepCantKeep() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B1.getStation()).isKeepingVehicleRentalAtDestinationAllowed = false;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      T2,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - null (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      T1,\n      E1,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n      \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeRentalFromStationWantToKeepCanKeep() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B1.getStation()).isKeepingVehicleRentalAtDestinationAllowed = true;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - E1 (601.19, 290)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      T2,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - null (601.19, 290)\"\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      T1,\n      E1,\n      40,\n      \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n      \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - CD street (560.19, 290)\",\n      \"null - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - E1 (601.19, 290)\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeRentalFromStationWantToKeepCanKeepButCostly() {\n    ((VehicleRentalStation) B1.getStation()).isKeepingVehicleRentalAtDestinationAllowed = true;\n    int keepRentedBicycleAtDestinationCost = 1000;\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      false,\n      keepRentedBicycleAtDestinationCost,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n      )\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      T2,\n      false,\n      keepRentedBicycleAtDestinationCost,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - null (651.38, 333)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - null (651.38, 333)\"\n      )\n    );\n\n    assertPath(\n      T1,\n      E1,\n      false,\n      keepRentedBicycleAtDestinationCost,\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION (may keep) - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - AB street (76.19, 38)\",\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (540.19, 280)\",\n        \"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - CD street (650.38, 333)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - E1 (651.38, 333)\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void noPathIfNoAllowedNetworks() {\n    assertNoRental(B, C, Set.of(), Set.of(NON_NETWORK));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void noPathIfBannedAndAllowedNetwork() {\n    assertNoRental(B, C, Set.of(TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK), Set.of(TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void pathIfWithOnlyAllowedNetworks() {\n    assertPathWithNetwork(B, C, Set.of(), Set.of(TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void noPathIfNetworkIsBanned() {\n    assertNoRental(B, C, Set.of(TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK), Set.of());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void pathIfWithoutBannedNetworks() {\n    assertPathWithNetwork(B, C, Set.of(NON_NETWORK), Set.of());\n  }\n\n  private void assertNoRental(\n    StreetVertex fromVertex,\n    StreetVertex toVertex,\n    Set<String> bannedNetworks,\n    Set<String> allowedNetworks\n  ) {\n    Consumer<RouteRequest> setter = options -> {\n      options.allowedVehicleRentalNetworks = allowedNetworks;\n      options.bannedVehicleRentalNetworks = bannedNetworks;\n    };\n\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(\"WALK - BEFORE_RENTING - BC street (1,503.76, 752)\"),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, false, setter),\n      \"departAt\"\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(\"WALK - HAVE_RENTED - BC street (1,503.76, 752)\"),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, true, setter),\n      \"arriveBy\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertPathWithNetwork(\n    StreetVertex fromVertex,\n    StreetVertex toVertex,\n    Set<String> bannedNetworks,\n    Set<String> allowedNetworks\n  ) {\n    Consumer<RouteRequest> setter = options -> {\n      options.allowedVehicleRentalNetworks = allowedNetworks;\n      options.bannedVehicleRentalNetworks = bannedNetworks;\n    };\n\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (464.00, 242)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - B2 (499.00, 257)\"\n      ),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, false, setter),\n      \"departAt\"\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(\n        \"BICYCLE - RENTING_FROM_STATION - BC street (464.00, 242)\",\n        \"null - HAVE_RENTED - B2 (499.00, 257)\"\n      ),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, true, setter),\n      \"arriveBy\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(Vertex fromVertex, Vertex toVertex, String... descriptor) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, true, List.of(descriptor), List.of(descriptor));\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean useAvailabilityInformation,\n    String... descriptor\n  ) {\n    assertPath(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      useAvailabilityInformation,\n      List.of(descriptor),\n      List.of(descriptor)\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    int keepRentedBicycleCost,\n    String... descriptor\n  ) {\n    assertPath(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      false,\n      keepRentedBicycleCost,\n      List.of(descriptor),\n      List.of(descriptor)\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean useAvailabilityInformation,\n    List<String> departAtDescriptor,\n    List<String> arriveByDescriptor\n  ) {\n    List<String> departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      false,\n      useAvailabilityInformation,\n      0\n    );\n    List<String> arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      true,\n      useAvailabilityInformation,\n      0\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(departAtDescriptor, departAt, \"departAt path\");\n    assertEquals(arriveByDescriptor, arriveBy, \"arriveBy path\");\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean useAvailabilityInformation,\n    int keepBicycleRentalCost,\n    List<String> departAtDescriptor,\n    List<String> arriveByDescriptor\n  ) {\n    List<String> departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      false,\n      useAvailabilityInformation,\n      keepBicycleRentalCost\n    );\n    List<String> arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      true,\n      useAvailabilityInformation,\n      keepBicycleRentalCost\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(departAtDescriptor, departAt, \"departAt path\");\n    assertEquals(arriveByDescriptor, arriveBy, \"arriveBy path\");\n  }\n\n  private List<String> runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean arriveBy,\n    boolean useAvailabilityInformation,\n    int keepRentedBicycleCost\n  ) {\n    return runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      arriveBy,\n      options -> {\n        options.useVehicleRentalAvailabilityInformation = useAvailabilityInformation;\n        options.allowKeepingRentedVehicleAtDestination = keepRentedBicycleCost > 0;\n        options.keepingRentedVehicleAtDestinationCost = keepRentedBicycleCost;\n      }\n    );\n  }\n\n  private List<String> runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean arriveBy,\n    Consumer<RouteRequest> optionsSetter\n  ) {\n    var options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setArriveBy(arriveBy);\n    options.vehicleRentalPickupTime = 42;\n    options.vehicleRentalPickupCost = 62;\n    options.vehicleRentalDropoffCost = 33;\n    options.vehicleRentalDropoffTime = 15;\n\n    optionsSetter.accept(options);\n\n    return runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      arriveBy,\n      options,\n      StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL\n    );\n  }\n\n  private List<String> runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean arriveBy,\n    RouteRequest options,\n    StreetMode streetMode\n  ) {\n    var bikeRentalOptions = options.getStreetSearchRequest(streetMode);\n\n    var tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(bikeRentalOptions, graph, fromVertex, toVertex))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    var path = tree.getPath(arriveBy ? fromVertex : toVertex);\n\n    if (path == null) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return path.states\n      .stream()\n      .filter(s -> s.getBackEdge() instanceof StreetEdge || s.getVertex() == toVertex)\n      .map(s ->\n        String.format(\n          Locale.ROOT,\n          \"%s - %s%s - %s (%,.2f, %d)\",\n          s.getBackMode(),\n          s.getVehicleRentalState(),\n          s.mayKeepRentedVehicleAtDestination() ? \" (may keep)\" : \"\",\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null,\n          s.getWeight(),\n          s.getElapsedTimeSeconds()\n        )\n      )\n      .collect(Collectors.toList());\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitEntranceVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitStopVertex;\n\n/**\n * Test switching between biking / walking over multiple edges.\n * <p>\n * arriveBy and departAt paths should be symmetric.\n */\npublic class BikeWalkingTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private TransitStopVertex S1, S2;\n  private TransitEntranceVertex E1;\n  private StreetVertex A, B, C, D, E, F, Q;\n  private StreetEdge AB, BC, CD, DE, EF;\n\n  @Test\n  public void testWalkOnly() {\n    assertWalkPath(\n      C,\n      F,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - CD street\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - DE street\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - EF street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // An initial cost should not be applied when biking all the way.\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeOnly() {\n    assertBikePath(\n      C,\n      F,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - CD street\",\n      \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - DE street\",\n      \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - EF street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // An cost should be applied when both dismounting and mounting the bike.\n  @Test\n  public void testMiddleWalkBike() {\n    AB.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.BICYCLE);\n    BC.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n    CD.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n    DE.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.BICYCLE);\n\n    assertPath(\n      A,\n      E,\n      StreetMode.BIKE,\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - AB street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 120 / 1100.0 - BC street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - CD street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 105 / 1010.0 - DE street\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 105 / 1010.0 - AB street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - BC street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 120 / 1100.0 - CD street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - DE street\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  // A cost should be applied when switching to walking only.\n  @Test\n  public void testEndingWalkBike() {\n    assertPath(\n      A,\n      F,\n      StreetMode.BIKE,\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - AB street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - BC street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 105 / 1010.0 - CD street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - DE street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - EF street\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - AB street\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 120 / 1100.0 - BC street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - CD street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - DE street\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - EF street\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  // No cost should be applied, since no dismounting/mounting takes place.\n  @Test\n  public void testOnlyWalkBike() {\n    assertBikePath(\n      A,\n      C,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - AB street\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - BC street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // A cost should not be applied when entering / leaving since no mount / dismount takes place.\n  @Test\n  public void testEntranceStopLinkWalking() {\n    assertWalkPath(\n      S1,\n      B,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - S1\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - AB street\"\n    );\n\n    assertWalkPath(\n      E,\n      E1,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - EF street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // A cost should not be applied when entering / leaving since no mount / dismount takes place.\n  @Test\n  public void testEntranceStopLinkBiking() {\n    AB.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      S1,\n      B,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - S1\",\n      \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - AB street\"\n    );\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      E,\n      E1,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - EF street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testEntranceStopLinkBikeWalking() {\n    EF.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      S1,\n      B,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - S1\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - AB street\"\n    );\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      E,\n      E1,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - EF street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // A cost should be applied, since a mounting / dismounting takes place when entering the street network.\n  @Test\n  public void testPathwayBiking() {\n    assertPath(\n      E,\n      S2,\n      StreetMode.BIKE,\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - EF street\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 160 / 1300.0 - E1S2 pathway\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 105 / 1010.0 - EF street\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 60 / 300.0 - E1S2 pathway\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testPathwayBikeWalking() {\n    EF.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      E,\n      S2,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - EF street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 60 / 300.0 - E1S2 pathway\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testPathwayWalking() {\n    assertWalkPath(\n      E,\n      S2,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - EF street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - E1\",\n      \"WALK - 60 / 120.0 - E1S2 pathway\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // A cost should be applied when dismounting bike only.\n  @Test\n  public void testElevatorWalking() {\n    assertWalkPath(\n      C,\n      Q,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 10 / 20.0 - CD street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 90 / 90.0 - Elevator\",\n      \"WALK - 20 / 20.0 - null\",\n      \"WALK - 0 / 1.0 - L-Q\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  // No cost is added since no mounting / dismounting takes place\n  @Test\n  public void testElevatorBikeWalking() {\n    CD.setPermission(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    assertBikePath(\n      C,\n      Q,\n      \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 100.0 - CD street\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 90 / 90.0 - Elevator\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 20 / 20.0 - null\",\n      \"🚲WALK - 0 / 1.0 - L-Q\",\n      \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testElevatorBiking() {\n    assertPath(\n      C,\n      Q,\n      StreetMode.BIKE,\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 5 / 10.0 - CD street\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 190 / 1090.0 - Elevator\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 20.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 0 / 1.0 - L-Q\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\"\n      ),\n      List.of(\n        \"null - 0 / 0.0 - null\",\n        \"BICYCLE - 105 / 1010.0 - CD street\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 90 / 90.0 - Elevator\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 20 / 20.0 - null\",\n        \"🚲WALK - 0 / 1.0 - L-Q\",\n        \"null - 0 / 1.0 - null\"\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  protected void setUp() throws Exception {\n    // Generate a very simple graph\n    //\n    //   TS1 <-> A <-> B <-> C <-> D <-> E <-> F <-> E1 <-> S2\n\n    var otpModel = modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          S1 = stop(\"S1\", 0, 45);\n          S2 = stop(\"S2\", 0.005, 45);\n          E1 = entrance(\"E1\", 0.004, 45);\n          A = intersection(\"A\", 0.001, 45);\n          B = intersection(\"B\", 0.002, 45);\n          C = intersection(\"C\", 0.003, 45);\n          D = intersection(\"D\", 0.004, 45);\n          E = intersection(\"E\", 0.005, 45);\n          F = intersection(\"F\", 0.006, 45);\n          Q = intersection(\"Q\", 0.009, 45);\n\n          elevator(StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE, D, Q);\n\n          biLink(A, S1);\n          AB = street(A, B, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          BC = street(B, C, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          CD = street(C, D, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n          DE = street(D, E, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n          EF = street(E, F, 100, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n          biLink(F, E1);\n          pathway(E1, S2, 60, 100);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = otpModel.graph();\n  }\n\n  private void assertBikePath(Vertex fromVertex, Vertex toVertex, String... descriptor) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, StreetMode.BIKE, List.of(descriptor), List.of(descriptor));\n  }\n\n  private void assertWalkPath(Vertex fromVertex, Vertex toVertex, String... descriptor) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, StreetMode.WALK, List.of(descriptor), List.of(descriptor));\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    List<String> expectedDepartAt,\n    List<String> expectedArriveBy\n  ) {\n    List<String> departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      streetMode,\n      false\n    );\n    List<String> arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      streetMode,\n      true\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(expectedDepartAt, departAt, \"departAt\");\n    assertEquals(expectedArriveBy, arriveBy, \"arriveBy\");\n  }\n\n  private List<String> runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    boolean arriveBy\n  ) {\n    var options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.bikeSwitchTime = 100;\n    options.bikeSwitchCost = 1000;\n    options.walkSpeed = 10;\n    options.bikeSpeed = 20;\n    options.bikeWalkingSpeed = 5;\n    options.setArriveBy(arriveBy);\n\n    var bikeOptions = options.getStreetSearchRequest(streetMode);\n\n    var tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(bikeOptions, graph, fromVertex, toVertex))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    var path = tree.getPath(arriveBy ? fromVertex : toVertex);\n\n    if (path == null) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return path.states\n      .stream()\n      .map(s ->\n        String.format(\n          \"%s%s - %s / %s - %s\",\n          s.getBackMode() != null && s.isBackWalkingBike() ? \"🚲\" : \"\",\n          s.getBackMode(),\n          s.getTimeDeltaSeconds(),\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? ((double) Math.round(s.getWeightDelta() * 10)) / 10 : 0.0,\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null\n        )\n      )\n      .collect(Collectors.toList());\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/CarPickupTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\n\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitEntranceVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitStopVertex;\n\n/**\n * Test CarPickup: - it may start with (WALK - WALK_TO_PICKUP, CAR - IN_CAR) - it may end with (WALK\n * - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF, CAR - IN_CAR) - StreetTransitEntityLink require mode changes to WALK -\n * StreetEdges may contain mode changes between CAR / WALK\n * <p>\n * arriveBy and departAt paths should be symmetric.\n */\npublic class CarPickupTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private TransitStopVertex S1;\n  private TransitEntranceVertex E1;\n  private StreetVertex A, B, C, D, E;\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupCarOnly() {\n    assertPath(B, C, \"null - IN_CAR - null, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupCarThenWalk() {\n    assertPath(\n      A,\n      C,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, WALK - WALK_TO_PICKUP - AB street, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupFromEntranceThenCar() {\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      C,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - S1, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupWalkFromEntranceThenCarThenWalk() {\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      D,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - S1, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street, WALK - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - CD street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupCarThenWalkToStop() {\n    assertPath(\n      B,\n      E1,\n      \"null - IN_CAR - null, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street, null - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - E1\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupWalkFromEntranceThenCarThenWalkToStop() {\n    assertPath(\n      S1,\n      E1,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - S1, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street, null - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - E1\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarPickupWalkThenCarThenWalk() {\n    assertPath(\n      A,\n      D,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, WALK - WALK_TO_PICKUP - AB street, CAR - IN_CAR - BC street, WALK - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - CD street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testWalkOnlyCarPickup() {\n    // This is a special case where the reverse states differ, due to both starting in the IN_CAR\n    // state and switching to walking when encountering the first edge. This is the only valid\n    // path since a CarPickup must be in `IN_CAR` or `WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF` to be a final state,\n    // and the path can't be traversed by car.\n    assertPath(\n      A,\n      B,\n      \"null - WALK_TO_PICKUP - null, WALK - WALK_TO_PICKUP - AB street\",\n      \"null - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - null, WALK - WALK_FROM_DROP_OFF - AB street\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  protected void setUp() throws Exception {\n    // Generate a very simple graph\n    //\n    //   A <-> B <-> C <-> D <-> E\n    //   TS1 <-^           ^-> TE1\n\n    var otpModel = modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          S1 = stop(\"S1\", 0, 45);\n          E1 = entrance(\"E1\", 0.004, 45);\n          A = intersection(\"A\", 0.001, 45);\n          B = intersection(\"B\", 0.002, 45);\n          C = intersection(\"C\", 0.003, 45);\n          D = intersection(\"D\", 0.004, 45);\n          E = intersection(\"E\", 0.005, 45);\n\n          biLink(B, S1);\n          biLink(C, E1);\n\n          street(A, B, 87, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n          street(B, C, 87, StreetTraversalPermission.CAR);\n          street(C, D, 87, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n          street(D, E, 87, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = otpModel.graph();\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(Vertex fromVertex, Vertex toVertex, String descriptor) {\n    String departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, false);\n    String arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, true);\n\n    assertDescriptors(descriptor, descriptor, arriveBy, departAt);\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    String expectedDepartAt,\n    String expectedArriveBy\n  ) {\n    String departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, false);\n    String arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(fromVertex, toVertex, true);\n\n    assertDescriptors(expectedDepartAt, expectedArriveBy, arriveBy, departAt);\n  }\n\n  private void assertDescriptors(\n    String expectedDepartAt,\n    String expectedArriveBy,\n    String arriveBy,\n    String departAt\n  ) {\n    String formatString = \"DepartAt: %s%nArriveBy: %s\";\n\n    assertEquals(\n      String.format(formatString, expectedDepartAt, expectedArriveBy),\n      String.format(formatString, departAt, arriveBy)\n    );\n  }\n\n  private String runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    boolean arriveBy\n  ) {\n    var options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setArriveBy(arriveBy);\n\n    var carPickupOptions = options.getStreetSearchRequest(StreetMode.CAR_PICKUP);\n    var tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(carPickupOptions, graph, fromVertex, toVertex))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n    var path = tree.getPath(arriveBy ? fromVertex : toVertex);\n\n    return path != null\n      ? path.states\n        .stream()\n        .map(s ->\n          String.format(\n            \"%s - %s - %s\",\n            s.getBackMode(),\n            s.getCarPickupState(),\n            s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null\n          )\n        )\n        .collect(Collectors.joining(\", \"))\n      : \"path not found\";\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/DominanceFunctionTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.StateData;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.DominanceFunction;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitStopVertex;\n\npublic class DominanceFunctionTest {\n\n  @Test\n  public void testGeneralDominanceFunction() {\n    DominanceFunction minimumWeightDominanceFunction = new DominanceFunction.MinimumWeight();\n    Vertex fromVertex = mock(TransitStopVertex.class);\n    Vertex toVertex = mock(TransitStopVertex.class);\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n\n    // Test if domination works in the general case\n\n    State stateA = new State(\n      fromVertex,\n      Instant.EPOCH,\n      null,\n      StateData.getInitialStateData(request)\n    );\n    State stateB = new State(toVertex, Instant.EPOCH, null, StateData.getInitialStateData(request));\n    stateA.weight = 1;\n    stateB.weight = 2;\n\n    assertTrue(minimumWeightDominanceFunction.betterOrEqualAndComparable(stateA, stateB));\n    assertFalse(minimumWeightDominanceFunction.betterOrEqualAndComparable(stateB, stateA));\n  }\n  // TODO: Make unit tests for rest of dominance functionality\n  // TODO: Make functional tests for concepts covered by dominance with current algorithm\n  // (Specific transfers, bike rental, park and ride, turn restrictions)\n}\n"
    },
