org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.WheelchairAccessibility;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.Route;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.TripPattern;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.organization.Agency;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.Entrance;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.PathwayMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.RegularStop;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic abstract class GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  public static final String TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK = \"test network\";\n\n  public static String graphPathToString(GraphPath graphPath) {\n    return graphPath.states\n      .stream()\n      .flatMap(s ->\n        Stream.of(\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null,\n          s.getVertex().getDefaultName()\n        )\n      )\n      .filter(Objects::nonNull)\n      .collect(Collectors.joining(\" - \"));\n  }\n\n  protected TestOtpModel modelOf(Builder builder) {\n    builder.build();\n    Graph graph = builder.graph();\n    TransitModel transitModel = builder.transitModel();\n    return new TestOtpModel(graph, transitModel).index();\n  }\n\n  protected GraphPath routeParkAndRide(\n    Graph graph,\n    StreetVertex from,\n    StreetVertex to,\n    TraverseModeSet traverseModeSet\n  ) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest(traverseModeSet);\n    RoutingContext rctx = new RoutingContext(request, graph, from, to);\n    request.parkAndRide = true;\n\n    return AStarBuilder.oneToOne().setContext(rctx).getShortestPathTree().getPath(to);\n  }\n\n  public abstract static class Builder {\n\n    private final Graph graph;\n    private final TransitModel transitModel;\n\n    protected Builder() {\n      var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n      var stopModel = new StopModel();\n      graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n      transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n    }\n\n    public abstract void build();\n\n    public Graph graph() {\n      return graph;\n    }\n\n    public TransitModel transitModel() {\n      return transitModel;\n    }\n\n    public <T> T v(String label) {\n      return vertex(label);\n    }\n\n    public <T> T vertex(String label) {\n      return (T) graph.getVertex(label);\n    }\n\n    // -- Street network\n    public IntersectionVertex intersection(String label, double latitude, double longitude) {\n      return new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, longitude, latitude);\n    }\n\n    public StreetEdge street(\n      StreetVertex from,\n      StreetVertex to,\n      int length,\n      StreetTraversalPermission permissions\n    ) {\n      return new StreetEdge(\n        from,\n        to,\n        GeometryUtils.makeLineString(from.getLat(), from.getLon(), to.getLat(), to.getLon()),\n        String.format(\"%s%s street\", from.getDefaultName(), to.getDefaultName()),\n        length,\n        permissions,\n        false\n      );\n    }\n\n    public List<StreetEdge> street(\n      StreetVertex from,\n      StreetVertex to,\n      int length,\n      StreetTraversalPermission forwardPermissions,\n      StreetTraversalPermission reversePermissions\n    ) {\n      return List.of(\n        new StreetEdge(\n          from,\n          to,\n          GeometryUtils.makeLineString(from.getLat(), from.getLon(), to.getLat(), to.getLon()),\n          String.format(\"%s%s street\", from.getDefaultName(), to.getDefaultName()),\n          length,\n          forwardPermissions,\n          false\n        ),\n        new StreetEdge(\n          to,\n          from,\n          GeometryUtils.makeLineString(to.getLat(), to.getLon(), from.getLat(), from.getLon()),\n          String.format(\"%s%s street\", from.getDefaultName(), to.getDefaultName()),\n          length,\n          reversePermissions,\n          true\n        )\n      );\n    }\n\n    public List<ElevatorEdge> elevator(StreetTraversalPermission permission, Vertex... vertices) {\n      List<ElevatorEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();\n      List<ElevatorOnboardVertex> onboardVertices = new ArrayList<>();\n\n      for (Vertex v : vertices) {\n        var level = String.format(\"L-%s\", v.getDefaultName());\n        var boardLabel = String.format(\"%s-onboard\", level);\n        var alightLabel = String.format(\"%s-offboard\", level);\n\n        var onboard = new ElevatorOnboardVertex(\n          graph,\n          boardLabel,\n          v.getX(),\n          v.getY(),\n          new NonLocalizedString(boardLabel)\n        );\n        var offboard = new ElevatorOffboardVertex(\n          graph,\n          alightLabel,\n          v.getX(),\n          v.getY(),\n          new NonLocalizedString(alightLabel)\n        );\n\n        new FreeEdge(v, offboard);\n        new FreeEdge(offboard, v);\n\n        edges.add(new ElevatorBoardEdge(offboard, onboard));\n        edges.add(new ElevatorAlightEdge(onboard, offboard, new NonLocalizedString(level)));\n\n        onboardVertices.add(onboard);\n      }\n\n      for (int i = 1; i < onboardVertices.size(); i++) {\n        var from = onboardVertices.get(i - 1);\n        var to = onboardVertices.get(i);\n\n        edges.add(new ElevatorHopEdge(from, to, permission, WheelchairAccessibility.POSSIBLE));\n        edges.add(new ElevatorHopEdge(to, from, permission, WheelchairAccessibility.POSSIBLE));\n      }\n\n      return edges;\n    }\n\n    // -- Transit network (pathways, linking)\n    public Entrance entranceEntity(String id, double latitude, double longitude) {\n      return Entrance\n        .of(TransitModelForTest.id(id))\n        .withName(new NonLocalizedString(id))\n        .withCode(id)\n        .withCoordinate(latitude, longitude)\n        .build();\n    }\n\n    public RegularStop stopEntity(String id, double latitude, double longitude) {\n      var stop = TransitModelForTest.stop(id).withCoordinate(latitude, longitude).build();\n      transitModel.mergeStopModels(StopModel.of().withRegularStop(stop).build());\n      return stop;\n    }\n\n    public TransitStopVertex stop(String id, double latitude, double longitude) {\n      return new TransitStopVertexBuilder()\n        .withGraph(graph)\n        .withStop(stopEntity(id, latitude, longitude))\n        .build();\n    }\n\n    public TransitEntranceVertex entrance(String id, double latitude, double longitude) {\n      return new TransitEntranceVertex(graph, entranceEntity(id, latitude, longitude));\n    }\n\n    public StreetTransitEntranceLink link(StreetVertex from, TransitEntranceVertex to) {\n      return new StreetTransitEntranceLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public StreetTransitEntranceLink link(TransitEntranceVertex from, StreetVertex to) {\n      return new StreetTransitEntranceLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public List<StreetTransitEntranceLink> biLink(StreetVertex from, TransitEntranceVertex to) {\n      return List.of(link(from, to), link(to, from));\n    }\n\n    public StreetTransitStopLink link(StreetVertex from, TransitStopVertex to) {\n      return new StreetTransitStopLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public StreetTransitStopLink link(TransitStopVertex from, StreetVertex to) {\n      return new StreetTransitStopLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public List<StreetTransitStopLink> biLink(StreetVertex from, TransitStopVertex to) {\n      return List.of(link(from, to), link(to, from));\n    }\n\n    public PathwayEdge pathway(Vertex from, Vertex to, int time, int length) {\n      return new PathwayEdge(\n        from,\n        to,\n        null,\n        new NonLocalizedString(\n          String.format(\"%s%s pathway\", from.getDefaultName(), to.getDefaultName())\n        ),\n        time,\n        length,\n        0,\n        0,\n        false,\n        PathwayMode.WALKWAY\n      );\n    }\n\n    // -- Street linking\n    public TemporaryStreetLocation streetLocation(\n      String name,\n      double latitude,\n      double longitude,\n      boolean endVertex\n    ) {\n      return new TemporaryStreetLocation(\n        name,\n        new Coordinate(longitude, latitude),\n        new NonLocalizedString(name),\n        endVertex\n      );\n    }\n\n    public TemporaryFreeEdge link(TemporaryVertex from, StreetVertex to) {\n      return new TemporaryFreeEdge(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public TemporaryFreeEdge link(StreetVertex from, TemporaryVertex to) {\n      return new TemporaryFreeEdge(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public List<TemporaryFreeEdge> biLink(StreetVertex from, TemporaryVertex to) {\n      return List.of(link(from, to), link(to, from));\n    }\n\n    // -- Vehicle rental\n    public VehicleRentalPlace vehicleRentalStationEntity(\n      String id,\n      double latitude,\n      double longitude,\n      String network\n    ) {\n      var vehicleRentalStation = new VehicleRentalStation();\n      vehicleRentalStation.id = new FeedScopedId(network, id);\n      vehicleRentalStation.name = new NonLocalizedString(id);\n      vehicleRentalStation.longitude = longitude;\n      vehicleRentalStation.latitude = latitude;\n      vehicleRentalStation.vehiclesAvailable = 2;\n      vehicleRentalStation.spacesAvailable = 2;\n      final RentalVehicleType vehicleType = RentalVehicleType.getDefaultType(network);\n      vehicleRentalStation.vehicleTypesAvailable = Map.of(vehicleType, 2);\n      vehicleRentalStation.vehicleSpacesAvailable = Map.of(vehicleType, 2);\n      vehicleRentalStation.isKeepingVehicleRentalAtDestinationAllowed = false;\n      return vehicleRentalStation;\n    }\n\n    public VehicleRentalPlaceVertex vehicleRentalStation(\n      String id,\n      double latitude,\n      double longitude,\n      String network\n    ) {\n      var vertex = new VehicleRentalPlaceVertex(\n        graph,\n        vehicleRentalStationEntity(id, latitude, longitude, network)\n      );\n      new VehicleRentalEdge(vertex, RentalVehicleType.getDefaultType(network).formFactor);\n      return vertex;\n    }\n\n    public VehicleRentalPlaceVertex vehicleRentalStation(\n      String id,\n      double latitude,\n      double longitude\n    ) {\n      return vehicleRentalStation(id, latitude, longitude, TEST_VEHICLE_RENTAL_NETWORK);\n    }\n\n    public StreetVehicleRentalLink link(StreetVertex from, VehicleRentalPlaceVertex to) {\n      return new StreetVehicleRentalLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public StreetVehicleRentalLink link(VehicleRentalPlaceVertex from, StreetVertex to) {\n      return new StreetVehicleRentalLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public List<StreetVehicleRentalLink> biLink(StreetVertex from, VehicleRentalPlaceVertex to) {\n      return List.of(link(from, to), link(to, from));\n    }\n\n    public VehicleParking vehicleParking(\n      String id,\n      double x,\n      double y,\n      boolean bicyclePlaces,\n      boolean carPlaces,\n      List<VehicleParkingEntranceCreator> entrances,\n      String... tags\n    ) {\n      return vehicleParking(id, x, y, bicyclePlaces, carPlaces, false, entrances, tags);\n    }\n\n    public VehicleParking vehicleParking(\n      String id,\n      double x,\n      double y,\n      boolean bicyclePlaces,\n      boolean carPlaces,\n      boolean wheelchairAccessibleCarPlaces,\n      List<VehicleParkingEntranceCreator> entrances,\n      String... tags\n    ) {\n      var vehicleParking = VehicleParking\n        .builder()\n        .id(TransitModelForTest.id(id))\n        .x(x)\n        .y(y)\n        .bicyclePlaces(bicyclePlaces)\n        .carPlaces(carPlaces)\n        .entrances(entrances)\n        .wheelchairAccessibleCarPlaces(wheelchairAccessibleCarPlaces)\n        .tags(List.of(tags))\n        .build();\n\n      var vertices = VehicleParkingHelper.createVehicleParkingVertices(graph, vehicleParking);\n      VehicleParkingHelper.linkVehicleParkingEntrances(vertices);\n      vertices.forEach(v -> biLink(v.getParkingEntrance().getVertex(), v));\n      return vehicleParking;\n    }\n\n    public VehicleParking.VehicleParkingEntranceCreator vehicleParkingEntrance(\n      StreetVertex streetVertex,\n      String id,\n      boolean carAccessible,\n      boolean walkAccessible\n    ) {\n      return builder ->\n        builder\n          .entranceId(TransitModelForTest.id(id))\n          .name(new NonLocalizedString(id))\n          .x(streetVertex.getX())\n          .y(streetVertex.getY())\n          .vertex(streetVertex)\n          .carAccessible(carAccessible)\n          .walkAccessible(walkAccessible);\n    }\n\n    public StreetVehicleParkingLink link(StreetVertex from, VehicleParkingEntranceVertex to) {\n      return new StreetVehicleParkingLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public StreetVehicleParkingLink link(VehicleParkingEntranceVertex from, StreetVertex to) {\n      return new StreetVehicleParkingLink(from, to);\n    }\n\n    public List<StreetVehicleParkingLink> biLink(\n      StreetVertex from,\n      VehicleParkingEntranceVertex to\n    ) {\n      return List.of(link(from, to), link(to, from));\n    }\n\n    public Route route(String id, TransitMode mode, Agency agency) {\n      return TransitModelForTest.route(id).withAgency(agency).withMode(mode).build();\n    }\n\n    // Transit\n    public void tripPattern(TripPattern tripPattern) {\n      transitModel.addTripPattern(tripPattern.getId(), tripPattern);\n    }\n\n    public StopTime st(TransitStopVertex s1) {\n      var st = new StopTime();\n      st.setStop(s1.getStop());\n      return st;\n    }\n\n    public StopTime st(TransitStopVertex s1, boolean board, boolean alight) {\n      var st = new StopTime();\n      st.setStop(s1.getStop());\n      st.setPickupType(board ? PickDrop.SCHEDULED : PickDrop.NONE);\n      st.setDropOffType(alight ? PickDrop.SCHEDULED : PickDrop.NONE);\n      return st;\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/ParkAndRideTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\n\npublic abstract class ParkAndRideTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  protected Graph graph;\n\n  public static List<String> graphPath(GraphPath graphPath) {\n    return graphPath.states\n      .stream()\n      .map(s ->\n        String.format(\n          \"%s%s - %s (%,.2f, %d)\",\n          s.getBackMode(),\n          s.isVehicleParked() ? \" (parked)\" : \"\",\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null,\n          s.getWeight(),\n          s.getElapsedTimeSeconds()\n        )\n      )\n      .collect(Collectors.toList());\n  }\n\n  protected void assertPathWithParking(\n    StreetVertex fromVertex,\n    StreetVertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    Set<String> bannedTags,\n    Set<String> requiredTags\n  ) {\n    assertNotEquals(\n      List.of(),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n        fromVertex,\n        toVertex,\n        streetMode,\n        false,\n        bannedTags,\n        requiredTags,\n        false\n      )\n    );\n    assertNotEquals(\n      List.of(),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n        fromVertex,\n        toVertex,\n        streetMode,\n        false,\n        bannedTags,\n        requiredTags,\n        true\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  protected void assertNoPathWithParking(\n    StreetVertex fromVertex,\n    StreetVertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    Set<String> bannedTags,\n    Set<String> requiredTags\n  ) {\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n        fromVertex,\n        toVertex,\n        streetMode,\n        false,\n        bannedTags,\n        requiredTags,\n        false\n      )\n    );\n    assertEquals(\n      List.of(),\n      runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n        fromVertex,\n        toVertex,\n        streetMode,\n        false,\n        bannedTags,\n        requiredTags,\n        true\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  protected void assertEmptyPath(Vertex fromVertex, Vertex toVertex, StreetMode streetMode) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, streetMode, false, List.of(), List.of());\n  }\n\n  protected void assertEmptyPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    boolean requireWheelChairAccessible\n  ) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, streetMode, requireWheelChairAccessible, List.of(), List.of());\n  }\n\n  protected void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    String... descriptor\n  ) {\n    assertPath(fromVertex, toVertex, streetMode, false, List.of(descriptor), List.of(descriptor));\n  }\n\n  protected void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    boolean requireWheelChairAccessible,\n    String... descriptor\n  ) {\n    assertPath(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      streetMode,\n      requireWheelChairAccessible,\n      List.of(descriptor),\n      List.of(descriptor)\n    );\n  }\n\n  protected List<String> runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    boolean requireWheelChairAccessible,\n    Set<String> bannedTags,\n    Set<String> requiredTags,\n    boolean arriveBy\n  ) {\n    var options = new RouteRequest().getStreetSearchRequest(streetMode);\n    options.bikeParkCost = 120;\n    options.bikeParkTime = 60;\n    options.carParkCost = 240;\n    options.carParkTime = 180;\n    options.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.makeDefault(requireWheelChairAccessible);\n    options.bannedVehicleParkingTags = bannedTags;\n    options.requiredVehicleParkingTags = requiredTags;\n    options.setArriveBy(arriveBy);\n\n    var tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, fromVertex, toVertex))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    var path = tree.getPath(arriveBy ? fromVertex : toVertex);\n\n    if (path == null) {\n      return List.of();\n    }\n\n    return path.states\n      .stream()\n      .map(s ->\n        String.format(\n          Locale.ROOT,\n          \"%s%s - %s (%,.2f, %d)\",\n          s.getBackMode(),\n          s.isVehicleParked() ? \" (parked)\" : \"\",\n          s.getBackEdge() != null ? s.getBackEdge().getDefaultName() : null,\n          s.getWeight(),\n          s.getElapsedTimeSeconds()\n        )\n      )\n      .collect(Collectors.toList());\n  }\n\n  private void assertPath(\n    Vertex fromVertex,\n    Vertex toVertex,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    boolean requireWheelChairAccessible,\n    List<String> departAtDescriptor,\n    List<String> arriveByDescriptor\n  ) {\n    var departAt = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      streetMode,\n      requireWheelChairAccessible,\n      Set.of(),\n      Set.of(),\n      false\n    );\n    var arriveBy = runStreetSearchAndCreateDescriptor(\n      fromVertex,\n      toVertex,\n      streetMode,\n      requireWheelChairAccessible,\n      Set.of(),\n      Set.of(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(departAtDescriptor, departAt, \"departAt path\");\n    assertEquals(arriveByDescriptor, arriveBy, \"arriveBy path\");\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/StreetModeLinkingTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\n\nimport java.util.function.BiFunction;\nimport java.util.function.Consumer;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Disabled;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.StreetLinkerModule;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TemporaryVerticesContainer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\n/**\n * This tests linking of GenericLocations to streets for each StreetMode. The test has 5 parallel\n * streets and a linking is performed for each mode in the middle of each street. Currently linking\n * is handled in three different ways: for CAR, CAR_PARK and everything else, which is reflected in\n * the tests.\n */\npublic class StreetModeLinkingTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private TransitModel transitModel;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  protected void setUp() throws Exception {\n    var otpModel = modelOf(\n      new GraphRoutingTest.Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          street(\n            intersection(\"A1\", 47.5000, 19.00),\n            intersection(\"A2\", 47.5020, 19.00),\n            100,\n            StreetTraversalPermission.CAR\n          );\n\n          street(\n            intersection(\"B1\", 47.5000, 19.01),\n            intersection(\"B2\", 47.5020, 19.01),\n            100,\n            StreetTraversalPermission.ALL\n          );\n\n          street(\n            intersection(\"C1\", 47.5000, 19.02),\n            intersection(\"C2\", 47.5020, 19.02),\n            100,\n            StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE\n          );\n\n          street(\n            intersection(\"D1\", 47.500, 19.03),\n            intersection(\"D2\", 47.502, 19.03),\n            100,\n            StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN\n          )\n            .setWheelchairAccessible(false);\n\n          street(\n            intersection(\"E1\", 47.500, 19.04),\n            intersection(\"E2\", 47.502, 19.04),\n            100,\n            StreetTraversalPermission.BICYCLE_AND_CAR\n          );\n\n          stop(\"STOP\", 47.501, 19.04);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = otpModel.graph();\n\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    transitModel = otpModel.transitModel();\n    StreetLinkerModule.linkStreetsForTestOnly(graph, transitModel);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarLinking() {\n    /*\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.00, \"A1A2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.01, \"B1B2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.02, \"B1B2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.03, \"E1E2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.04, \"E1E2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n     */\n    assertLinkedFromTo(\"STOP\", \"E1E2 street\", StreetMode.CAR);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCarParkLinking() {\n    var setup = (BiFunction<Double, Double, Consumer<RouteRequest>>) (\n      Double latitude,\n      Double longitude\n    ) -> {\n      return (RouteRequest rr) -> {\n        rr.setFrom(new GenericLocation(latitude, longitude));\n        rr.setTo(new GenericLocation(latitude, longitude));\n        rr.parkAndRide = true;\n      };\n    };\n\n    assertLinking(setup.apply(47.501, 19.00), \"A1A2 street\", \"B1B2 street\", StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK);\n    assertLinking(setup.apply(47.501, 19.01), \"B1B2 street\", \"B1B2 street\", StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK);\n    assertLinking(setup.apply(47.501, 19.02), \"B1B2 street\", \"C1C2 street\", StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK);\n    assertLinking(setup.apply(47.501, 19.03), \"E1E2 street\", \"D1D2 street\", StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK);\n    assertLinking(setup.apply(47.501, 19.04), \"E1E2 street\", \"D1D2 street\", StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK);\n    assertLinking(\n      rr -> {\n        rr.setFrom(new GenericLocation(null, TransitModelForTest.id(\"STOP\"), null, null));\n        rr.setTo(new GenericLocation(null, TransitModelForTest.id(\"STOP\"), null, null));\n      },\n      \"E1E2 street\",\n      \"D1D2 street\",\n      StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Only CAR linking is handled specially, since walking with a bike is always a possibility,\n  // and so no difference is made between BIKE/WALK:\n\n  @Test\n  public void testDefaultLinking() {\n    var streetModes = new StreetMode[] {\n      StreetMode.WALK,\n      StreetMode.BIKE,\n      StreetMode.BIKE_TO_PARK,\n      StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL,\n      StreetMode.SCOOTER_RENTAL,\n      StreetMode.FLEXIBLE,\n      StreetMode.CAR_PICKUP,\n      StreetMode.CAR_RENTAL,\n    };\n\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.00, \"B1B2 street\", streetModes);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.01, \"B1B2 street\", streetModes);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.02, \"C1C2 street\", streetModes);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.03, \"D1D2 street\", streetModes);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(47.501, 19.04, \"D1D2 street\", streetModes);\n    assertLinkedFromTo(\"STOP\", \"D1D2 street\", streetModes);\n  }\n\n  // Linking to wheelchair accessible streets is currently not implemented.\n\n  @Test\n  @Disabled\n  public void testWheelchairLinking() {\n    assertLinking(\n      rr -> {\n        rr.setFrom(new GenericLocation(47.5010, 19.03));\n        rr.setTo(new GenericLocation(47.5010, 19.03));\n        rr.wheelchairAccessibility = WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.makeDefault(true);\n      },\n      \"C1C2 street\",\n      \"C1C2 street\",\n      StreetMode.WALK\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertLinkedFromTo(\n    double latitude,\n    double longitude,\n    String streetName,\n    StreetMode... streetModes\n  ) {\n    assertLinking(\n      rr -> {\n        rr.setFrom(new GenericLocation(latitude, longitude));\n        rr.setTo(new GenericLocation(latitude, longitude));\n      },\n      streetName,\n      streetName,\n      streetModes\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertLinkedFromTo(String stopId, String streetName, StreetMode... streetModes) {\n    assertLinking(\n      rr -> {\n        rr.setFrom(new GenericLocation(null, TransitModelForTest.id(stopId), null, null));\n        rr.setTo(new GenericLocation(null, TransitModelForTest.id(stopId), null, null));\n      },\n      streetName,\n      streetName,\n      streetModes\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertLinking(\n    Consumer<RouteRequest> consumer,\n    String fromStreetName,\n    String toStreetName,\n    StreetMode... streetModes\n  ) {\n    for (final StreetMode streetMode : streetModes) {\n      var routingRequest = new RouteRequest().getStreetSearchRequest(streetMode);\n\n      consumer.accept(routingRequest);\n\n      // Remove to, so that origin and destination are different\n      routingRequest.setTo(new GenericLocation(null, null));\n\n      try (var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, routingRequest)) {\n        RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(\n          routingRequest,\n          graph,\n          temporaryVertices\n        );\n\n        if (fromStreetName != null) {\n          assertFromLink(fromStreetName, streetMode, routingContext);\n        }\n      }\n\n      routingRequest = new RouteRequest().getStreetSearchRequest(streetMode);\n\n      consumer.accept(routingRequest);\n\n      // Remove from, so that origin and destination are different\n      routingRequest.setFrom(new GenericLocation(null, null));\n\n      try (var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, routingRequest)) {\n        RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(\n          routingRequest,\n          graph,\n          temporaryVertices\n        );\n\n        if (toStreetName != null) {\n          assertToLink(toStreetName, streetMode, routingContext);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  private void assertFromLink(\n    String streetName,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    RoutingContext routingContext\n  ) {\n    var fromVertex = routingContext.fromVertices.iterator().next();\n    var outgoing = fromVertex\n      .getOutgoing()\n      .iterator()\n      .next()\n      .getToVertex()\n      .getOutgoing()\n      .iterator()\n      .next();\n    assertEquals(\n      streetName,\n      outgoing.getDefaultName(),\n      String.format(\"%s should be linked to %s\", streetMode, streetName)\n    );\n  }\n\n  private void assertToLink(\n    String streetName,\n    StreetMode streetMode,\n    RoutingContext routingContext\n  ) {\n    var toVertex = routingContext.toVertices.iterator().next();\n    var outgoing = toVertex\n      .getIncoming()\n      .iterator()\n      .next()\n      .getFromVertex()\n      .getIncoming()\n      .iterator()\n      .next();\n\n    assertEquals(\n      streetName,\n      outgoing.getDefaultName(),\n      streetMode + \" should be linked to \" + streetName\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/TestBanning.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.id;\n\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.Route;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.organization.Agency;\n\n/**\n * Test the banning and whitelisting functionality in the RoutingRequest.\n * TODO This does not test the that banning/whitelisting affects the routing correctly.\n */\npublic class TestBanning {\n\n  @Test\n  public void testSetBannedOnRequest() {\n    Collection<Route> routes = getTestRoutes();\n\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n\n    routingRequest.setBannedRoutesFromString(\"F__RUT:Route:1\");\n    routingRequest.setBannedAgenciesFromSting(\"F:RUT:Agency:2\");\n\n    Collection<FeedScopedId> bannedRoutes = routingRequest.getBannedRoutes(routes);\n\n    assertEquals(2, bannedRoutes.size());\n    assertTrue(bannedRoutes.contains(id(\"RUT:Route:1\")));\n    assertTrue(bannedRoutes.contains(id(\"RUT:Route:3\")));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testSetWhiteListedOnRequest() {\n    Collection<Route> routes = getTestRoutes();\n\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n\n    routingRequest.setWhiteListedRoutesFromString(\"F__RUT:Route:1\");\n    routingRequest.setWhiteListedAgenciesFromSting(\"F:RUT:Agency:2\");\n\n    Collection<FeedScopedId> bannedRoutes = routingRequest.getBannedRoutes(routes);\n\n    assertEquals(1, bannedRoutes.size());\n    assertTrue(bannedRoutes.contains(id(\"RUT:Route:2\")));\n  }\n\n  private List<Route> getTestRoutes() {\n    Agency agency1 = TransitModelForTest.agency(\"A\").copy().withId(id(\"RUT:Agency:1\")).build();\n    Agency agency2 = TransitModelForTest.agency(\"B\").copy().withId(id(\"RUT:Agency:2\")).build();\n\n    return List.of(\n      TransitModelForTest.route(\"RUT:Route:1\").withAgency(agency1).build(),\n      TransitModelForTest.route(\"RUT:Route:2\").withAgency(agency1).build(),\n      TransitModelForTest.route(\"RUT:Route:3\").withAgency(agency2).build()\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/TurnCostTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.TurnRestriction;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.TurnRestrictionType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.intersection_model.ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic class TurnCostTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n\n  private Vertex topRight;\n\n  private Vertex bottomLeft;\n\n  private StreetEdge maple_main1, broad1_2;\n\n  private RouteRequest proto;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void before() {\n    graph = new Graph();\n\n    // Graph for a fictional grid city with turn restrictions\n    StreetVertex maple1 = vertex(\"maple_1st\", 2.0, 2.0);\n    StreetVertex maple2 = vertex(\"maple_2nd\", 1.0, 2.0);\n    StreetVertex maple3 = vertex(\"maple_3rd\", 0.0, 2.0);\n\n    StreetVertex main1 = vertex(\"main_1st\", 2.0, 1.0);\n    StreetVertex main2 = vertex(\"main_2nd\", 1.0, 1.0);\n    StreetVertex main3 = vertex(\"main_3rd\", 0.0, 1.0);\n\n    StreetVertex broad1 = vertex(\"broad_1st\", 2.0, 0.0);\n    StreetVertex broad2 = vertex(\"broad_2nd\", 1.0, 0.0);\n    StreetVertex broad3 = vertex(\"broad_3rd\", 0.0, 0.0);\n\n    // Each block along the main streets has unit length and is one-way\n    StreetEdge maple1_2 = edge(maple1, maple2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge maple2_3 = edge(maple2, maple3, 100.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge main1_2 = edge(main1, main2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main2_3 = edge(main2, main3, 100.0, false);\n\n    broad1_2 = edge(broad1, broad2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge broad2_3 = edge(broad2, broad3, 100.0, false);\n\n    // Each cross-street connects\n    maple_main1 = edge(maple1, main1, 50.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad1 = edge(main1, broad1, 100.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge maple_main2 = edge(maple2, main2, 50.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad2 = edge(main2, broad2, 50.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge maple_main3 = edge(maple3, main3, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad3 = edge(main3, broad3, 100.0, false);\n\n    // Turn restrictions are only for driving modes.\n    // - can't turn from 1st onto Main.\n    // - can't turn from 2nd onto Main.\n    // - can't turn from 2nd onto Broad.\n    DisallowTurn(maple_main1, main1_2);\n    DisallowTurn(maple_main2, main2_3);\n    DisallowTurn(main_broad2, broad2_3);\n\n    // Hold onto some vertices for the tests\n    topRight = maple1;\n    bottomLeft = broad3;\n\n    // Make a prototype routing request.\n    proto = new RouteRequest();\n    proto.carSpeed = 1.0;\n    proto.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    proto.bikeSpeed = 1.0;\n    proto.turnReluctance = (1.0);\n    proto.setNonTransitReluctance(1.0);\n    proto.stairsReluctance = (1.0);\n\n    // Turn costs are all 0 by default.\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel(0.0));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardDefaultNoTurnCosts() {\n    // Without turn costs, this path costs 2x100 + 2x50 = 300.\n    checkForwardRouteDuration(new RoutingContext(proto, graph, topRight, bottomLeft), 300);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardDefaultConstTurnCosts() {\n    RouteRequest options = proto.clone();\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel(10.0));\n\n    // Without turn costs, this path costs 2x100 + 2x50 = 300.\n    // Since we traverse 3 intersections, the total cost should be 330.\n    GraphPath path = checkForwardRouteDuration(\n      new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft),\n      330\n    );\n\n    // The intersection traversal cost should be applied to the state *after*\n    // the intersection itself.\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_2nd\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n\n    assertEquals(0, states.get(0).getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(50, states.get(1).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // maple_main1 = 50\n    assertEquals(160, states.get(2).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // main1_2 = 100\n    assertEquals(220, states.get(3).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // main_broad2 = 50\n    assertEquals(330, states.get(4).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // broad2_3 = 100\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardCarNoTurnCosts() {\n    RouteRequest options = proto.clone();\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n\n    // Without turn costs, this path costs 3x100 + 1x50 = 300.\n    GraphPath path = checkForwardRouteDuration(\n      new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft),\n      350\n    );\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_1st\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardCarConstTurnCosts() {\n    RouteRequest options = proto.clone();\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new ConstantIntersectionTraversalCostModel(10.0));\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n\n    // Without turn costs, this path costs 3x100 + 1x50 = 350.\n    // Since there are 3 turns, the total cost should be 380.\n    GraphPath path = checkForwardRouteDuration(\n      new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft),\n      380\n    );\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_1st\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n\n    assertEquals(0, states.get(0).getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(50, states.get(1).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // maple_main1 = 50\n    assertEquals(160, states.get(2).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // main1_2 = 100\n    assertEquals(270, states.get(3).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // broad1_2 = 100\n    assertEquals(380, states.get(4).getElapsedTimeSeconds()); // broad2_3 = 100\n  }\n\n  private GraphPath checkForwardRouteDuration(RoutingContext context, int expectedDuration) {\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder.oneToOne().setContext(context).getShortestPathTree();\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(bottomLeft);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // Without turn costs, this path costs 2x100 + 2x50 = 300.\n    assertEquals(expectedDuration, path.getDuration());\n\n    // Weight == duration when reluctances == 0.\n    assertEquals(expectedDuration, (int) path.getWeight());\n\n    for (State s : path.states) {\n      assertEquals(s.getElapsedTimeSeconds(), (int) s.getWeight());\n    }\n\n    return path;\n  }\n\n  /****\n   * Private Methods\n   ****/\n\n  private StreetVertex vertex(String label, double lat, double lon) {\n    return new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, lat, lon);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create an edge. If twoWay, create two edges (back and forth).\n   *\n   * @param back true if this is a reverse edge\n   */\n  private StreetEdge edge(StreetVertex vA, StreetVertex vB, double length, boolean back) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm = StreetTraversalPermission.ALL;\n    StreetEdge pse = new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, back);\n    pse.setCarSpeed(1.0f);\n    return pse;\n  }\n\n  private void DisallowTurn(StreetEdge from, StreetEdge to) {\n    TurnRestrictionType rType = TurnRestrictionType.NO_TURN;\n    TraverseModeSet restrictedModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    TurnRestriction restrict = new TurnRestriction(from, to, rType, restrictedModes, null);\n    from.addTurnRestriction(restrict);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/TurnRestrictionTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.TurnRestriction;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.TurnRestrictionType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic class TurnRestrictionTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n\n  private Vertex topRight;\n\n  private Vertex bottomLeft;\n\n  private StreetEdge maple_main1, broad1_2;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void before() {\n    graph = new Graph();\n\n    // Graph for a fictional grid city with turn restrictions\n    StreetVertex maple1 = vertex(\"maple_1st\", 2.0, 2.0);\n    StreetVertex maple2 = vertex(\"maple_2nd\", 1.0, 2.0);\n    StreetVertex maple3 = vertex(\"maple_3rd\", 0.0, 2.0);\n\n    StreetVertex main1 = vertex(\"main_1st\", 2.0, 1.0);\n    StreetVertex main2 = vertex(\"main_2nd\", 1.0, 1.0);\n    StreetVertex main3 = vertex(\"main_3rd\", 0.0, 1.0);\n\n    StreetVertex broad1 = vertex(\"broad_1st\", 2.0, 0.0);\n    StreetVertex broad2 = vertex(\"broad_2nd\", 1.0, 0.0);\n    StreetVertex broad3 = vertex(\"broad_3rd\", 0.0, 0.0);\n\n    // Each block along the main streets has unit length and is one-way\n    StreetEdge maple1_2 = edge(maple1, maple2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge maple2_3 = edge(maple2, maple3, 100.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge main1_2 = edge(main1, main2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main2_3 = edge(main2, main3, 100.0, false);\n\n    broad1_2 = edge(broad1, broad2, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge broad2_3 = edge(broad2, broad3, 100.0, false);\n\n    // Each cross-street connects\n    maple_main1 = edge(maple1, main1, 50.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad1 = edge(main1, broad1, 100.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge maple_main2 = edge(maple2, main2, 50.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad2 = edge(main2, broad2, 50.0, false);\n\n    StreetEdge maple_main3 = edge(maple3, main3, 100.0, false);\n    StreetEdge main_broad3 = edge(main3, broad3, 100.0, false);\n\n    // Turn restrictions are only for driving modes.\n    // - can't turn from 1st onto Main.\n    // - can't turn from 2nd onto Main.\n    // - can't turn from 2nd onto Broad.\n    DisallowTurn(maple_main1, main1_2);\n    DisallowTurn(maple_main2, main2_3);\n    DisallowTurn(main_broad2, broad2_3);\n\n    // Hold onto some vertices for the tests\n    topRight = maple1;\n    bottomLeft = broad3;\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testHasExplicitTurnRestrictions() {\n    assertFalse(maple_main1.getTurnRestrictions().isEmpty());\n    assertTrue(broad1_2.getTurnRestrictions().isEmpty());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardDefault() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.carSpeed = 1.0;\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(bottomLeft);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // Since there are no turn restrictions applied to the default modes (walking + transit)\n    // the shortest path is 1st to Main, Main to 2nd, 2nd to Broad and Broad until the\n    // corner of Broad and 3rd.\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_2nd\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardAsPedestrian() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest(TraverseMode.WALK);\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(bottomLeft);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // Since there are no turn restrictions applied to the default modes (walking + transit)\n    // the shortest path is 1st to Main, Main to 2nd, 2nd to Broad and Broad until the\n    // corner of Broad and 3rd.\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_2nd\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testForwardAsCar() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    options.carSpeed = 1.0;\n\n    ShortestPathTree tree = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, topRight, bottomLeft))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = tree.getPath(bottomLeft);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // If not for turn restrictions, the shortest path would be to take 1st to Main,\n    // Main to 2nd, 2nd to Broad and Broad until the corner of Broad and 3rd.\n    // However, most of these turns are not allowed. Instead, the shortest allowed\n    // path is 1st to Broad, Broad to 3rd.\n\n    List<State> states = path.states;\n    assertEquals(5, states.size());\n\n    assertEquals(\"maple_1st\", states.get(0).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"main_1st\", states.get(1).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_1st\", states.get(2).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_2nd\", states.get(3).getVertex().getLabel());\n    assertEquals(\"broad_3rd\", states.get(4).getVertex().getLabel());\n  }\n\n  /****\n   * Private Methods\n   ****/\n\n  private StreetVertex vertex(String label, double lat, double lon) {\n    return new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, lat, lon);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create an edge. If twoWay, create two edges (back and forth).\n   *\n   * @param back true if this is a reverse edge\n   */\n  private StreetEdge edge(StreetVertex vA, StreetVertex vB, double length, boolean back) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm = StreetTraversalPermission.ALL;\n    return new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, back);\n  }\n\n  private void DisallowTurn(StreetEdge from, StreetEdge to) {\n    TurnRestrictionType rType = TurnRestrictionType.NO_TURN;\n    TraverseModeSet restrictedModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    TurnRestriction restrict = new TurnRestriction(from, to, rType, restrictedModes, null);\n    from.addTurnRestriction(restrict);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/mapping/BikeRentalSnapshotTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping;\n\nimport au.com.origin.snapshots.junit5.SnapshotExtension;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CompletionException;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.ResourceLock;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.Resources;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RequestModes;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.error.RoutingValidationException;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.MainAndSubMode;\n\n@ExtendWith(SnapshotExtension.class)\n@ResourceLock(Resources.LOCALE)\npublic class BikeRentalSnapshotTest extends SnapshotTestBase {\n\n  private static final Locale DEFAULT_LOCALE = Locale.getDefault();\n\n  static GenericLocation p1 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"SW Johnson St. & NW 24th Ave. (P1)\",\n    null,\n    45.52832,\n    -122.70059\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p2 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Hoyt St. & NW 20th Ave. (P2)\",\n    null,\n    45.52704,\n    -122.69240\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p3 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Everett St. & NW 5th Ave. (P3)\",\n    null,\n    45.52523,\n    -122.67525\n  );\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void beforeClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(Locale.US);\n    loadGraphBeforeClass(false);\n  }\n\n  @AfterAll\n  public static void afterClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(DEFAULT_LOCALE);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct BIKE_RENTAL\")\n  @Test\n  public void directBikeRental() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * The next to two tests are an example where departAt and arriveBy searches return different (but\n   * still correct) results.\n   * <p>\n   * It's probably down to the intersection traversal because when you use a constant cost they\n   * become the same route again.\n   * <p>\n   * More discussion: https://github.com/opentripplanner/OpenTripPlanner/pull/3574\n   */\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct BIKE_RENTAL while keeping the bicycle at the destination with departAt\")\n  @Test\n  public void directBikeRentalArrivingAtDestinationWithDepartAt() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.allowKeepingRentedVehicleAtDestination = true;\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct BIKE_RENTAL while keeping the bicycle at the destination with arriveBy\")\n  @Test\n  public void directBikeRentalArrivingAtDestinationWithArriveBy() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.allowKeepingRentedVehicleAtDestination = true;\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n    request.setArriveBy(true);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Access BIKE_RENTAL\")\n  @Test\n  public void accessBikeRental() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 14, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.NOT_SET)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p3);\n\n    try {\n      expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n    } catch (CompletionException e) {\n      RoutingValidationException.unwrapAndRethrowCompletionException(e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Egress BIKE_RENTAL\")\n  @Test\n  public void egressBikeRental() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.NOT_SET)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(p3);\n    request.setTo(p1);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping;\n\nimport au.com.origin.snapshots.junit5.SnapshotExtension;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.ResourceLock;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.Resources;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RequestModes;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\n\n@ExtendWith(SnapshotExtension.class)\n@ResourceLock(Resources.LOCALE)\npublic class CarSnapshotTest extends SnapshotTestBase {\n\n  private static final Locale DEFAULT_LOCALE = Locale.getDefault();\n\n  static GenericLocation p1 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Pettygrove Ave. & NW 24th Ave. (P1)\",\n    null,\n    45.53261,\n    -122.70075\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p2 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Marshall St. & NW 24th Ave. (P2)\",\n    null,\n    45.53046,\n    -122.70067\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p3 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"Chapman Elementary School (P3)\",\n    null,\n    45.53335,\n    -122.70517\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p4 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"Legacy Good Samaritan Medical Center (P4)\",\n    null,\n    45.53060,\n    -122.69771\n  );\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void beforeClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(Locale.US);\n    loadGraphBeforeClass(false);\n  }\n\n  @AfterAll\n  public static void afterClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(DEFAULT_LOCALE);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct CAR_TO_PARK\")\n  @Test\n  public void directCarPark() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.CAR_TO_PARK).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct CAR_PICKUP (with walking both ends)\")\n  @Test\n  public void directCarPickupWithWalking() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.CAR_PICKUP).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p3);\n    request.setTo(p4);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct CAR_PICKUP (with walking both ends) - arriveBy\")\n  @Test\n  public void directCarPickupWithWalkingArriveBy() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 16, 54);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.CAR_PICKUP).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p3);\n    request.setTo(p4);\n    request.setArriveBy(true);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct CAR_PICKUP (without walking at either end)\")\n  @Test\n  public void directCarPickupWithoutWalking() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.CAR_PICKUP).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n    request.walkSpeed = 1;\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

java.util.concurrent.CompletionException;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.ResourceLock;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.Resources;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RequestModes;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.BicycleOptimizeType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.error.RoutingValidationException;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.MainAndSubMode;\n\n@ExtendWith(SnapshotExtension.class)\n@ResourceLock(Resources.LOCALE)\npublic class ElevationSnapshotTest extends SnapshotTestBase {\n\n  private static final Locale DEFAULT_LOCALE = Locale.getDefault();\n\n  static GenericLocation p1 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"SW Johnson St. & NW 24th Ave. (P1)\",\n    null,\n    45.52832,\n    -122.70059\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p2 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Hoyt St. & NW 20th Ave. (P2)\",\n    null,\n    45.52704,\n    -122.69240\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p3 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Everett St. & NW 5th Ave. (P3)\",\n    null,\n    45.52523,\n    -122.67525\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p4 = new GenericLocation(\"Sulzer Pump (P4)\", null, 45.54549, -122.69659);\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void beforeClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(Locale.US);\n    loadGraphBeforeClass(true);\n  }\n\n  @AfterAll\n  public static void afterClass() {\n    Locale.setDefault(DEFAULT_LOCALE);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct WALK\")\n  @Test\n  public void directWalk() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes = RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p4);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct BIKE_RENTAL\")\n  @Test\n  public void directBikeRental() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Direct BIKE\")\n  @Test\n  public void directBike() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n    request.bicycleOptimizeType = BicycleOptimizeType.TRIANGLE;\n    request.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor = 0.3;\n    request.bikeTriangleTimeFactor = 0.3;\n    request.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor = 0.4;\n\n    request.modes = RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.BIKE).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p4);\n    request.setArriveBy(true);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"Access BIKE_RENTAL\")\n  @Test\n  public void accessBikeRental() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 14, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.BIKE_RENTAL)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.NOT_SET)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p3);\n\n    try {\n      expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n    } catch (CompletionException e) {\n      RoutingValidationException.unwrapAndRethrowCompletionException(e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  @DisplayName(\"TRANSIT\")\n  @Test\n  public void transit() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 10, 21, 16, 10, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.NOT_SET)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(p3);\n    request.setTo(p1);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Override\n  protected TestOtpModel getGraph() {\n    return ConstantsForTests.getInstance().getCachedPortlandGraphWithElevation();\n  }\n}\n"

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/mapping/SnapshotTestBase.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping;\n\nimport static au.com.origin.snapshots.SnapshotMatcher.expect;\nimport static java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_TIME;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\n\nimport au.com.origin.snapshots.serializers.SerializerType;\nimport au.com.origin.snapshots.serializers.SnapshotSerializer;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonAutoDetect;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.util.DefaultIndenter;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.util.DefaultPrettyPrinter;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.util.Separators;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.MapperFeature;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jdk8.Jdk8Module;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jsr310.JavaTimeModule;\nimport java.net.URLEncoder;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestServerContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.api.mapping.ItineraryMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.api.parameter.ApiRequestMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.api.parameter.QualifiedMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.api.parameter.Qualifier;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.Itinerary;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.Leg;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.response.RoutingResponse;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.api.OtpServerRequestContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.MainAndSubMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.TransitMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.TestUtils;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.time.TimeUtils;\n\n/**\n * A base class for creating snapshots test of itinerary generation using the Portland graph.\n * <p>\n * If the snapshots need to be recreated run `mvn clean -Pclean-test-snapshots` to remove the\n * existing snapshots. When the tests are rerun new snapshots will be created.\n */\npublic abstract class SnapshotTestBase {\n\n  private static final DateTimeFormatter apiDateFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\n    \"MM-dd-yyyy\"\n  );\n  private static final DateTimeFormatter apiTimeFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\"H:mm%20a\");\n  private static final SnapshotSerializer snapshotSerializer = new SnapshotItinerarySerializer();\n  private static final ItineraryMapper itineraryMapper = new ItineraryMapper(Locale.ENGLISH, true);\n\n  static final boolean verbose = Boolean.getBoolean(\"otp.test.verbose\");\n\n  protected OtpServerRequestContext serverContext;\n\n  public static void loadGraphBeforeClass(boolean withElevation) {\n    if (withElevation) {\n      ConstantsForTests.getInstance().getCachedPortlandGraphWithElevation();\n    } else {\n      ConstantsForTests.getInstance().getCachedPortlandGraph();\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected OtpServerRequestContext serverContext() {\n    if (serverContext == null) {\n      TestOtpModel model = getGraph();\n      serverContext = TestServerContext.createServerContext(model.graph(), model.transitModel());\n    }\n\n    return serverContext;\n  }\n\n  protected TestOtpModel getGraph() {\n    return ConstantsForTests.getInstance().getCachedPortlandGraph();\n  }\n\n  protected RouteRequest createTestRequest(\n    int year,\n    int month,\n    int day,\n    int hour,\n    int minute,\n    int second\n  ) {\n    OtpServerRequestContext serverContext = serverContext();\n\n    RouteRequest request = serverContext.defaultRoutingRequest();\n    request.setDateTime(\n      TestUtils.dateInstant(\n        serverContext.transitService().getTimeZone().getId(),\n        year,\n        month,\n        day,\n        hour,\n        minute,\n        second\n      )\n    );\n    request.maxTransfers = 6;\n    request.setNumItineraries(6);\n    request.setSearchWindow(Duration.ofHours(5));\n\n    return request;\n  }\n\n  protected void printItineraries(\n    List<Itinerary> itineraries,\n    long startMillis,\n    long endMillis,\n    ZoneId timeZone\n  ) {\n    System.out.println(\"\\n\");\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < itineraries.size(); i++) {\n      Itinerary itinerary = itineraries.get(i);\n      System.out.printf(\n        \"Itinerary %2d - duration: %s [%5s] (effective: %s [%5s]) - wait time: %s, transit time: %s \\n\",\n        i,\n        TimeUtils.durationToStrCompact(itinerary.getDuration()),\n        itinerary.getDuration(),\n        TimeUtils.durationToStrCompact(itinerary.effectiveDuration()),\n        itinerary.effectiveDuration(),\n        itinerary.getWaitingDuration(),\n        itinerary.getTransitDuration()\n      );\n\n      for (int j = 0; j < itinerary.getLegs().size(); j++) {\n        Leg leg = itinerary.getLegs().get(j);\n        String mode = leg.getMode().isTransit() ? \"T\" : leg.getMode().name().substring(0, 1);\n        System.out.printf(\n          \" - leg %2d - %52.52s %9s --%s-> %-9s %-52.52s\\n\",\n          j,\n          leg.getFrom().toStringShort(),\n          ISO_LOCAL_TIME.format(leg.getStartTime().toInstant().atZone(timeZone)),\n          mode,\n          ISO_LOCAL_TIME.format(leg.getEndTime().toInstant().atZone(timeZone)),\n          leg.getTo().toStringShort()\n        );\n      }\n\n      System.out.println();\n    }\n\n    long printMillis = System.currentTimeMillis();\n\n    System.out.println(\n      \"  Request duration: \" + Duration.ofMillis(endMillis - startMillis).toMillis() + \" ms\"\n    );\n    System.out.println(\n      \"Request print time: \" + Duration.ofMillis(printMillis - endMillis).toMillis() + \" ms\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  protected void expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(RouteRequest request) {\n    List<Itinerary> itineraries = retrieveItineraries(request);\n\n    logDebugInformationOnFailure(request, () -> expectItinerariesToMatchSnapshot(itineraries));\n  }\n\n  protected void expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(RouteRequest request) {\n    RouteRequest departAt = request.clone();\n    List<Itinerary> departByItineraries = retrieveItineraries(departAt);\n\n    logDebugInformationOnFailure(request, () -> assertFalse(departByItineraries.isEmpty()));\n\n    logDebugInformationOnFailure(\n      departAt,\n      () -> expectItinerariesToMatchSnapshot(departByItineraries)\n    );\n\n    RouteRequest arriveBy = request.clone();\n    arriveBy.setArriveBy(true);\n    arriveBy.setDateTime(departByItineraries.get(0).lastLeg().getEndTime().toInstant());\n\n    List<Itinerary> arriveByItineraries = retrieveItineraries(arriveBy);\n\n    var departAtItinerary = departByItineraries.get(0);\n    var arriveByItinerary = arriveByItineraries.get(0);\n\n    logDebugInformationOnFailure(\n      arriveBy,\n      () ->\n        assertEquals(\n          asJsonString(itineraryMapper.mapItinerary(departAtItinerary)),\n          asJsonString(itineraryMapper.mapItinerary(arriveByItinerary))\n        )\n    );\n  }\n\n  protected void expectItinerariesToMatchSnapshot(List<Itinerary> itineraries) {\n    expect(itineraryMapper.mapItineraries(itineraries))\n      .serializer(snapshotSerializer)\n      .toMatchSnapshot();\n  }\n\n  protected void logDebugInformationOnFailure(RouteRequest request, Runnable task) {\n    try {\n      task.run();\n    } catch (Throwable e) {\n      System.err.println(\n        \"\\nTo recreate the snapshots used for verifying the tests run `mvn clean -Pclean-test-snapshots`.\"\n      );\n      System.err.println(\n        \"The basic details of the request may be viewed using the debug client (not all parameters can be encoded in the link):\"\n      );\n      System.err.println(\"\\n\\t\" + createDebugUrlForRequest(request));\n      throw e;\n    }\n  }\n\n  private static List<ApiRequestMode> mapModes(Collection<MainAndSubMode> reqModes) {\n    Set<TransitMode> transitModes = reqModes\n      .stream()\n      .map(MainAndSubMode::mainMode)\n      .collect(Collectors.toSet());\n    List<ApiRequestMode> result = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    if (ApiRequestMode.TRANSIT.getTransitModes().equals(transitModes)) {\n      return List.of(ApiRequestMode.TRANSIT);\n    }\n\n    for (TransitMode it : transitModes) {\n      for (ApiRequestMode apiCandidate : ApiRequestMode.values()) {\n        if (apiCandidate.getTransitModes().contains(it)) {\n          result.add(apiCandidate);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return result;\n  }\n\n  private static String asJsonString(Object object) {\n    return snapshotSerializer.apply(new Object[] { object });\n  }\n\n  private List<Itinerary> retrieveItineraries(RouteRequest request) {\n    long startMillis = System.currentTimeMillis();\n    RoutingResponse response = serverContext.routingService().route(request);\n\n    List<Itinerary> itineraries = response.getTripPlan().itineraries;\n\n    if (verbose) {\n      printItineraries(\n        itineraries,\n        startMillis,\n        System.currentTimeMillis(),\n        serverContext.transitService().getTimeZone()\n      );\n    }\n    return itineraries;\n  }\n\n  private String createDebugUrlForRequest(RouteRequest request) {\n    var dateTime = Instant\n      .ofEpochSecond(request.dateTime().getEpochSecond())\n      .atZone(serverContext().transitService().getTimeZone())\n      .toLocalDateTime();\n\n    var transitModes = mapModes(request.modes.transitModes);\n\n    var modes = Stream\n      .concat(\n        Stream\n          .of(\n            asQualifiedMode(request.modes.directMode, false),\n            asQualifiedMode(request.modes.accessMode, false),\n            asQualifiedMode(request.modes.egressMode, true)\n          )\n          .filter(Objects::nonNull)\n          .map(QualifiedMode::toString),\n        transitModes.stream().map(ApiRequestMode::name)\n      )\n      .distinct()\n      .collect(Collectors.joining(\",\"));\n\n    return String.format(\n      \"http://localhost:8080/?module=planner&fromPlace=%s&toPlace=%s&date=%s&time=%s&mode=%s&arriveBy=%s&wheelchair=%s\",\n      formatPlace(request.from()),\n      formatPlace(request.to()),\n      dateTime.toLocalDate().format(apiDateFormatter),\n      dateTime.toLocalTime().format(apiTimeFormatter),\n      modes,\n      request.arriveBy(),\n      request.wheelchairAccessibility\n    );\n  }\n\n  private String formatPlace(GenericLocation location) {\n    String formatted;\n    if (location.stopId != null) {\n      formatted = String.format(\"%s::%s\", location.label, location.stopId);\n    } else {\n      formatted = String.format(\"%s::%s,%s\", location.label, location.lat, location.lng);\n    }\n    return URLEncoder.encode(formatted, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n  }\n\n  private QualifiedMode asQualifiedMode(StreetMode streetMode, boolean isEgress) {\n    if (streetMode == null) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    switch (streetMode) {\n      case WALK:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.WALK);\n      case BIKE:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.BICYCLE);\n      case BIKE_TO_PARK:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.BICYCLE, Qualifier.PARK);\n      case BIKE_RENTAL:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.BICYCLE, Qualifier.RENT);\n      case SCOOTER_RENTAL:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.SCOOTER, Qualifier.RENT);\n      case CAR:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.CAR);\n      case CAR_TO_PARK:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.CAR, Qualifier.PARK);\n      case CAR_PICKUP:\n        return new QualifiedMode(\n          ApiRequestMode.CAR,\n          isEgress ? Qualifier.PICKUP : Qualifier.DROPOFF\n        );\n      case CAR_RENTAL:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.CAR, Qualifier.RENT);\n      case FLEXIBLE:\n        return new QualifiedMode(ApiRequestMode.FLEX);\n      default:\n        return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  private static class SnapshotItinerarySerializer implements SnapshotSerializer {\n\n    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;\n    private final DefaultPrettyPrinter pp;\n\n    private SnapshotItinerarySerializer() {\n      objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();\n      objectMapper.configure(SerializationFeature.ORDER_MAP_ENTRIES_BY_KEYS, true);\n      objectMapper.enable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_WITH_ZONE_ID);\n      objectMapper.disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS);\n      objectMapper.disable(SerializationFeature.FAIL_ON_EMPTY_BEANS);\n      objectMapper.configure(MapperFeature.SORT_PROPERTIES_ALPHABETICALLY, true);\n      objectMapper.setSerializationInclusion(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL);\n      objectMapper.registerModule(new JavaTimeModule());\n      objectMapper.registerModule(new Jdk8Module());\n\n      objectMapper.setVisibility(\n        objectMapper\n          .getSerializationConfig()\n          .getDefaultVisibilityChecker()\n          .withFieldVisibility(JsonAutoDetect.Visibility.ANY)\n          .withGetterVisibility(JsonAutoDetect.Visibility.NONE)\n          .withSetterVisibility(JsonAutoDetect.Visibility.NONE)\n          .withCreatorVisibility(JsonAutoDetect.Visibility.NONE)\n      );\n\n      pp =\n        new DefaultPrettyPrinter(\"\") {\n          @Override\n          public DefaultPrettyPrinter withSeparators(Separators separators) {\n            this._separators = separators;\n            this._objectFieldValueSeparatorWithSpaces =\n              separators.getObjectFieldValueSeparator() + \" \";\n            return this;\n          }\n\n          @Override\n          public DefaultPrettyPrinter createInstance() {\n            return this;\n          }\n        };\n\n      DefaultPrettyPrinter.Indenter lfOnlyIndenter = new DefaultIndenter(\"  \", \"\\n\");\n      pp.indentArraysWith(lfOnlyIndenter);\n      pp.indentObjectsWith(lfOnlyIndenter);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String apply(Object[] objects) {\n      try {\n        return objectMapper.writer(pp).writeValueAsString(objects);\n      } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {\n        throw new RuntimeException(\"Failed to process snapshot JSON\", e);\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getOutputFormat() {\n      return SerializerType.JSON.name();\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },
