      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping;\n\nimport au.com.origin.snapshots.junit5.SnapshotExtension;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Disabled;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RequestModes;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.StreetMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.MainAndSubMode;\n\n@ExtendWith(SnapshotExtension.class)\npublic class TransitSnapshotTest extends SnapshotTestBase {\n\n  static GenericLocation ptc = GenericLocation.fromStopId(\n    \"Rose Quarter Transit Center\",\n    \"prt\",\n    \"79-tc\"\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation ps = GenericLocation.fromStopId(\"NE 12th & Couch\", \"prt\", \"6577\");\n\n  static GenericLocation p0 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"SE Stark    St. & SE 17th Ave. (P0)\",\n    null,\n    45.519320,\n    -122.648567\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p1 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"SE Morrison St. & SE 17th Ave. (P1)\",\n    null,\n    45.51726,\n    -122.64847\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p2 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Northrup St. & NW 22nd Ave. (P2)\",\n    null,\n    45.53122,\n    -122.69659\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p3 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NW Northrup St. & NW 24th Ave. (P3)\",\n    null,\n    45.53100,\n    -122.70029\n  );\n\n  static GenericLocation p4 = new GenericLocation(\n    \"NE Thompson St. & NE 18th Ave. (P4)\",\n    null,\n    45.53896,\n    -122.64699\n  );\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void beforeClass() {\n    loadGraphBeforeClass(false);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_walk_only() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes = RequestModes.of().withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK).clearTransitModes().build();\n    request.setFrom(p0);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_walk_only_stop() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .clearTransitModes()\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(ps);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_walk_only_stop_collection() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .clearTransitModes()\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(ptc);\n    request.setTo(p3);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n    // not equal - expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_transit() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(p1);\n    request.setTo(p2);\n\n    expectRequestResponseToMatchSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_transit_stop() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(ps);\n    request.setTo(p3);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  @Disabled\n  public void test_trip_planning_with_transit_stop_collection() {\n    RouteRequest request = createTestRequest(2009, 11, 17, 10, 0, 0);\n\n    request.modes =\n      RequestModes\n        .of()\n        .withAccessMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withEgressMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withDirectMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransferMode(StreetMode.WALK)\n        .withTransitModes(MainAndSubMode.all())\n        .build();\n    request.setFrom(ptc);\n    request.setTo(p3);\n\n    expectArriveByToMatchDepartAtAndSnapshot(request);\n  }\n}\n"

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/raptoradapter/transit/cost/PatternCostCalculatorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.raptoradapter.transit.cost;\n\nimport static graphql.Assert.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.raptoradapter.transit.cost.PatternCostCalculator.UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_RELUCTANCE;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.agency;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.id;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestRoute.route;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.function.DoubleFunction;\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.raptoradapter.transit.mappers.McCostParamsMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RequestFunctions;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.test.support.VariableSource;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.network.RouteBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.organization.Agency;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestTransitData;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestTripPattern;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestTripSchedule;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.api.transit.CostCalculator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.api.transit.RaptorTransferConstraint;\n\npublic class PatternCostCalculatorTest {\n\n  private static final int BOARD_COST_SEC = 5;\n  private static final int TRANSFER_COST_SEC = 2;\n  private static final double WAIT_RELUCTANCE_FACTOR = 0.5;\n  private static final int TRANSIT_TIME = 1000;\n  private static final double UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_PENALTY = 300.0;\n  private static final FeedScopedId UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_ID = id(\"999\");\n  private static final FeedScopedId UNPREFERRED_AGENCY_ID = id(\"contoso-travels\");\n  private static final Agency UNPREFERRED_AGENCY = agency(UNPREFERRED_AGENCY_ID.getId());\n  private static final FeedScopedId DEFAULT_ROUTE_ID = id(\"101\");\n  // Default cost function: a + bx\n  private static final DoubleFunction<Double> unprefCostFn = RequestFunctions.createLinearFunction(\n    RaptorCostConverter.toRaptorCost(UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_PENALTY),\n    RaptorCostConverter.toRaptorCost(UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_RELUCTANCE)\n  );\n\n  protected static final DefaultCostCalculator<TestTripSchedule> defaultCostCalculator = new DefaultCostCalculator<>(\n    BOARD_COST_SEC,\n    TRANSFER_COST_SEC,\n    WAIT_RELUCTANCE_FACTOR,\n    null,\n    null\n  );\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> testCases = Stream\n    .of(\n      // !prefAgency | !prefRoute | unPrefA | unPrefR | expected cost\n      \"       -      |      -     |    -    |    -    |     0\",\n      \"       -      |      -     |    -    |    x    |   300\",\n      \"       -      |      -     |    x    |    -    |   300\",\n      \"       -      |      x     |    -    |    -    |   300\",\n      \"       x      |      -     |    -    |    -    |   300\",\n      \"       -      |      -     |    x    |    x    |   300\",\n      \"       x      |      x     |    -    |    -    |   300\",\n      \"       x      |      -     |    -    |    x    |   600\",\n      \"       -      |      x     |    x    |    -    |   600\",\n      \"       x      |      x     |    x    |    x    |   600\"\n    )\n    .map(Arguments::of);\n\n  @Test\n  @DisplayName(\"cost mapper should create penalty map\")\n  public void testMcCostParameterMapping() {\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    routingRequest.setUnpreferredRoutes(List.of(UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_ID));\n    routingRequest.setUnpreferredAgencies(List.of(UNPREFERRED_AGENCY_ID));\n    routingRequest.setUnpreferredCost(\"300 + 1.0 x\");\n\n    var data = new TestTransitData();\n    final TestTripPattern unpreferredRoutePattern = pattern(true, false);\n    final TestTripPattern unpreferredAgencyPattern = pattern(false, true);\n    final TestTripPattern unpreferredRouteAgencyPattern = pattern(false, true);\n    final TestTripPattern defaultPattern = pattern(false, false);\n\n    data.withRoutes(\n      route(unpreferredRoutePattern),\n      route(unpreferredAgencyPattern),\n      route(unpreferredRouteAgencyPattern),\n      route(defaultPattern)\n    );\n\n    McCostParams costParams = McCostParamsMapper.map(routingRequest, data.getPatterns());\n    var unpreferredPatterns = costParams.unpreferredPatterns();\n\n    assertTrue(unpreferredPatterns.get(unpreferredRoutePattern.patternIndex()));\n    assertTrue(unpreferredPatterns.get(unpreferredAgencyPattern.patternIndex()));\n    assertTrue(unpreferredPatterns.get(unpreferredRouteAgencyPattern.patternIndex()));\n    assertFalse(unpreferredPatterns.get(defaultPattern.patternIndex()));\n\n    // test creation of linear cost function\n    double expected = 300 + 1.0 * TRANSIT_TIME;\n    double actual = costParams.unnpreferredCost().apply(TRANSIT_TIME);\n\n    assertEquals(expected, actual);\n  }\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"should apply penalty for scenario: {0}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"testCases\")\n  public void testPreferencesPenaltyForRoute(String testCaseDescription) {\n    RoutePenaltyTC tc = new RoutePenaltyTC(testCaseDescription);\n\n    var schedule = tc.createSchedule();\n    var costCalculator = tc.createCostCalculator(schedule);\n\n    int defaultArrCost = transitArrivalCost(schedule, defaultCostCalculator);\n    int defaultBoardCost = boardingCost(schedule, defaultCostCalculator);\n    int arrCost = transitArrivalCost(schedule, costCalculator);\n    int boardCost = boardingCost(schedule, costCalculator);\n\n    // if we either have just unpreferred routes or just unpreferred agencies\n    if (tc.unPrefRoute || tc.unPrefAgency) {\n      var expectedArr = unprefCostFn.apply(TRANSIT_TIME);\n      var errorMessageArr = String.format(\"Invalid arrival cost: %s\", tc);\n      assertEquals(expectedArr, arrCost - defaultArrCost, errorMessageArr);\n      /*\n      TODO: separate boarding cost calculation can be tested when boarding and arrival cost\n            constants are differentiated\n      var expectedBoard = unprefCostFn.apply(0);\n      var errorMessageBoard = String.format(\"Invalid boarding cost: %s\", tc);\n      assertEquals(expectedBoard, boardCost - defaultBoardCost, errorMessageBoard);\n      */\n    }\n\n    if (tc.isDefault()) {\n      // expect default penalties\n      assertEquals(defaultArrCost, arrCost);\n      assertEquals(defaultBoardCost, boardCost);\n    }\n    // TODO: preferred route\n    // TODO: preferred agency\n  }\n\n  private int transitArrivalCost(TestTripSchedule schedule, CostCalculator<TestTripSchedule> calc) {\n    int boardCost = boardingCost(schedule, calc);\n    return calc.transitArrivalCost(boardCost, 0, TRANSIT_TIME, schedule, 6);\n  }\n\n  private int boardingCost(TestTripSchedule schedule, CostCalculator<TestTripSchedule> calc) {\n    return calc.boardingCost(true, 0, 5, 100, schedule, RaptorTransferConstraint.REGULAR_TRANSFER);\n  }\n\n  private static class RoutePenaltyTC {\n\n    final boolean prefAgency;\n    final boolean prefRoute;\n    final boolean unPrefAgency;\n    final boolean unPrefRoute;\n    public final int expectedCost;\n\n    RoutePenaltyTC(String input) {\n      String[] cells = input.replace(\" \", \"\").split(\"\\\\|\");\n      this.prefAgency = \"x\".equalsIgnoreCase(cells[0]);\n      this.prefRoute = \"x\".equalsIgnoreCase(cells[1]);\n      this.unPrefAgency = \"x\".equalsIgnoreCase(cells[2]);\n      this.unPrefRoute = \"x\".equalsIgnoreCase(cells[3]);\n      this.expectedCost = Integer.parseInt(cells[4]);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n      final StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n      if (prefAgency) {\n        sb.append(\", prefAgency=X\");\n      }\n      if (prefRoute) {\n        sb.append(\", prefRoute=X\");\n      }\n      if (unPrefAgency) {\n        sb.append(\", unPrefAgency=X\");\n      }\n      if (unPrefRoute) {\n        sb.append(\", unPrefRoute=X\");\n      }\n\n      return \"RoutePenaltyTC {\" + sb.substring(sb.isEmpty() ? 0 : 2) + \"}\";\n    }\n\n    boolean isDefault() {\n      return !(prefAgency || prefRoute || unPrefAgency || unPrefRoute);\n    }\n\n    CostCalculator<TestTripSchedule> createCostCalculator(TestTripSchedule schedule) {\n      McCostParams costParams = McCostParamsMapper.map(\n        createRoutingRequest(),\n        List.of(schedule.pattern())\n      );\n\n      return CostCalculatorFactory.createCostCalculator(costParams, null);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Create a TripSchedule for initialized scenario for testing penalties with RouteCostCalculator\n     * implementation.\n     *\n     * @return Test schedule\n     */\n    TestTripSchedule createSchedule() {\n      return TestTripSchedule\n        .schedule(\"12:00 12:01\")\n        .pattern(pattern(unPrefRoute, unPrefAgency))\n        .build();\n    }\n\n    RouteRequest createRoutingRequest() {\n      RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n\n      request.unpreferredCost =\n        RequestFunctions.createLinearFunction(\n          UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_PENALTY,\n          UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_RELUCTANCE\n        );\n      request.walkBoardCost = BOARD_COST_SEC;\n      request.transferCost = TRANSFER_COST_SEC;\n      request.waitReluctance = WAIT_RELUCTANCE_FACTOR;\n\n      if (prefAgency) {\n        // TODO\n        request.setPreferredAgencies(List.of());\n      }\n      if (prefRoute) {\n        // TODO\n        request.setPreferredRoutes(List.of());\n      }\n      if (unPrefAgency) {\n        request.setUnpreferredAgencies(List.of(UNPREFERRED_AGENCY_ID));\n      }\n      if (unPrefRoute) {\n        request.setUnpreferredRoutes(List.of(UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_ID));\n      }\n      return request;\n    }\n  }\n\n  private static TestTripPattern pattern(boolean unpreferredRoute, boolean unpreferredAgency) {\n    RouteBuilder builder = TransitModelForTest.route(\n      unpreferredRoute ? UNPREFERRED_ROUTE_ID : DEFAULT_ROUTE_ID\n    );\n\n    if (unpreferredAgency) {\n      builder.withAgency(UNPREFERRED_AGENCY);\n    }\n\n    return TestTripPattern.pattern(1, 2).withRoute(builder.build());\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/algorithm/raptoradapter/transit/mappers/McCostParamsMapperTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.raptoradapter.transit.mappers;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.agency;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.id;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest.route;\n\nimport com.google.common.collect.ArrayListMultimap;\nimport com.google.common.collect.Multimap;\nimport java.util.BitSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.FeedScopedId;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestRoute;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestTransitData;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor._data.transit.TestTripPattern;\n\nclass McCostParamsMapperTest {\n\n  static FeedScopedId regularAgency = id(\"regular-agency\");\n  static FeedScopedId unpreferredAgency = id(\"unpreferred-agency\");\n  static FeedScopedId agencyWithNoRoutes = id(\"agency-without-routes\");\n\n  static FeedScopedId route1 = id(\"route1\");\n  static FeedScopedId route2 = id(\"route2\");\n  static FeedScopedId route3 = id(\"route3\");\n  static Multimap<FeedScopedId, FeedScopedId> routesByAgencies = ArrayListMultimap.create();\n  static TestTransitData data;\n\n  static {\n    routesByAgencies.putAll(regularAgency, List.of(route1));\n    routesByAgencies.putAll(unpreferredAgency, List.of(route2, route3));\n    data = new TestTransitData();\n\n    for (var it : routesByAgencies.entries()) {\n      data.withRoute(testTripPattern(it.getKey(), it.getValue()));\n    }\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void shouldExtractRoutesFromAgencies() {\n    var routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    routingRequest.setUnpreferredAgencies(List.of(unpreferredAgency));\n\n    BitSet unpreferredPatterns = McCostParamsMapper\n      .map(routingRequest, data.getPatterns())\n      .unpreferredPatterns();\n\n    for (var pattern : data.getPatterns()) {\n      assertEquals(\n        pattern.route().getAgency().getId().equals(unpreferredAgency),\n        unpreferredPatterns.get(pattern.patternIndex())\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void dealWithEmptyList() {\n    var routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    routingRequest.setUnpreferredAgencies(List.of(agencyWithNoRoutes));\n\n    assertEquals(\n      new BitSet(),\n      McCostParamsMapper.map(routingRequest, data.getPatterns()).unpreferredPatterns()\n    );\n  }\n\n  private static TestRoute testTripPattern(FeedScopedId agencyId, FeedScopedId routeId) {\n    return TestRoute.route(\n      TestTripPattern\n        .pattern(1, 2)\n        .withRoute(route(routeId).withAgency(agency(agencyId.getId())).build())\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/core/RoutingRequestTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.core;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotSame;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode.CAR;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\n\npublic class RoutingRequestTest {\n\n  @Test\n  public void testRequest() {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n\n    request.addMode(CAR);\n    assertTrue(request.streetSubRequestModes.getCar());\n    request.removeMode(CAR);\n    assertFalse(request.streetSubRequestModes.getCar());\n\n    request.setStreetSubRequestModes(new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, TraverseMode.WALK));\n    assertFalse(request.streetSubRequestModes.getCar());\n    assertTrue(request.streetSubRequestModes.getBicycle());\n    assertTrue(request.streetSubRequestModes.getWalk());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testIntermediatePlaces() {\n    RouteRequest req = new RouteRequest();\n    assertFalse(req.hasIntermediatePlaces());\n\n    req.clearIntermediatePlaces();\n    assertFalse(req.hasIntermediatePlaces());\n\n    req.addIntermediatePlace(randomLocation());\n    assertTrue(req.hasIntermediatePlaces());\n\n    req.clearIntermediatePlaces();\n    assertFalse(req.hasIntermediatePlaces());\n\n    req.addIntermediatePlace(randomLocation());\n    req.addIntermediatePlace(randomLocation());\n    assertTrue(req.hasIntermediatePlaces());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void shouldCloneObjectFields() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n\n    var clone = req.clone();\n\n    assertNotSame(clone, req);\n    assertNotSame(clone.itineraryFilters, req.itineraryFilters);\n    assertNotSame(clone.raptorDebugging, req.raptorDebugging);\n    assertNotSame(clone.raptorOptions, req.raptorOptions);\n\n    assertEquals(50, req.numItineraries());\n    assertEquals(50, clone.numItineraries());\n  }\n\n  private GenericLocation randomLocation() {\n    return new GenericLocation(Math.random(), Math.random());\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/core/StateEditorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.core;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\n\npublic class StateEditorTest {\n\n  @Test\n  public final void testIncrementTimeInSeconds() {\n    Graph graph = new Graph();\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, (Vertex) null, null);\n    StateEditor stateEditor = new StateEditor(routingContext, null);\n\n    stateEditor.setTimeSeconds(0);\n    stateEditor.incrementTimeInSeconds(999999999);\n\n    assertEquals(999999999, stateEditor.child.getTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public final void testWeightIncrement() {\n    Graph graph = new Graph();\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, (Vertex) null, null);\n    StateEditor stateEditor = new StateEditor(routingContext, null);\n\n    stateEditor.setTimeSeconds(0);\n    stateEditor.incrementWeight(10);\n\n    assertNotNull(stateEditor.makeState());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public final void testNanWeightIncrement() {\n    Graph graph = new Graph();\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, (Vertex) null, null);\n    StateEditor stateEditor = new StateEditor(routingContext, null);\n\n    stateEditor.setTimeSeconds(0);\n    stateEditor.incrementWeight(Double.NaN);\n\n    assertNull(stateEditor.makeState());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public final void testInfinityWeightIncrement() {\n    Graph graph = new Graph();\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, (Vertex) null, null);\n    StateEditor stateEditor = new StateEditor(routingContext, null);\n\n    stateEditor.setTimeSeconds(0);\n    stateEditor.incrementWeight(Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);\n\n    assertNull(stateEditor.makeState(), \"Infinity weight increment\");\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/core/TemporaryVerticesContainerTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.core;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.GeometryFactory;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.geometry.SphericalDistanceLibrary;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.TemporaryEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Edge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TemporaryVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic class TemporaryVerticesContainerTest {\n\n  private final GeometryFactory gf = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory();\n  // Given:\n  // - a graph with 3 intersections/vertexes\n  private Graph g = new Graph(new Deduplicator());\n  private final StreetVertex a = new IntersectionVertex(g, \"A\", 1.0, 1.0);\n  private final StreetVertex b = new IntersectionVertex(g, \"B\", 0.0, 1.0);\n  private final StreetVertex c = new IntersectionVertex(g, \"C\", 1.0, 0.0);\n  private final List<Vertex> permanentVertexes = Arrays.asList(a, b, c);\n  // - And travel *origin* is 0,4 degrees on the road from B to A\n  private final GenericLocation from = new GenericLocation(1.0, 0.4);\n  // - and *destination* is slightly off 0.7 degrees on road from C to A\n  private final GenericLocation to = new GenericLocation(0.701, 1.001);\n  private TemporaryVerticesContainer subject;\n\n  // - and some roads\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void setup() {\n    createStreetEdge(a, b, \"a -> b\");\n    createStreetEdge(b, a, \"b -> a\");\n    createStreetEdge(a, c, \"a -> c\");\n    g.index(new StopModel());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void temporaryChangesRemovedOnClose() {\n    // Given - A request\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n\n    // When - the container is created\n    subject = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(g, request);\n\n    // Then:\n    originAndDestinationInsertedCorrect();\n\n    // And When:\n    subject.close();\n\n    // Then - permanent vertexes\n    for (Vertex v : permanentVertexes) {\n      // - does not reference the any temporary nodes anymore\n      for (Edge e : v.getIncoming()) {\n        assertVertexEdgeIsNotReferencingTemporaryElements(v, e, e.getFromVertex());\n      }\n      for (Edge e : v.getOutgoing()) {\n        assertVertexEdgeIsNotReferencingTemporaryElements(v, e, e.getToVertex());\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  private static <T extends Collection<String>> T findAllReachableVertexes(\n    Vertex vertex,\n    boolean forward,\n    T list\n  ) {\n    if (list.contains(vertex.getDefaultName())) {\n      return list;\n    }\n\n    list.add(vertex.getDefaultName());\n    if (forward) {\n      vertex.getOutgoing().forEach(it -> findAllReachableVertexes(it.getToVertex(), forward, list));\n    } else {\n      vertex\n        .getIncoming()\n        .forEach(it -> findAllReachableVertexes(it.getFromVertex(), forward, list));\n    }\n    return list;\n  }\n\n  private void originAndDestinationInsertedCorrect() {\n    // Then - the origin and destination is\n    assertEquals(\"Origin\", subject.getFromVertices().iterator().next().getDefaultName());\n    assertEquals(\"Destination\", subject.getToVertices().iterator().next().getDefaultName());\n\n    // And - from the origin\n    Collection<String> vertexesReachableFromOrigin = findAllReachableVertexes(\n      subject.getFromVertices().iterator().next(),\n      true,\n      new ArrayList<>()\n    );\n    String msg = \"All reachable vertexes from origin: \" + vertexesReachableFromOrigin;\n\n    // it is possible to reach the A, B, C and the Destination Vertex\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromOrigin.contains(\"A\"), msg);\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromOrigin.contains(\"B\"), msg);\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromOrigin.contains(\"C\"), msg);\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromOrigin.contains(\"Destination\"), msg);\n\n    // And - from the destination we can backtrack\n    Collection<String> vertexesReachableFromDestination = findAllReachableVertexes(\n      subject.getToVertices().iterator().next(),\n      false,\n      new ArrayList<>()\n    );\n    msg = \"All reachable vertexes back from destination: \" + vertexesReachableFromDestination;\n\n    // and reach the A, B and the Origin Vertex\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromDestination.contains(\"A\"), msg);\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromDestination.contains(\"B\"), msg);\n    assertTrue(vertexesReachableFromDestination.contains(\"Origin\"), msg);\n\n    // But - not the C Vertex\n    assertFalse(vertexesReachableFromDestination.contains(\"C\"), msg);\n  }\n\n  private void createStreetEdge(StreetVertex v0, StreetVertex v1, String name) {\n    LineString geom = gf.createLineString(\n      new Coordinate[] { v0.getCoordinate(), v1.getCoordinate() }\n    );\n    double dist = SphericalDistanceLibrary.distance(v0.getCoordinate(), v1.getCoordinate());\n    new StreetEdge(v0, v1, geom, name, dist, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n  }\n\n  private void assertVertexEdgeIsNotReferencingTemporaryElements(Vertex src, Edge e, Vertex v) {\n    String sourceName = src.getDefaultName();\n    assertFalse(e instanceof TemporaryEdge, sourceName + \" -> \" + e.getDefaultName());\n    assertFalse(v instanceof TemporaryVertex, sourceName + \" -> \" + v.getDefaultName());\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/core/TraverseResultTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.core;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\n\npublic class TraverseResultTest {\n\n  @Test\n  public void testAddToExistingResultChain() {\n    State resultChain = null;\n\n    /* note: times are rounded to seconds toward zero */\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n      State r = new State(\n        null,\n        Instant.ofEpochSecond(i * 1000),\n        null,\n        StateData.getInitialStateData(new RouteRequest())\n      );\n      resultChain = r.addToExistingResultChain(resultChain);\n    }\n\n    assertEquals(3000, resultChain.getTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n\n    resultChain = resultChain.getNextResult();\n    assertEquals(2000, resultChain.getTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n\n    resultChain = resultChain.getNextResult();\n    assertEquals(1000, resultChain.getTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n\n    resultChain = resultChain.getNextResult();\n    assertEquals(0000, resultChain.getTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n\n    resultChain = resultChain.getNextResult();\n    assertNull(resultChain);\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/core/intersection_model/SimpleTraversalCostModelTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.core.intersection_model;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.osm.WayPropertySetSource.DrivingDirection;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\n/**\n * Tests for SimpleIntersectionTraversalCostModel.\n * <p>\n * TODO(flamholz): add tests for traversal with traffic lights and without a car.\n *\n * @author avi\n */\npublic class SimpleTraversalCostModelTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n\n  private RouteRequest options;\n\n  public SimpleIntersectionTraversalCostModel costModel;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void before() {\n    graph = new Graph();\n    costModel = new SimpleIntersectionTraversalCostModel(DrivingDirection.RIGHT_HAND_TRAFFIC);\n\n    // Initialize the routing request.\n    options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.carSpeed = 1.0;\n    options.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    options.carDecelerationSpeed = (2.0);\n    options.carAccelerationSpeed = (2.0);\n    options.setStreetSubRequestModes(TraverseModeSet.allModes());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testCalculateTurnAngle() {\n    // Graph for a fictional grid city with turn restrictions\n    IntersectionVertex v1 = vertex(\"maple_1st\", new Coordinate(2.0, 2.0), false);\n    IntersectionVertex v2 = vertex(\"maple_2nd\", new Coordinate(2.0, 1.0), false);\n    v2.trafficLight = true;\n\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 1.0, false);\n\n    // Edge has same first and last angle.\n    assertEquals(90, e1.getInAngle());\n    assertEquals(90, e1.getOutAngle());\n\n    // 2 new ones\n    IntersectionVertex v3 = vertex(\"test2\", new Coordinate(1.0, 1.0), false);\n\n    // Second edge\n    StreetEdge e2 = edge(v2, v3, 1.0, false);\n\n    assertEquals(0, e2.getInAngle());\n    assertEquals(0, e2.getOutAngle());\n\n    // Difference should be about 90.\n    int diff = (e1.getOutAngle() - e2.getInAngle());\n    assertEquals(90, diff);\n\n    // calculate the angle for driving on the right hand side\n\n    int rightHandDriveAngle = costModel.calculateTurnAngle(e1, e2);\n    assertEquals(270, rightHandDriveAngle);\n    assertTrue(costModel.isTurnAcrossTraffic(rightHandDriveAngle));\n    assertFalse(costModel.isSafeTurn(rightHandDriveAngle));\n\n    // and on the left hand side\n\n    var leftHandDriveCostModel = new SimpleIntersectionTraversalCostModel(\n      DrivingDirection.LEFT_HAND_TRAFFIC\n    );\n    int leftHandDriveAngle = leftHandDriveCostModel.calculateTurnAngle(e1, e2);\n    assertEquals(270, leftHandDriveAngle);\n\n    assertTrue(leftHandDriveCostModel.isSafeTurn(leftHandDriveAngle));\n    assertFalse(leftHandDriveCostModel.isTurnAcrossTraffic(leftHandDriveAngle));\n\n    // on a bike the turn cost for crossing traffic (left turn in left hand driving countries)\n    // should be higher than going the opposite direction\n\n    assertEquals(\n      1.6875,\n      costModel.computeTraversalCost(v2, e1, e2, TraverseMode.BICYCLE, 40, 40),\n      0.1\n    );\n    assertEquals(\n      0.5625,\n      costModel.computeTraversalCost(v2, e2, e1, TraverseMode.BICYCLE, 40, 40),\n      0.1\n    );\n\n    // in left hand driving countries it should be the opposite\n\n    assertEquals(\n      0.5625,\n      leftHandDriveCostModel.computeTraversalCost(v2, e1, e2, TraverseMode.BICYCLE, 40, 40),\n      0.1\n    );\n    assertEquals(\n      1.6875,\n      leftHandDriveCostModel.computeTraversalCost(v2, e2, e1, TraverseMode.BICYCLE, 40, 40),\n      0.1\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTurnDirectionChecking() {\n    // 3 points on a roughly on line\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(-73.990989, 40.750167);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(-73.988049, 40.749094);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(-73.984981, 40.747761);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n\n    // Two edges.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    int turnAngle = costModel.calculateTurnAngle(fromEdge, toEdge);\n    assertFalse(costModel.isSafeTurn(turnAngle));\n    assertFalse(costModel.isTurnAcrossTraffic(turnAngle));\n    // AKA is a straight ahead.\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testFreeFlowing() {\n    // 3 points on a roughly on line\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(-73.990989, 40.750167);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(-73.988049, 40.749094);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(-73.984981, 40.747761);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n    v.freeFlowing = true;\n\n    // Two edges.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    float fromSpeed = 1.0f;\n    float toSpeed = 1.0f;\n    TraverseMode mode = TraverseMode.CAR;\n\n    double traversalCost = costModel.computeTraversalCost(\n      v,\n      fromEdge,\n      toEdge,\n      mode,\n      fromSpeed,\n      toSpeed\n    );\n\n    // Vertex is free-flowing so cost should be 0.0.\n    assertEquals(0.0, traversalCost, 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testInferredFreeFlowing() {\n    // 3 points on a roughly on line\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(-73.990989, 40.750167);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(-73.988049, 40.749094);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(-73.984981, 40.747761);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n\n    // Two edges - will infer that the vertex is free-flowing since there is no light.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    float fromSpeed = 1.0f;\n    float toSpeed = 1.0f;\n    TraverseMode mode = TraverseMode.CAR;\n\n    double traversalCost = costModel.computeTraversalCost(\n      v,\n      fromEdge,\n      toEdge,\n      mode,\n      fromSpeed,\n      toSpeed\n    );\n\n    // Vertex is free-flowing so cost should be 0.0.\n    assertEquals(0.0, traversalCost, 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testStraightNoLightInCar() {\n    // 3 points on a roughly on line\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(-73.990989, 40.750167);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(-73.988049, 40.749094);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(-73.984981, 40.747761);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n\n    // Two edges.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    // 3rd edge prevents inferral of free-flowingness\n    StreetEdge extraEdge = edge(v, u, 1.0, false);\n\n    float fromSpeed = 1.0f;\n    float toSpeed = 1.0f;\n    TraverseMode mode = TraverseMode.CAR;\n\n    double traversalCost = costModel.computeTraversalCost(\n      v,\n      fromEdge,\n      toEdge,\n      mode,\n      fromSpeed,\n      toSpeed\n    );\n\n    // Cost with default values = 5.0\n    assertEquals(5, traversalCost, 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testRightNoLightInCar() {\n    // 3 points that form a right turn on the map\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(40.750167, -73.990989);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(40.749094, -73.988049);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(40.748509, -73.988693);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n\n    // Two edges.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    // 3rd edge prevents inferral of free-flowingness\n    StreetEdge extraEdge = edge(v, u, 1.0, false);\n\n    int turnAngle = costModel.calculateTurnAngle(fromEdge, toEdge);\n    assertTrue(costModel.isSafeTurn(turnAngle));\n    assertFalse(costModel.isTurnAcrossTraffic(turnAngle));\n\n    float fromSpeed = 1.0f;\n    float toSpeed = 1.0f;\n    TraverseMode mode = TraverseMode.CAR;\n\n    double traversalCost = costModel.computeTraversalCost(\n      v,\n      fromEdge,\n      toEdge,\n      mode,\n      fromSpeed,\n      toSpeed\n    );\n\n    // Cost with default values = 8.0\n    assertEquals(8.0, traversalCost, 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testLeftNoLightInCar() {\n    // 3 points that form a right turn on the map\n    Coordinate a = new Coordinate(40.750167, -73.990989);\n    Coordinate b = new Coordinate(40.749094, -73.988049);\n    Coordinate c = new Coordinate(40.749760, -73.987749);\n\n    // A vertex for each. No light.\n    IntersectionVertex u = vertex(\"from_v\", a, false);\n    IntersectionVertex v = vertex(\"intersection\", b, false);\n    IntersectionVertex w = vertex(\"to_v\", c, false);\n\n    // Two edges.\n    StreetEdge fromEdge = edge(u, v, 1.0, false);\n    StreetEdge toEdge = edge(v, w, 1.0, false);\n\n    // 3rd edge prevents inferral of free-flowingness\n    StreetEdge extraEdge = edge(v, u, 1.0, false);\n\n    int turnAngle = costModel.calculateTurnAngle(fromEdge, toEdge);\n    assertFalse(costModel.isSafeTurn(turnAngle));\n    assertTrue(costModel.isTurnAcrossTraffic(turnAngle));\n\n    float fromSpeed = 1.0f;\n    float toSpeed = 1.0f;\n    TraverseMode mode = TraverseMode.CAR;\n\n    double traversalCost = costModel.computeTraversalCost(\n      v,\n      fromEdge,\n      toEdge,\n      mode,\n      fromSpeed,\n      toSpeed\n    );\n\n    // Cost with default values = 8.0\n    assertEquals(8.0, traversalCost, 0.0);\n  }\n\n  /****\n   * Private Methods\n   ****/\n\n  private IntersectionVertex vertex(String label, Coordinate coord, boolean hasLight) {\n    IntersectionVertex v = new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, coord.y, coord.x);\n    v.trafficLight = hasLight;\n    return v;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create an edge. If twoWay, create two edges (back and forth).\n   *\n   * @param back true if this is a reverse edge\n   */\n  private StreetEdge edge(StreetVertex vA, StreetVertex vB, double length, boolean back) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm = StreetTraversalPermission.ALL;\n    return new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, back);\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/ElevatorHopEdgeTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityFeature;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.SimpleVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.test.support.VariableSource;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.WheelchairAccessibility;\n\nclass ElevatorHopEdgeTest {\n\n  Graph graph = new Graph();\n  Vertex from = new SimpleVertex(graph, \"from\", 0, 0);\n  Vertex to = new SimpleVertex(graph, \"to\", 0, 0);\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> noTraverse = Stream\n    .of(WheelchairAccessibility.NO_INFORMATION, WheelchairAccessibility.NOT_POSSIBLE)\n    .map(Arguments::of);\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"{0} should be allowed to traverse when requesting onlyAccessible\")\n  @VariableSource(\"noTraverse\")\n  public void shouldNotTraverse(WheelchairAccessibility wheelchair) {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    WheelchairAccessibilityFeature feature = WheelchairAccessibilityFeature.ofOnlyAccessible();\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(true, feature, feature, feature, 25, 8, 10, 25);\n    State result = traverse(wheelchair, req);\n    assertNull(result);\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> all = Stream.of(\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(WheelchairAccessibility.POSSIBLE, 20),\n    // low extra cost\n    Arguments.of(WheelchairAccessibility.NO_INFORMATION, 40),\n    // high extra cost\n    Arguments.of(WheelchairAccessibility.NOT_POSSIBLE, 3620)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"{0} should allowed to traverse with a cost of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"all\")\n  public void allowByDefault(WheelchairAccessibility wheelchair, double expectedCost) {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    var result = traverse(wheelchair, req);\n    assertNotNull(result);\n    assertTrue(result.weight > 1);\n\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility = WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.DEFAULT.withEnabled(true);\n    var wheelchairResult = traverse(wheelchair, req);\n    assertNotNull(wheelchairResult);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, wheelchairResult.weight);\n  }\n\n  private State traverse(WheelchairAccessibility wheelchair, RouteRequest req) {\n    var edge = new ElevatorHopEdge(from, to, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, wheelchair);\n    var ctx = new RoutingContext(req, graph, from, to);\n    var state = new State(ctx);\n\n    return edge.traverse(state);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/PathwayEdgeTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityFeature.ofOnlyAccessible;\n\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.SimpleVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.test.support.VariableSource;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.NonLocalizedString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.PathwayMode;\n\nclass PathwayEdgeTest {\n\n  Graph graph = new Graph();\n  Vertex from = new SimpleVertex(graph, \"A\", 10, 10);\n  Vertex to = new SimpleVertex(graph, \"B\", 10.001, 10.001);\n\n  @Test\n  void zeroLength() {\n    // if elevators have a traversal time and distance of 0 we cannot interpolate the distance\n    // from the vertices as they most likely have identical coordinates\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      0,\n      0,\n      0,\n      0,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.ELEVATOR\n    );\n\n    assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void zeroLengthWithSteps() {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      0,\n      0,\n      2,\n      0,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.STAIRS\n    );\n\n    assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void traversalTime() {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      60,\n      0,\n      0,\n      0,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.ESCALATOR\n    );\n\n    var state = assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge);\n    assertEquals(60, state.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(120, state.getWeight());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void traversalTimeOverridesLength() {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      60,\n      1000,\n      0,\n      0,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.MOVING_SIDEWALK\n    );\n\n    assertEquals(1000, edge.getDistanceMeters());\n\n    var state = assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge);\n    assertEquals(60, state.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(120, state.getWeight());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void distance() {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      0,\n      100,\n      0,\n      0,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.WALKWAY\n    );\n\n    var state = assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge);\n    assertEquals(133, state.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(266, state.getWeight());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void wheelchair() {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      0,\n      100,\n      0,\n      0,\n      false,\n      PathwayMode.WALKWAY\n    );\n\n    var state = assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge, true);\n    assertEquals(133, state.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(6650.0, state.getWeight());\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> slopeCases = Stream.of(\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.07, 120),\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.08, 120),\n    // 1 % above max\n    Arguments.of(0.09, 239),\n    // 1.1 % above the max slope, tiny extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.091, 251),\n    // 1.15 % above the max slope, will incur larger cost\n    Arguments.of(0.0915, 257),\n    // 3 % above max slope, will incur very large cost\n    Arguments.of(0.11, 480)\n  );\n\n  /**\n   * This makes sure that when you exceed the max slope in a wheelchair there isn't a hard cut-off\n   * but rather the cost increases proportional to how much you go over the maximum.\n   * <p>\n   * In other words: 0.1 % over the limit only has a small cost but 2% over increases it\n   * dramatically to the point where it's only used as a last resort.\n   */\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"slope of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"slopeCases\")\n  public void shouldScaleCostWithMaxSlope(double slope, long expectedCost) {\n    var edge = new PathwayEdge(\n      from,\n      to,\n      null,\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"pathway\"),\n      60,\n      100,\n      0,\n      slope,\n      true,\n      PathwayMode.WALKWAY\n    );\n\n    var state = assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge, true);\n    assertEquals(60, state.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (int) state.getWeight());\n  }\n\n  private State assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(PathwayEdge edge) {\n    return assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(edge, false);\n  }\n\n  private State assertThatEdgeIsTraversable(PathwayEdge edge, boolean wheelchair) {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(\n        wheelchair,\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        25,\n        0.08,\n        1,\n        25\n      );\n    var state = new State(new RoutingContext(req, graph, from, to));\n\n    var afterTraversal = edge.traverse(state);\n    assertNotNull(afterTraversal);\n\n    assertTrue(afterTraversal.getWeight() > 0);\n    return afterTraversal;\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/StreetEdgeCostTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\n\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.GraphRoutingTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.test.support.VariableSource;\n\nclass StreetEdgeCostTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  StreetVertex V1;\n  StreetVertex V2;\n\n  Graph graph;\n\n  public StreetEdgeCostTest() {\n    var otpModel = modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          V1 = intersection(\"V1\", 0.0, 0.0);\n          V2 = intersection(\"V2\", 2.0, 0.0);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = otpModel.graph();\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> walkReluctanceCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(0.5, 37),\n    Arguments.of(1, 75),\n    Arguments.of(2, 150),\n    Arguments.of(3, 225)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"walkRelucance of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"walkReluctanceCases\")\n  public void walkReluctance(double walkReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 100;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"edge\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setWalkReluctance(walkReluctance);\n    State result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertNotNull(result);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(76, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> bikeReluctanceCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(0.5, 10),\n    Arguments.of(1, 20),\n    Arguments.of(2, 40),\n    Arguments.of(3, 60)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"bikeReluctance of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"bikeReluctanceCases\")\n  public void bikeReluctance(double bikeReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 100;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"edge\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    req.setBikeReluctance(bikeReluctance);\n\n    State result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertNotNull(result);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(20, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> carReluctanceCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(0.5, 4),\n    Arguments.of(1, 8),\n    Arguments.of(2, 17),\n    Arguments.of(3, 26)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"carReluctance of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"carReluctanceCases\")\n  public void carReluctance(double carReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 100;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"edge\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    req.setCarReluctance(carReluctance);\n\n    State result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertNotNull(result);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(9, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> stairsCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(1, 22),\n    Arguments.of(1.5, 33),\n    Arguments.of(3, 67)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"stairs reluctance of of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"stairsCases\")\n  public void stairsReluctance(double stairsReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 10;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"stairs\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n    edge.setStairs(true);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.stairsReluctance = stairsReluctance;\n\n    var result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(23, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n\n    edge.setStairs(false);\n    var notStairsResult = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(15, (long) notStairsResult.weight);\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> walkSafetyCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(0, 15),\n    Arguments.of(0.5, 22),\n    Arguments.of(1, 30)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(name = \"walk safety factor of of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\")\n  @VariableSource(\"walkSafetyCases\")\n  public void walkSafetyFactor(double walkSafetyFactor, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 10;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(\n      V1,\n      V2,\n      null,\n      \"test edge\",\n      length,\n      StreetTraversalPermission.ALL,\n      false\n    );\n    edge.setWalkSafetyFactor(2);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.walkSafetyFactor = walkSafetyFactor;\n\n    var result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(8, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n\n    edge.setWalkSafetyFactor(1);\n    var defaultSafetyResult = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(15, (long) defaultSafetyResult.weight);\n  }\n\n  private State traverse(StreetEdge edge, RouteRequest req) {\n    var ctx = new RoutingContext(req, graph, V1, V2);\n    var state = new State(ctx);\n\n    assertEquals(0, state.weight);\n    return edge.traverse(state);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/StreetEdgeTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertArrayEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.impl.PackedCoordinateSequence;\nimport org.opentripplanner.common.TurnRestriction;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.StateData;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic class StreetEdgeTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private IntersectionVertex v0, v1, v2;\n  private RouteRequest proto;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void before() {\n    graph = new Graph();\n\n    v0 = vertex(\"maple_0th\", 0.0, 0.0); // label, X, Y\n    v1 = vertex(\"maple_1st\", 2.0, 2.0);\n    v2 = vertex(\"maple_2nd\", 1.0, 2.0);\n\n    proto = new RouteRequest();\n    proto.carSpeed = 15.0f;\n    proto.walkSpeed = 1.0;\n    proto.bikeSpeed = 5.0f;\n    proto.bikeWalkingSpeed = 0.8;\n    proto.bikeReluctance = 1.0;\n    proto.carReluctance = 1.0;\n    proto.walkReluctance = 1.0;\n    proto.stairsReluctance = 1.0;\n    proto.turnReluctance = 1.0;\n    proto.setStreetSubRequestModes(TraverseModeSet.allModes());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testInAndOutAngles() {\n    // An edge heading straight West\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n\n    // Edge has same first and last angle.\n    assertEquals(90, e1.getInAngle());\n    assertEquals(90, e1.getOutAngle());\n\n    // 2 new ones\n    StreetVertex u = vertex(\"test1\", 2.0, 1.0);\n    StreetVertex v = vertex(\"test2\", 2.0, 2.0);\n\n    // Second edge, heading straight North\n    StreetEdge e2 = edge(u, v, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n\n    // 180 degrees could be expressed as 180 or -180. Our implementation happens to use -180.\n    assertEquals(180, Math.abs(e2.getInAngle()));\n    assertEquals(180, Math.abs(e2.getOutAngle()));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTraverseAsPedestrian() {\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 100.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    e1.setCarSpeed(10.0f);\n\n    RouteRequest options = proto.clone();\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.WALK);\n\n    State s0 = new State(new RoutingContext(options, graph, v1, v2));\n    State s1 = e1.traverse(s0);\n\n    // Should use the speed on the edge.\n    double expectedWeight = e1.getDistanceMeters() / options.walkSpeed;\n    long expectedDuration = (long) Math.ceil(expectedWeight);\n    assertEquals(expectedDuration, s1.getElapsedTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(expectedWeight, s1.getWeight(), 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTraverseAsCar() {\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 100.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    e1.setCarSpeed(10.0f);\n\n    RouteRequest options = proto.clone();\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n\n    State s0 = new State(new RoutingContext(options, graph, v1, v2));\n    State s1 = e1.traverse(s0);\n\n    // Should use the speed on the edge.\n    double expectedWeight = e1.getDistanceMeters() / e1.getCarSpeed();\n    long expectedDuration = (long) Math.ceil(expectedWeight);\n    assertEquals(expectedDuration, s1.getElapsedTimeSeconds(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(expectedWeight, s1.getWeight(), 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testModeSetCanTraverse() {\n    StreetEdge e = edge(v1, v2, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n\n    TraverseModeSet modes = TraverseModeSet.allModes();\n    assertTrue(e.canTraverse(modes));\n\n    modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, TraverseMode.WALK);\n    assertTrue(e.canTraverse(modes));\n\n    e = edge(v1, v2, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.CAR);\n    assertFalse(e.canTraverse(modes));\n\n    modes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR, TraverseMode.WALK);\n    assertTrue(e.canTraverse(modes));\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Test the traversal of two edges with different traverse modes, with a focus on cycling. This\n   * test will fail unless the following three conditions are met: 1. Turn costs are computed based\n   * on the back edge's traverse mode during reverse traversal. 2. Turn costs are computed such that\n   * bike walking is taken into account correctly. 3. User-specified bike speeds are applied\n   * correctly during turn cost computation.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void testTraverseModeSwitchBike() {\n    StreetEdge e0 = edge(v0, v1, 50.0, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 18.4, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n\n    v1.trafficLight = true;\n\n    RouteRequest forward = proto.clone();\n    forward.bikeSpeed = 3.0f;\n    forward.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n\n    State s0 = new State(new RoutingContext(forward, graph, v0, v2));\n    State s1 = e0.traverse(s0);\n    State s2 = e1.traverse(s1);\n\n    RouteRequest reverse = proto.clone();\n    reverse.setArriveBy(true);\n    reverse.bikeSpeed = 3.0f;\n    reverse.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n\n    State s3 = new State(new RoutingContext(reverse, graph, v0, v2));\n    State s4 = e1.traverse(s3);\n    State s5 = e0.traverse(s4);\n\n    assertEquals(104, s2.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(104, s5.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Test the traversal of two edges with different traverse modes, with a focus on walking. This\n   * test will fail unless the following three conditions are met: 1. Turn costs are computed based\n   * on the back edge's traverse mode during reverse traversal. 2. Turn costs are computed such that\n   * bike walking is taken into account correctly. 3. Enabling bike mode on a routing request bases\n   * the bike walking speed on the walking speed.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void testTraverseModeSwitchWalk() {\n    StreetEdge e0 = edge(v0, v1, 50.0, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 18.4, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n\n    v1.trafficLight = true;\n\n    RouteRequest forward = proto.clone();\n    forward.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n\n    State s0 = new State(new RoutingContext(forward, graph, v0, v2));\n    State s1 = e0.traverse(s0);\n    State s2 = e1.traverse(s1);\n\n    RouteRequest reverse = proto.clone();\n    reverse.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    reverse.setArriveBy(true);\n\n    State s3 = new State(new RoutingContext(reverse, graph, v0, v2));\n    State s4 = e1.traverse(s3);\n    State s5 = e0.traverse(s4);\n\n    assertEquals(42, s2.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(42, s5.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Test the bike switching penalty feature, both its cost penalty and its separate time penalty.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void testBikeSwitch() {\n    StreetEdge e0 = edge(v0, v1, 0.0, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN);\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 0.0, StreetTraversalPermission.BICYCLE);\n    StreetEdge e2 = edge(v2, v0, 0.0, StreetTraversalPermission.PEDESTRIAN_AND_BICYCLE);\n\n    RouteRequest noPenalty = proto.clone();\n    noPenalty.bikeSwitchTime = 0;\n    noPenalty.bikeSwitchCost = 0;\n    noPenalty.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n\n    State s0 = new State(new RoutingContext(noPenalty, graph, v0, v0));\n    State s1 = e0.traverse(s0);\n    State s2 = e1.traverse(s1);\n    State s3 = e2.traverse(s2);\n\n    RouteRequest withPenalty = proto.clone();\n    withPenalty.bikeSwitchTime = 42;\n    withPenalty.bikeSwitchCost = 23;\n    withPenalty.setMode(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n\n    State s4 = new State(new RoutingContext(withPenalty, graph, v0, v0));\n    State s5 = e0.traverse(s4);\n    State s6 = e1.traverse(s5);\n    State s7 = e2.traverse(s6);\n\n    assertNull(s0.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.WALK, s1.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, s2.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, s3.getBackMode());\n\n    assertNull(s4.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.WALK, s5.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, s6.getBackMode());\n    assertEquals(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, s7.getBackMode());\n\n    assertEquals(0, s0.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(0, s1.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(0, s2.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(0, s3.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n\n    assertEquals(0.0, s0.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(0.0, s1.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(0.0, s2.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(0.0, s3.getWeight(), 0.0);\n\n    assertEquals(0.0, s4.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(0.0, s5.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(23.0, s6.getWeight(), 0.0);\n    assertEquals(23.0, s7.getWeight(), 0.0);\n\n    assertEquals(0, s4.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(0, s5.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(42, s6.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(42, s7.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTurnRestriction() {\n    StreetEdge e0 = edge(v0, v1, 50.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    StreetEdge e1 = edge(v1, v2, 18.4, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = proto.clone();\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, v0, v2);\n    State state = new State(\n      v2,\n      Instant.EPOCH,\n      routingContext,\n      StateData.getInitialStateData(routingRequest)\n    );\n\n    state.getOptions().setArriveBy(true);\n    e1.addTurnRestriction(new TurnRestriction(e1, e0, null, TraverseModeSet.allModes(), null));\n\n    assertNotNull(e0.traverse(e1.traverse(state)));\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testElevationProfile() {\n    var elevationProfile = new PackedCoordinateSequence.Double(\n      new double[] { 0, 10, 50, 12 },\n      2,\n      0\n    );\n    StreetEdge e0 = edge(v0, v1, 50.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, elevationProfile);\n\n    assertArrayEquals(\n      elevationProfile.toCoordinateArray(),\n      e0.getElevationProfile().toCoordinateArray()\n    );\n  }\n\n  /****\n   * Private Methods\n   ****/\n\n  private IntersectionVertex vertex(String label, double x, double y) {\n    return new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, x, y);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create an edge. If twoWay, create two edges (back and forth).\n   */\n  private StreetEdge edge(\n    StreetVertex vA,\n    StreetVertex vB,\n    double length,\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm\n  ) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    return new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, false);\n  }\n\n  private StreetEdge edge(\n    StreetVertex vA,\n    StreetVertex vB,\n    double length,\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm,\n    PackedCoordinateSequence elevationProfile\n  ) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, false);\n    StreetElevationExtension.addToEdge(edge, elevationProfile, false);\n    return edge;\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/StreetEdgeWheelchairCostTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityFeature.ofOnlyAccessible;\n\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.Arguments;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.impl.PackedCoordinateSequence;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.GraphRoutingTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.test.support.VariableSource;\n\nclass StreetEdgeWheelchairCostTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  StreetVertex V1;\n  StreetVertex V2;\n\n  Graph graph;\n\n  public StreetEdgeWheelchairCostTest() {\n    TestOtpModel model = modelOf(\n      new Builder() {\n        @Override\n        public void build() {\n          V1 = intersection(\"V1\", 0.0, 0.0);\n          V2 = intersection(\"V2\", 2.0, 0.0);\n        }\n      }\n    );\n    graph = model.graph();\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> slopeCases = Stream.of(\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.07, 1, 5081),\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.08, 1, 5945),\n    // no extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.09, 1, 6908),\n    // 0.1 % above the max slope, tiny extra cost\n    Arguments.of(0.091, 1, 7708),\n    // 3 % above max slope, will incur very large cost\n    Arguments.of(0.091, 3, 9110),\n    // 0.1 % above the max slope, but high reluctance will large cost\n    Arguments.of(0.0915, 1, 8116),\n    // 2 % above max slope, but lowered reluctance\n    Arguments.of(0.11, 0.5, 17649),\n    // 2 % above max slope, will incur very large cost\n    Arguments.of(0.11, 1, 26474),\n    // 3 % above max slope, will incur very large cost\n    Arguments.of(0.12, 1, 37978)\n  );\n\n  /**\n   * This makes sure that when you exceed the max slope in a wheelchair there isn't a hard cut-off\n   * but rather the cost increases proportional to how much you go over the maximum.\n   * <p>\n   * In other words: 0.1 % over the limit only has a small cost but 1% over doubles the cost\n   * dramatically to the point where it's only used as a last resort.\n   */\n  @ParameterizedTest(\n    name = \"slope of {0} with maxSlopeExceededReluctance of {1} should lead to traversal costs of {2}\"\n  )\n  @VariableSource(\"slopeCases\")\n  public void shouldScaleCostWithMaxSlope(double slope, double reluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 1000;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(\n      V1,\n      V2,\n      null,\n      \"edge with elevation\",\n      length,\n      StreetTraversalPermission.ALL,\n      false\n    );\n\n    Coordinate[] profile = new Coordinate[] {\n      new Coordinate(0, 0),\n      new Coordinate(length, slope * length),\n    };\n\n    PackedCoordinateSequence elev = new PackedCoordinateSequence.Double(profile);\n    StreetElevationExtension.addToEdge(edge, elev, true);\n\n    assertEquals(slope, edge.getMaxSlope(), 0.0001);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(\n        true,\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        25,\n        0.09,\n        reluctance,\n        10\n      );\n    State result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertNotNull(result);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> wheelchairStairsCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(1, 22),\n    Arguments.of(10, 225),\n    Arguments.of(100, 2255)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(\n    name = \"wheelchair stairs reluctance of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\"\n  )\n  @VariableSource(\"wheelchairStairsCases\")\n  public void wheelchairStairsReluctance(double stairsReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 10;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"stairs\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n    edge.setStairs(true);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(\n        true,\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        25,\n        0,\n        1.1f,\n        stairsReluctance\n      );\n\n    req.walkReluctance = 1;\n\n    var result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    edge.setStairs(false);\n    var notStairsResult = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(7, (long) notStairsResult.weight);\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> inaccessibleStreetCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(1f, 15),\n    Arguments.of(10f, 150),\n    Arguments.of(100f, 1503)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(\n    name = \"an inaccessible street with the reluctance of {0} should lead to traversal costs of {1}\"\n  )\n  @VariableSource(\"inaccessibleStreetCases\")\n  public void inaccessibleStreet(float inaccessibleStreetReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 10;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"stairs\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n    edge.setWheelchairAccessible(false);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(\n        true,\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        ofOnlyAccessible(),\n        inaccessibleStreetReluctance,\n        0,\n        1.1f,\n        25\n      );\n\n    var result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    // reluctance should have no effect when the edge is accessible\n    edge.setWheelchairAccessible(true);\n    var accessibleResult = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(15, (long) accessibleResult.weight);\n  }\n\n  static Stream<Arguments> walkReluctanceCases = Stream.of(\n    Arguments.of(0.5, 3),\n    Arguments.of(1, 7),\n    Arguments.of(10, 75),\n    Arguments.of(100, 751)\n  );\n\n  @ParameterizedTest(\n    name = \"walkReluctance of {0} should affect wheelchair users and lead to traversal costs of {1}\"\n  )\n  @VariableSource(\"walkReluctanceCases\")\n  public void walkReluctance(double walkReluctance, long expectedCost) {\n    double length = 10;\n    var edge = new StreetEdge(V1, V2, null, \"stairs\", length, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL, false);\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setWalkReluctance(walkReluctance);\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility = WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.DEFAULT.withEnabled(true);\n\n    var result = traverse(edge, req);\n    assertEquals(expectedCost, (long) result.weight);\n\n    assertEquals(8, result.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n  }\n\n  private State traverse(StreetEdge edge, RouteRequest req) {\n    var ctx = new RoutingContext(req, graph, V1, V2);\n    var state = new State(ctx);\n\n    assertEquals(0, state.weight);\n    return edge.traverse(state);\n  }\n}\n"
    },
