      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/StreetTransitEntityLinkTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.WheelchairAccessibility.NOT_POSSIBLE;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.WheelchairAccessibility.NO_INFORMATION;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.WheelchairAccessibility.POSSIBLE;\n\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityFeature;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.SimpleVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitStopVertexBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.TransitMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.site.RegularStop;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\n\nclass StreetTransitEntityLinkTest {\n\n  private static Graph graph;\n  private static TransitModel transitModel;\n\n  RegularStop inaccessibleStop = TransitModelForTest.stopForTest(\n    \"A:inaccessible\",\n    \"wheelchair inaccessible stop\",\n    10.001,\n    10.001,\n    null,\n    NOT_POSSIBLE\n  );\n  RegularStop accessibleStop = TransitModelForTest.stopForTest(\n    \"A:accessible\",\n    \"wheelchair accessible stop\",\n    10.001,\n    10.001,\n    null,\n    POSSIBLE\n  );\n\n  RegularStop unknownStop = TransitModelForTest.stopForTest(\n    \"A:unknown\",\n    \"unknown\",\n    10.001,\n    10.001,\n    null,\n    NO_INFORMATION\n  );\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  static void setup() {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    transitModel = new TransitModel(new StopModel(), deduplicator);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void disallowInaccessibleStop() {\n    var afterTraversal = traverse(inaccessibleStop, true);\n    assertNull(afterTraversal);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void allowAccessibleStop() {\n    State afterTraversal = traverse(accessibleStop, true);\n\n    assertNotNull(afterTraversal);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void unknownStop() {\n    var afterTraversal = traverse(unknownStop, false);\n    assertNotNull(afterTraversal);\n\n    var afterStrictTraversal = traverse(unknownStop, true);\n    assertNull(afterStrictTraversal);\n  }\n\n  private State traverse(RegularStop stop, boolean onlyAccessible) {\n    var from = new SimpleVertex(graph, \"A\", 10, 10);\n    var to = new TransitStopVertexBuilder()\n      .withGraph(graph)\n      .withStop(stop)\n      .withModes(Set.of(TransitMode.RAIL))\n      .build();\n\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    WheelchairAccessibilityFeature feature;\n    if (onlyAccessible) {\n      feature = WheelchairAccessibilityFeature.ofOnlyAccessible();\n    } else {\n      feature = WheelchairAccessibilityFeature.ofCost(100, 100);\n    }\n    req.wheelchairAccessibility =\n      new WheelchairAccessibilityRequest(true, feature, feature, feature, 25, 8, 10, 25);\n\n    var ctx = new RoutingContext(req, graph, from, to);\n    var state = new State(ctx);\n\n    var edge = new StreetTransitStopLink(from, to);\n\n    return edge.traverse(state);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/TemporaryPartialStreetEdgeTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.linking.DisposableEdgeCollection;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.intersection_model.IntersectionTraversalCostModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Edge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.StreetVertexIndex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.location.TemporaryStreetLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.NonLocalizedString;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\npublic class TemporaryPartialStreetEdgeTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private IntersectionVertex v1, v2, v3, v4;\n  private StreetEdge e1, e1Reverse, e2, e3;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void setUp() throws Exception {\n    graph = new Graph();\n\n    // Graph for a fictional grid city with turn restrictions\n    v1 = vertex(\"maple_1st\", 2.0, 2.0);\n    v2 = vertex(\"maple_2nd\", 1.0, 2.0);\n    v3 = vertex(\"maple_3rd\", 0.0, 2.0);\n    v4 = vertex(\"maple_4th\", -1.0, 2.0);\n\n    e1 = edge(v1, v2, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    e1Reverse = edge(v2, v1, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    e2 = edge(v2, v3, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n    e3 = edge(v3, v4, 1.0, StreetTraversalPermission.ALL);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testConstruction() {\n    TemporaryPartialStreetEdge pEdge = newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      e1,\n      v1,\n      v2,\n      e1.getGeometry(),\n      \"partial e1\",\n      e1.getDistanceMeters()\n    );\n\n    assertTrue(pEdge.isEquivalentTo(e1));\n    assertFalse(pEdge.isBack());\n    assertFalse(pEdge.isReverseOf(e1));\n    assertTrue(pEdge.isReverseOf(e1Reverse));\n    assertEquals(e1.getPermission(), pEdge.getPermission());\n    assertEquals(e1.getCarSpeed(), pEdge.getCarSpeed(), 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTraversal() {\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(options, graph, v1, v2);\n\n    // Partial edge with same endpoints as the parent.\n    TemporaryPartialStreetEdge pEdge1 = newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      e1,\n      v1,\n      v2,\n      e1.getGeometry(),\n      \"partial e1\",\n      e1.getDistanceMeters()\n    );\n    TemporaryPartialStreetEdge pEdge2 = newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      e2,\n      v2,\n      v3,\n      e2.getGeometry(),\n      \"partial e2\",\n      e2.getDistanceMeters()\n    );\n\n    // Traverse both the partial and parent edges.\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n    State s1 = e1.traverse(s0);\n\n    State partialS0 = new State(routingContext);\n    State partialS1 = pEdge1.traverse(partialS0);\n\n    // Traversal of original and partial edges should yield the same results.\n    assertEquals(s1.getTimeSeconds(), partialS1.getTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(s1.getElapsedTimeSeconds(), partialS1.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(s1.getWeight(), partialS1.getWeight(), 0.0);\n\n    // Now traverse the second partial/parent edge pair.\n    State s2 = e2.traverse(s1);\n    State partialS2 = pEdge2.traverse(partialS1);\n\n    // Same checks as above.\n    assertEquals(s2.getTimeSeconds(), partialS2.getTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(s2.getElapsedTimeSeconds(), partialS2.getElapsedTimeSeconds());\n    assertEquals(s2.getWeight(), partialS2.getWeight(), 0.0);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTraversalOfSubdividedEdge() {\n    DisposableEdgeCollection tempEdges = new DisposableEdgeCollection(graph);\n    Coordinate nearestPoint = new Coordinate(0.5, 2.0);\n    List<StreetEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();\n    edges.add(e2);\n    TemporaryStreetLocation end = StreetVertexIndex.createTemporaryStreetLocationForTest(\n      \"middle of e2\",\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"foo\"),\n      edges,\n      nearestPoint,\n      true,\n      tempEdges\n    );\n    TemporaryStreetLocation start = StreetVertexIndex.createTemporaryStreetLocationForTest(\n      \"middle of e2\",\n      new NonLocalizedString(\"foo\"),\n      edges,\n      nearestPoint,\n      false,\n      tempEdges\n    );\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setMode(TraverseMode.CAR);\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(options, graph, v1, v2);\n\n    // All intersections take 10 minutes - we'll notice if one isn't counted.\n    double turnDurationSecs = 10.0 * 60.0;\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new DummyCostModel(turnDurationSecs));\n    options.turnReluctance = (1.0);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n    State s1 = e1.traverse(s0);\n    State s2 = e2.traverse(s1);\n    State s3 = e3.traverse(s2);\n\n    Edge partialE2First = end.getIncoming().iterator().next();\n    Edge partialE2Second = start.getOutgoing().iterator().next();\n\n    State partialS0 = new State(routingContext);\n    State partialS1 = e1.traverse(partialS0);\n    State partialS2A = partialE2First.traverse(partialS1);\n    State partialS2B = partialE2Second.traverse(partialS2A);\n    State partialS3 = e3.traverse(partialS2B);\n\n    // Should start at the same time.\n    assertEquals(s0.getTimeSeconds(), partialS0.getTimeSeconds());\n\n    // Time and cost should be the same up to a rounding difference.\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(s3.getTimeSeconds() - partialS3.getTimeSeconds()) <= 1);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(s3.getElapsedTimeSeconds() - partialS3.getElapsedTimeSeconds()) <= 1);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(s3.getWeight() - partialS3.getWeight()) <= 1);\n\n    // All intersections take 0 seconds now.\n    graph.setIntersectionTraversalCostModel(new DummyCostModel(0.0));\n\n    State s0NoCost = new State(routingContext);\n    State s1NoCost = e1.traverse(s0NoCost);\n    State s2NoCost = e2.traverse(s1NoCost);\n    State s3NoCost = e3.traverse(s2NoCost);\n\n    State partialS0NoCost = new State(routingContext);\n    State partialS1NoCost = e1.traverse(partialS0NoCost);\n    State partialS2ANoCost = partialE2First.traverse(partialS1NoCost);\n    State partialS2BNoCost = partialE2Second.traverse(partialS2ANoCost);\n    State partialS3NoCost = e3.traverse(partialS2BNoCost);\n\n    // Time and cost should be the same up to a rounding difference.\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(s3NoCost.getTimeSeconds() - partialS3NoCost.getTimeSeconds()) <= 1);\n    assertTrue(\n      Math.abs(s3NoCost.getElapsedTimeSeconds() - partialS3NoCost.getElapsedTimeSeconds()) <= 1\n    );\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(s3NoCost.getWeight() - partialS3NoCost.getWeight()) <= 1);\n\n    // Difference in duration and weight between now and before should be\n    // entirely due to the crossing of 2 intersections at v2 and v3.\n    double expectedDifference = 2 * 10 * 60.0;\n    double durationDiff = s3.getTimeSeconds() - s3NoCost.getTimeSeconds();\n    double partialDurationDiff = partialS3.getTimeSeconds() - partialS3NoCost.getTimeSeconds();\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(durationDiff - expectedDifference) <= 1);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(partialDurationDiff - expectedDifference) <= 1);\n\n    // Turn reluctance is 1.0, so weight == duration.\n    double weightDiff = s3.getWeight() - s3NoCost.getWeight();\n    double partialWeightDiff = partialS3.getWeight() - partialS3NoCost.getWeight();\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(weightDiff - expectedDifference) <= 1);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(partialWeightDiff - expectedDifference) <= 1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testReverseEdge() {\n    TemporaryPartialStreetEdge pEdge1 = newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      e1,\n      v1,\n      v2,\n      e1.getGeometry(),\n      \"partial e1\",\n      e1.getDistanceMeters()\n    );\n    TemporaryPartialStreetEdge pEdge2 = newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      e1Reverse,\n      v2,\n      v1,\n      e1Reverse.getGeometry(),\n      \"partial e2\",\n      e1Reverse.getDistanceMeters()\n    );\n\n    assertFalse(e1.isReverseOf(pEdge1));\n    assertFalse(pEdge1.isReverseOf(e1));\n\n    assertFalse(e1Reverse.isReverseOf(pEdge2));\n    assertFalse(pEdge2.isReverseOf(e1Reverse));\n\n    assertTrue(e1.isReverseOf(pEdge2));\n    assertTrue(e1Reverse.isReverseOf(pEdge1));\n    assertTrue(e1Reverse.isReverseOf(e1));\n    assertTrue(e1.isReverseOf(e1Reverse));\n    assertTrue(pEdge1.isReverseOf(pEdge2));\n    assertTrue(pEdge2.isReverseOf(pEdge1));\n  }\n\n  /* Private Methods */\n\n  static TemporaryPartialStreetEdge newTemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n    StreetEdge parentEdge,\n    StreetVertex v1,\n    StreetVertex v2,\n    LineString geometry,\n    String name,\n    double length\n  ) {\n    return new TemporaryPartialStreetEdge(\n      parentEdge,\n      v1,\n      v2,\n      geometry,\n      new NonLocalizedString(name),\n      length\n    );\n  }\n\n  private IntersectionVertex vertex(String label, double lat, double lon) {\n    return new IntersectionVertex(graph, label, lat, lon);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create an edge. If twoWay, create two edges (back and forth).\n   */\n  private StreetEdge edge(\n    StreetVertex vA,\n    StreetVertex vB,\n    double length,\n    StreetTraversalPermission perm\n  ) {\n    String labelA = vA.getLabel();\n    String labelB = vB.getLabel();\n    String name = String.format(\"%s_%s\", labelA, labelB);\n    Coordinate[] coords = new Coordinate[2];\n    coords[0] = vA.getCoordinate();\n    coords[1] = vB.getCoordinate();\n    LineString geom = GeometryUtils.getGeometryFactory().createLineString(coords);\n\n    return new StreetEdge(vA, vB, geom, name, length, perm, false);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Dummy cost model. Returns what you put in.\n   */\n  private static class DummyCostModel implements IntersectionTraversalCostModel {\n\n    private final double turnCostSecs;\n\n    public DummyCostModel(double turnCostSecs) {\n      this.turnCostSecs = turnCostSecs;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public double computeTraversalCost(\n      IntersectionVertex v,\n      StreetEdge from,\n      StreetEdge to,\n      TraverseMode mode,\n      float fromSpeed,\n      float toSpeed\n    ) {\n      return this.turnCostSecs;\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/TriangleTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Coordinate;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.GeometryFactory;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.LineString;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.impl.PackedCoordinateSequence;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.BicycleOptimizeType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.util.ElevationUtils;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.util.SlopeCosts;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.StreetVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.NonLocalizedString;\n\npublic class TriangleTest {\n\n  float DELTA = 0.001f;\n\n  @Test\n  public void testPreciseValues() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(0.5, 0.4, 0.1);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0.5, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0.4, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 0.1, DELTA);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testNormalizationGreaterThan1() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(1, 1, 1);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(10, 20, 30);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0.166, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 0.5, DELTA);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testNormalizationLessThan1() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(0.1, 0.1, 0.1);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testZero() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(0, 0, 0.1);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 1, DELTA);\n\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(0, 0, 0);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 0.333, DELTA);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testLessThanZero() {\n    var req = new RouteRequest();\n    req.setTriangleNormalized(-1, -1, 0.1);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSafetyFactor, 0, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleSlopeFactor, 0, DELTA);\n    assertEquals(req.bikeTriangleTimeFactor, 1, DELTA);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTriangle() {\n    Coordinate c1 = new Coordinate(-122.575033, 45.456773);\n    Coordinate c2 = new Coordinate(-122.576668, 45.451426);\n\n    StreetVertex v1 = new IntersectionVertex(null, \"v1\", c1.x, c1.y, (NonLocalizedString) null);\n    StreetVertex v2 = new IntersectionVertex(null, \"v2\", c2.x, c2.y, (NonLocalizedString) null);\n\n    GeometryFactory factory = new GeometryFactory();\n    LineString geometry = factory.createLineString(new Coordinate[] { c1, c2 });\n\n    double length = 650.0;\n\n    StreetEdge testStreet = new StreetEdge(\n      v1,\n      v2,\n      geometry,\n      \"Test Lane\",\n      length,\n      StreetTraversalPermission.ALL,\n      false\n    );\n    testStreet.setBicycleSafetyFactor(0.74f); // a safe street\n\n    Coordinate[] profile = new Coordinate[] {\n      new Coordinate(0, 0), // slope = 0.1\n      new Coordinate(length / 2, length / 20.0),\n      new Coordinate(length, 0), // slope = -0.1\n    };\n    PackedCoordinateSequence elev = new PackedCoordinateSequence.Double(profile);\n    StreetElevationExtension.addToEdge(testStreet, elev, false);\n\n    SlopeCosts costs = ElevationUtils.getSlopeCosts(elev, true);\n    double trueLength = costs.lengthMultiplier * length;\n    double slopeWorkLength = testStreet.getEffectiveBikeDistanceForWorkCost();\n    double slopeSpeedLength = testStreet.getEffectiveBikeDistance();\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    options.bicycleOptimizeType = BicycleOptimizeType.TRIANGLE;\n    options.bikeSpeed = 6.0;\n    options.setNonTransitReluctance(1);\n\n    options.setTriangleNormalized(0, 0, 1);\n    State startState = new State(v1, options, null);\n    State result = testStreet.traverse(startState);\n    double timeWeight = result.getWeight();\n    double expectedTimeWeight = slopeSpeedLength / options.getSpeed(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(expectedTimeWeight - timeWeight) < 0.00001);\n\n    options.setTriangleNormalized(0, 1, 0);\n    startState = new State(v1, options, null);\n    result = testStreet.traverse(startState);\n    double slopeWeight = result.getWeight();\n    double expectedSlopeWeight = slopeWorkLength / options.getSpeed(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(expectedSlopeWeight - slopeWeight) < 0.00001);\n    assertTrue(length * 1.5 / options.getSpeed(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false) < slopeWeight);\n    assertTrue(length * 1.5 * 10 / options.getSpeed(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false) > slopeWeight);\n\n    options.setTriangleNormalized(1, 0, 0);\n    startState = new State(v1, options, null);\n    result = testStreet.traverse(startState);\n    double safetyWeight = result.getWeight();\n    double slopeSafety = costs.slopeSafetyCost;\n    double expectedSafetyWeight =\n      (trueLength * 0.74 + slopeSafety) / options.getSpeed(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false);\n    assertTrue(Math.abs(expectedSafetyWeight - safetyWeight) < 0.00001);\n\n    final double ONE_THIRD = 1 / 3.0;\n    options.setTriangleNormalized(ONE_THIRD, ONE_THIRD, ONE_THIRD);\n    startState = new State(v1, options, null);\n    result = testStreet.traverse(startState);\n    double averageWeight = result.getWeight();\n    assertTrue(\n      Math.abs(\n        safetyWeight * ONE_THIRD + slopeWeight * ONE_THIRD + timeWeight * ONE_THIRD - averageWeight\n      ) <\n      0.00000001\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/VehicleParkingEdgeTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.GraphRoutingTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.State;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vehicle_parking.VehicleParking;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vehicle_parking.VehicleParkingSpaces;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.VehicleParkingEntranceVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model._data.TransitModelForTest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.basic.NonLocalizedString;\n\nclass VehicleParkingEdgeTest extends GraphRoutingTest {\n\n  Graph graph;\n  VehicleParkingEdge vehicleParkingEdge;\n  RoutingContext routingContext;\n\n  @Test\n  public void availableCarPlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.CAR, false, true);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void notAvailableCarPlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.CAR, false, false);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeAvailableCarPlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(\n      TraverseMode.CAR,\n      false,\n      true,\n      VehicleParkingSpaces.builder().carSpaces(1).build(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeAvailableCarPlacesFallbackTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.CAR, false, true, null, true);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeNotAvailableCarPlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(\n      TraverseMode.CAR,\n      false,\n      true,\n      VehicleParkingSpaces.builder().carSpaces(0).build(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void availableBicyclePlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, true, false);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void notAvailableBicyclePlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, false, false);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeAvailableBicyclePlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(\n      TraverseMode.BICYCLE,\n      true,\n      false,\n      VehicleParkingSpaces.builder().bicycleSpaces(1).build(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeAvailableBicyclePlacesFallbackTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(TraverseMode.BICYCLE, true, false, null, true);\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNotNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void realtimeNotAvailableBicyclePlacesTest() {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(\n      TraverseMode.BICYCLE,\n      true,\n      false,\n      VehicleParkingSpaces.builder().bicycleSpaces(0).build(),\n      true\n    );\n\n    State s0 = new State(routingContext);\n\n    State s1 = vehicleParkingEdge.traverse(s0);\n\n    assertNull(s1);\n  }\n\n  private void initEdgeAndRequest(\n    TraverseMode parkingMode,\n    boolean bicyclePlaces,\n    boolean carPlaces\n  ) {\n    initEdgeAndRequest(parkingMode, bicyclePlaces, carPlaces, null, false);\n  }\n\n  private void initEdgeAndRequest(\n    TraverseMode parkingMode,\n    boolean bicyclePlaces,\n    boolean carPlaces,\n    VehicleParkingSpaces availability,\n    boolean realtime\n  ) {\n    graph = new Graph();\n\n    var vehicleParking = createVehicleParking(bicyclePlaces, carPlaces, availability);\n    var vertex = new VehicleParkingEntranceVertex(graph, vehicleParking.getEntrances().get(0));\n\n    vehicleParkingEdge = new VehicleParkingEdge(vertex);\n\n    RouteRequest routingRequest = new RouteRequest();\n    routingRequest.parkAndRide = true;\n    routingRequest.useVehicleParkingAvailabilityInformation = realtime;\n    routingRequest.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.WALK, parkingMode);\n    routingContext = new RoutingContext(routingRequest, graph, vertex, vertex);\n  }\n\n  private VehicleParking createVehicleParking(\n    boolean bicyclePlaces,\n    boolean carPlaces,\n    VehicleParkingSpaces availability\n  ) {\n    return VehicleParking\n      .builder()\n      .id(TransitModelForTest.id(\"VehicleParking\"))\n      .bicyclePlaces(bicyclePlaces)\n      .carPlaces(carPlaces)\n      .availability(availability)\n      .entrances(List.of(vehicleParkingEntrance()))\n      .build();\n  }\n\n  private VehicleParking.VehicleParkingEntranceCreator vehicleParkingEntrance() {\n    String id = \"Entrance\";\n    return builder ->\n      builder.entranceId(TransitModelForTest.id(id)).name(new NonLocalizedString(id)).x(0).y(0);\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/edgetype/loader/GeometryProcessorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.loader;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.gtfs.GtfsContextBuilder.contextBuilder;\n\nimport com.google.common.collect.Iterables;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.time.ZonedDateTime;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Disabled;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.DataImportIssue;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.DataImportIssueStore;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.issues.NegativeHopTime;\nimport org.opentripplanner.graph_builder.module.StreetLinkerModule;\nimport org.opentripplanner.gtfs.GtfsContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.astar.AStarBuilder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.WheelchairAccessibilityRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetEdge;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTransitStopLink;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.edgetype.StreetTraversalPermission;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Vertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.GraphPath;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.spt.ShortestPathTree;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.IntersectionVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.vertextype.TransitStopVertex;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.model.framework.Deduplicator;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.StopModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.service.TransitModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.TestUtils;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.geometry.GeometryUtils;\n\n/**\n * TODO OTP2 - Test is too close to the implementation and will need to be reimplemented.\n */\n@Disabled\npublic class GeometryProcessorTest {\n\n  private Graph graph;\n  private TransitModel transitModel;\n\n  private GtfsContext context;\n  private String feedId;\n  private DataImportIssueStore issueStore;\n\n  @BeforeEach\n  public void setUp() throws Exception {\n    var deduplicator = new Deduplicator();\n    var stopModel = new StopModel();\n    graph = new Graph(deduplicator);\n    transitModel = new TransitModel(stopModel, deduplicator);\n    this.issueStore = new DataImportIssueStore();\n\n    context =\n      contextBuilder(ConstantsForTests.FAKE_GTFS).withIssueStoreAndDeduplicator(graph).build();\n\n    feedId = context.getFeedId().getId();\n    transitModel.updateCalendarServiceData(true, context.getCalendarServiceData(), null);\n\n    String[] stops = {\n      feedId + \":A\",\n      feedId + \":B\",\n      feedId + \":C\",\n      feedId + \":D\",\n      feedId + \":E\",\n      feedId + \":entrance_a\",\n      feedId + \":entrance_b\",\n    };\n    for (String s : stops) {\n      TransitStopVertex stop = (TransitStopVertex) (graph.getVertex(s));\n\n      IntersectionVertex front = new IntersectionVertex(\n        graph,\n        \"near_1_\" + stop.getStop().getId(),\n        stop.getX() + 0.0001,\n        stop.getY() + 0.0001\n      );\n      IntersectionVertex back = new IntersectionVertex(\n        graph,\n        \"near_2_\" + stop.getStop().getId(),\n        stop.getX() - 0.0001,\n        stop.getY() - 0.0001\n      );\n\n      StreetEdge street1 = new StreetEdge(\n        front,\n        back,\n        GeometryUtils.makeLineString(\n          stop.getX() + 0.0001,\n          stop.getY() + 0.0001,\n          stop.getX() - 0.0001,\n          stop.getY() - 0.0001\n        ),\n        \"street\",\n        100,\n        StreetTraversalPermission.ALL,\n        false\n      );\n      StreetEdge street2 = new StreetEdge(\n        back,\n        front,\n        GeometryUtils.makeLineString(\n          stop.getX() - 0.0001,\n          stop.getY() - 0.0001,\n          stop.getX() + 0.0001,\n          stop.getY() + 0.0001\n        ),\n        \"street\",\n        100,\n        StreetTraversalPermission.ALL,\n        true\n      );\n    }\n\n    //Linkers aren't run otherwise\n    graph.hasStreets = true;\n    StreetLinkerModule.linkStreetsForTestOnly(graph, transitModel);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testIssue() {\n    boolean found = false;\n    for (DataImportIssue it : issueStore.getIssues()) {\n      if (it instanceof NegativeHopTime) {\n        NegativeHopTime nht = (NegativeHopTime) it;\n        assertTrue(nht.st0.getDepartureTime() > nht.st1.getArrivalTime());\n        found = true;\n      }\n    }\n    assertTrue(found);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testRoutingOverMidnight() {\n    // this route only runs on weekdays\n    Vertex stop_g = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":G_depart\");\n    Vertex stop_h = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":H_arrive\");\n\n    ShortestPathTree spt;\n    GraphPath path;\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n\n    // Friday evening\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 18, 23, 20, 0));\n\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_g, stop_h))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_h);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(4, path.states.size());\n\n    // Saturday morning\n    long startTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 19, 0, 5, 0);\n    options.setDateTime(Instant.ofEpochSecond(startTime));\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_g, stop_h))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_h);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(4, path.states.size());\n    long endTime = path.getEndTime();\n    assertTrue(endTime < startTime + 60 * 60);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testPickupDropoff() {\n    Vertex stop_o = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":O_depart\");\n    Vertex stop_p = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":P\");\n    assertEquals(2, stop_o.getOutgoing().size());\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 19, 12, 0, 0));\n    ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_o, stop_p))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n    GraphPath path = spt.getPath(stop_p);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    long endTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 19, 12, 10, 0);\n    assertEquals(endTime, path.getEndTime());\n\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 19, 12, 0, 1));\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_o, stop_p))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_p);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    endTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 19, 15, 10, 0);\n    assertEquals(endTime, path.getEndTime());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testTimelessStops() {\n    Vertex stop_d = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":D\");\n    Vertex stop_c = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":C\");\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 1, 10, 0, 0));\n    ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_d, stop_c))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = spt.getPath(stop_c);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 1, 11, 0, 0),\n      path.getEndTime()\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testWheelchairAccessible() {\n    Vertex near_a = graph.getVertex(\"near_1_\" + feedId + \"_entrance_a\");\n    Vertex near_b = graph.getVertex(\"near_1_\" + feedId + \"_entrance_b\");\n    Vertex near_c = graph.getVertex(\"near_1_\" + feedId + \"_C\");\n    Vertex near_e = graph.getVertex(\"near_1_\" + feedId + \"_E\");\n\n    Vertex stop_d = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":D\");\n    Vertex split_d = null;\n    for (StreetTransitStopLink e : Iterables.filter(\n      stop_d.getOutgoing(),\n      StreetTransitStopLink.class\n    )) {\n      split_d = e.getToVertex();\n    }\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.wheelchairAccessibility = WheelchairAccessibilityRequest.makeDefault(true);\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 18, 0, 0, 0));\n\n    ShortestPathTree spt;\n    GraphPath path;\n\n    // stop B is accessible, so there should be a path.\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, near_a, near_b))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    path = spt.getPath(near_b);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // stop C is not accessible, so there should be no path.\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, near_a, near_c))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    path = spt.getPath(near_c);\n    assertNull(path);\n\n    // stop E has no accessibility information, but we should still be able to route to it.\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, near_a, near_e))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    path = spt.getPath(near_e);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n\n    // from stop A to stop D would normally be trip 1.1 to trip 2.1, arriving at 00:30. But trip\n    // 2 is not accessible, so we'll do 1.1 to 3.1, arriving at 01:00\n    LocalDateTime ldt = LocalDateTime.of(2009, 7, 18, 0, 0, 0);\n    ZonedDateTime zdt = ZonedDateTime.of(ldt, ZoneId.of(\"America/New_York\"));\n    options.setDateTime(zdt.toInstant());\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, near_a, split_d))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    ZonedDateTime endTime = zdt.plusHours(1).plusSeconds(1);\n    // 1 sec for the StreetTransitLink\n    path = spt.getPath(split_d);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(endTime.toEpochSecond(), path.getEndTime());\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testRunForTrain() {\n    // This is the notorious Byrd bug: we're going from Q to T at 8:30.\n    // There's a trip from S to T at 8:50 and a second one at 9:50.\n    // To get to S by 8:50, we need to take trip 12.1 from Q to R, and 13.1\n    // from R to S.  If we take the direct-but-slower 11.1, we'll miss\n    // the 8:50 and have to catch the 9:50.\n    Vertex destination = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":T\");\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    // test is designed such that transfers must be instantaneous\n    options.transferSlack = 0;\n    LocalDateTime ldt = LocalDateTime.of(2009, 10, 2, 8, 30, 0);\n    ZonedDateTime startTime = ZonedDateTime.of(ldt, ZoneId.of(\"America/New_York\"));\n    options.setDateTime(startTime.toInstant());\n    Vertex q = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":Q\");\n    ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, q, destination))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n    GraphPath path = spt.getPath(destination);\n\n    long endTime = path.getEndTime();\n    assertTrue(endTime - startTime.toEpochSecond() < 7200);\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testFrequencies() {\n    Vertex stop_u = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":U_depart\");\n    Vertex stop_v = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":V_arrive\");\n\n    ShortestPathTree spt;\n    GraphPath path;\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setStreetSubRequestModes(new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.TRANSIT));\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 0, 0, 0));\n\n    // U to V - original stop times - shouldn't be used\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_u, stop_v))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_v);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(4, path.states.size());\n    long endTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 6, 40, 0);\n    assertEquals(endTime, path.getEndTime());\n\n    // U to V - first frequency\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 7, 0, 0));\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_u, stop_v))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_v);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(4, path.states.size());\n    endTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 7, 40, 0);\n    assertEquals(endTime, path.getEndTime());\n\n    // U to V - second frequency\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 14, 0, 0));\n    spt =\n      AStarBuilder\n        .oneToOne()\n        .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, stop_u, stop_v))\n        .getShortestPathTree();\n    path = spt.getPath(stop_v);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(4, path.states.size());\n    endTime = TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 7, 14, 40, 0);\n    assertEquals(endTime, path.getEndTime());\n    // TODO more detailed testing of frequencies\n\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void testPathways() {\n    Vertex entrance = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":entrance_a\");\n    assertNotNull(entrance);\n    Vertex stop = graph.getVertex(feedId + \":A\");\n    assertNotNull(stop);\n\n    RouteRequest options = new RouteRequest();\n    options.setDateTime(TestUtils.dateInstant(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 1, 16, 0, 0));\n    ShortestPathTree spt = AStarBuilder\n      .oneToOne()\n      .setContext(new RoutingContext(options, graph, entrance, stop))\n      .getShortestPathTree();\n\n    GraphPath path = spt.getPath(stop);\n    assertNotNull(path);\n    assertEquals(\n      TestUtils.dateInSeconds(\"America/New_York\", 2009, 8, 1, 16, 0, 34),\n      path.getEndTime()\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/street/BarrierRoutingTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.street;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertAll;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode.BICYCLE;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode.CAR;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.test.support.PolylineAssert.assertThatPolylinesAreEqual;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.function.Consumer;\nimport java.util.function.Function;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.function.Executable;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Geometry;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.Itinerary;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.plan.WalkStep;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping.GraphPathToItineraryMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TemporaryVerticesContainer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.PolylineEncoder;\n\npublic class BarrierRoutingTest {\n\n  private static final Instant dateTime = Instant.now();\n\n  private static Graph graph;\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void createGraph() {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmGraph(\n      ConstantsForTests.HERRENBERG_BARRIER_GATES_OSM\n    );\n    graph = model.graph();\n    graph.index(model.transitModel().getStopModel());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Access restrictions on nodes should be taken into account, with walking the bike if needed.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void shouldWalkForBarriers() {\n    var from = new GenericLocation(48.59384, 8.86848);\n    var to = new GenericLocation(48.59370, 8.87079);\n\n    // This takes a detour to avoid walking with the bike\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(graph, from, to, BICYCLE);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"o~qgH_ccu@DGFENQZ]NOLOHMFKFILB`BOGo@AeD]U}BaA]Q??\");\n\n    // The reluctance for walking with the bike is reduced, so a detour is not taken\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(\n      graph,\n      from,\n      to,\n      BICYCLE,\n      rr -> rr.bikeWalkingReluctance = 1,\n      itineraries ->\n        itineraries\n          .stream()\n          .flatMap(i ->\n            Stream.of(\n              () -> assertEquals(1, i.getLegs().size()),\n              () -> assertEquals(BICYCLE, i.getLegs().get(0).getMode()),\n              () ->\n                assertEquals(\n                  List.of(false, true, false, true, false),\n                  i\n                    .getLegs()\n                    .get(0)\n                    .getWalkSteps()\n                    .stream()\n                    .map(WalkStep::isWalkingBike)\n                    .collect(Collectors.toList())\n                )\n            )\n          )\n    );\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline2, \"o~qgH_ccu@Bi@Bk@Bi@Bg@NaA@_@Dm@Dq@a@KJy@@I@M@E??\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Car-only barriers should be driven around.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void shouldDriveAroundBarriers() {\n    var from = new GenericLocation(48.59291, 8.87037);\n    var to = new GenericLocation(48.59262, 8.86879);\n\n    // This takes a detour to avoid walking with the bike\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(graph, from, to, CAR);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"sxqgHyncu@ZTnAFRdEyAFPpA\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void shouldDriveToBarrier() {\n    var from = new GenericLocation(48.59291, 8.87037);\n    var to = new GenericLocation(48.59276, 8.86963);\n\n    // This takes a detour to avoid walking with the bike\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(graph, from, to, CAR);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"sxqgHyncu@ZT?~B\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void shouldDriveFromBarrier() {\n    var from = new GenericLocation(48.59273, 8.86931);\n    var to = new GenericLocation(48.59291, 8.87037);\n\n    // This takes a detour to avoid walking with the bike\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(graph, from, to, CAR);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"qwqgHchcu@BTxAGSeEoAG[U\");\n  }\n\n  private static String computePolyline(\n    Graph graph,\n    GenericLocation from,\n    GenericLocation to,\n    TraverseMode traverseMode\n  ) {\n    return computePolyline(\n      graph,\n      from,\n      to,\n      traverseMode,\n      ignored -> {},\n      itineraries ->\n        itineraries\n          .stream()\n          .flatMap(i -> i.getLegs().stream())\n          .map(l ->\n            () -> assertEquals(traverseMode, l.getMode(), \"Allow only \" + traverseMode + \" legs\")\n          )\n    );\n  }\n\n  private static String computePolyline(\n    Graph graph,\n    GenericLocation from,\n    GenericLocation to,\n    TraverseMode traverseMode,\n    Consumer<RouteRequest> options,\n    Function<List<Itinerary>, Stream<Executable>> assertions\n  ) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n    request.setDateTime(dateTime);\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n    request.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(traverseMode);\n\n    options.accept(request);\n\n    var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, request);\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, temporaryVertices);\n\n    var gpf = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(5));\n    var paths = gpf.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n    GraphPathToItineraryMapper graphPathToItineraryMapper = new GraphPathToItineraryMapper(\n      ZoneId.of(\"Europe/Berlin\"),\n      graph.streetNotesService,\n      graph.ellipsoidToGeoidDifference\n    );\n\n    var itineraries = graphPathToItineraryMapper.mapItineraries(paths);\n\n    assertAll(assertions.apply(itineraries));\n\n    Geometry legGeometry = itineraries.get(0).getLegs().get(0).getLegGeometry();\n    temporaryVertices.close();\n\n    return PolylineEncoder.encodeGeometry(legGeometry).points();\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.street;\n\nimport static org.opentripplanner.test.support.PolylineAssert.assertThatPolylinesAreEqual;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Geometry;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping.GraphPathToItineraryMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.BicycleOptimizeType;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TemporaryVerticesContainer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.PolylineEncoder;\n\npublic class BicycleRoutingTest {\n\n  static final Instant dateTime = Instant.now();\n  private final Graph herrenbergGraph;\n\n  {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmGraph(ConstantsForTests.HERRENBERG_OSM);\n    herrenbergGraph = model.graph();\n\n    model.transitModel().index();\n    herrenbergGraph.index(model.transitModel().getStopModel());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * https://www.openstreetmap.org/way/22392895 is access=destination which means that both bicycles\n   * and motor vehicles must not pass through.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void shouldRespectGeneralNoThroughTraffic() {\n    var mozartStr = new GenericLocation(48.59713, 8.86107);\n    var fritzLeharStr = new GenericLocation(48.59696, 8.85806);\n\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, mozartStr, fritzLeharStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"_srgHutau@h@B|@Jf@BdAG?\\\\JT@jA?DSp@_@fFsAT{@DBpC\");\n\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, fritzLeharStr, mozartStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline2, \"{qrgH{aau@CqCz@ErAU^gFRq@?EAkAKUeACg@A_AM_AEDQF@H?\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Tests that https://www.openstreetmap.org/way/35097400 is allowed for cars due to\n   * motor_vehicle=destination being meant for cars only.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void shouldNotRespectMotorCarNoThru() {\n    var schiessmauer = new GenericLocation(48.59737, 8.86350);\n    var zeppelinStr = new GenericLocation(48.59972, 8.86239);\n\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, schiessmauer, zeppelinStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"otrgH{cbu@S_AU_AmAdAyApAGDs@h@_@\\\\_ClBe@^?S\");\n\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, zeppelinStr, schiessmauer);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline2, \"ccsgH{|au@?Rd@_@~BmB^]r@i@FExAqAlAeAT~@R~@\");\n  }\n\n  private static String computePolyline(Graph graph, GenericLocation from, GenericLocation to) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n    request.setDateTime(dateTime);\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n    request.bicycleOptimizeType = BicycleOptimizeType.QUICK;\n\n    request.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.BICYCLE);\n    var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, request);\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, temporaryVertices);\n\n    var gpf = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(5));\n    var paths = gpf.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n    GraphPathToItineraryMapper graphPathToItineraryMapper = new GraphPathToItineraryMapper(\n      ZoneId.of(\"Europe/Berlin\"),\n      graph.streetNotesService,\n      graph.ellipsoidToGeoidDifference\n    );\n\n    var itineraries = graphPathToItineraryMapper.mapItineraries(paths);\n    temporaryVertices.close();\n\n    // make sure that we only get BICYLE legs\n    itineraries.forEach(i ->\n      i.getLegs().forEach(l -> Assertions.assertEquals(l.getMode(), TraverseMode.BICYCLE))\n    );\n    Geometry legGeometry = itineraries.get(0).getLegs().get(0).getLegGeometry();\n    return PolylineEncoder.encodeGeometry(legGeometry).points();\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/street/CarRoutingTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.street;\n\nimport static org.opentripplanner.test.support.PolylineAssert.assertThatPolylinesAreEqual;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Geometry;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping.GraphPathToItineraryMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TemporaryVerticesContainer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.PolylineEncoder;\n\npublic class CarRoutingTest {\n\n  static final Instant dateTime = Instant.now();\n\n  private static Graph herrenbergGraph;\n\n  @BeforeAll\n  public static void setup() {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmGraph(ConstantsForTests.HERRENBERG_OSM);\n    herrenbergGraph = model.index().graph();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * The OTP algorithm tries hard to never visit the same node twice. This is generally a good idea\n   * because it avoids useless loops in the traversal leading to way faster processing time.\n   * <p>\n   * However there is are certain rare pathological cases where through a series of turn\n   * restrictions and roadworks you absolutely must visit a vertex twice if you want to produce a\n   * result. One example would be a route like this: https://tinyurl.com/ycqux93g (Note: At the time\n   * of writing this Hindenburgstr. (https://www.openstreetmap.org/way/415545869) is closed due to\n   * roadworks.)\n   * <p>\n   * This test checks that such a loop is possible.\n   * <p>\n   * More information: https://github.com/opentripplanner/OpenTripPlanner/issues/3393\n   */\n  @Test\n  @DisplayName(\"car routes can contain loops (traversing the same edge twice)\")\n  public void shouldAllowLoopCausedByTurnRestrictions() {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmGraph(\n      ConstantsForTests.HERRENBERG_HINDENBURG_STR_UNDER_CONSTRUCTION_OSM\n    );\n    var hindenburgStrUnderConstruction = model.index().graph();\n\n    var gueltsteinerStr = new GenericLocation(48.59240, 8.87024);\n    var aufDemGraben = new GenericLocation(48.59487, 8.87133);\n\n    var polyline = computePolyline(hindenburgStrUnderConstruction, gueltsteinerStr, aufDemGraben);\n\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      polyline,\n      \"ouqgH}mcu@gAE]U}BaA]Q}@]uAs@[SAm@Ee@AUEi@XEQkBQ?Bz@Dt@Dh@@TGBC@KBSHGx@\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void shouldRespectGeneralNoThroughTraffic() {\n    var mozartStr = new GenericLocation(48.59521, 8.88391);\n    var fritzLeharStr = new GenericLocation(48.59460, 8.88291);\n\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, mozartStr, fritzLeharStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline1, \"_grgHkcfu@OjBC\\\\ARGjAKzAfBz@j@n@Rk@E}D\");\n\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, fritzLeharStr, mozartStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(polyline2, \"gcrgHc}eu@D|DSj@k@o@gB{@J{AFkA@SB]NkB\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Tests that that https://www.openstreetmap.org/way/35097400 is not taken due to\n   * motor_vehicle=destination.\n   */\n  @Test\n  public void shouldRespectMotorCarNoThru() {\n    var schiessmauer = new GenericLocation(48.59737, 8.86350);\n    var zeppelinStr = new GenericLocation(48.59972, 8.86239);\n\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, schiessmauer, zeppelinStr);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      polyline1,\n      \"otrgH{cbu@v@|D?bAElBEv@Cj@APGAY?YD]Fm@X_@Pw@d@eAn@k@VM@]He@Fo@Bi@??c@?Q@gD?Q?Q@mD?S\"\n    );\n\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, zeppelinStr, schiessmauer);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      polyline2,\n      \"ccsgH{|au@?RAlD?P?PAfD?P?b@h@?n@Cd@G\\\\ILAj@WdAo@v@e@^Ql@Y\\\\GXEX?F@@QBk@Dw@DmB?cAw@}D\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  public void planningFromNoThroughTrafficPlaceTest() {\n    var noThroughTrafficPlace = new GenericLocation(48.59634, 8.87020);\n    var destination = new GenericLocation(48.59463, 8.87218);\n\n    var polyline1 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, noThroughTrafficPlace, destination);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      polyline1,\n      \"corgHkncu@OEYUOMH?J?LINMNMHTDO@YMm@HS`A}BPGRWLYDEt@HJ@b@?Fc@DONm@t@OXCBz@B\\\\\"\n    );\n\n    var polyline2 = computePolyline(herrenbergGraph, destination, noThroughTrafficPlace);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      polyline2,\n      \"scrgH_zcu@C]C{@YBu@NOl@ENGb@c@?KAu@IEDMXSVQFaA|BIRLl@AXENIUOLOLMHK?I?NLXTND\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  private static String computePolyline(Graph graph, GenericLocation from, GenericLocation to) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n    request.setDateTime(dateTime);\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n\n    request.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n\n    var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, request);\n    final RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, temporaryVertices);\n\n    var gpf = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(5));\n    var paths = gpf.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n    GraphPathToItineraryMapper graphPathToItineraryMapper = new GraphPathToItineraryMapper(\n      ZoneId.of(\"Europe/Berlin\"),\n      graph.streetNotesService,\n      graph.ellipsoidToGeoidDifference\n    );\n\n    var itineraries = graphPathToItineraryMapper.mapItineraries(paths);\n    temporaryVertices.close();\n\n    // make sure that we only get CAR legs\n    itineraries.forEach(i ->\n      i.getLegs().forEach(l -> Assertions.assertEquals(l.getMode(), TraverseMode.CAR))\n    );\n    Geometry legGeometry = itineraries.get(0).getLegs().get(0).getLegGeometry();\n    return PolylineEncoder.encodeGeometry(legGeometry).points();\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/org/opentripplanner/routing/street/SplitEdgeTurnRestrictionsTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.routing.street;\n\nimport static org.opentripplanner.test.support.PolylineAssert.assertThatPolylinesAreEqual;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.locationtech.jts.geom.Geometry;\nimport org.opentripplanner.ConstantsForTests;\nimport org.opentripplanner.TestOtpModel;\nimport org.opentripplanner.model.GenericLocation;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.algorithm.mapping.GraphPathToItineraryMapper;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.RoutingContext;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TemporaryVerticesContainer;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseMode;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.core.TraverseModeSet;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.graph.Graph;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.impl.GraphPathFinder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.PolylineEncoder;\nimport org.opentripplanner.util.TestUtils;\n\n/*\n * When bus stops are added to graph they split an existing edge in two parts so that an artificial\n * intersection can be added and routes can be found to the station.\n *\n * During this process turn restrictions also need to be propagated from the old edges to the newly\n * created ones. This test makes sure that this happens correctly.\n */\npublic class SplitEdgeTurnRestrictionsTest {\n\n  static final Instant dateTime = TestUtils.dateInstant(\"Europe/Berlin\", 2020, 3, 3, 7, 0, 0);\n  // Deufringen\n  static final GenericLocation hardtheimerWeg = new GenericLocation(48.67765, 8.87212);\n  static final GenericLocation steinhaldenWeg = new GenericLocation(48.67815, 8.87305);\n  static final GenericLocation k1022 = new GenericLocation(48.67846, 8.87021);\n  // Böblingen\n  static final GenericLocation paulGerhardtWegEast = new GenericLocation(48.68363, 9.00728);\n  static final GenericLocation paulGerhardtWegWest = new GenericLocation(48.68297, 9.00520);\n  static final GenericLocation parkStrasse = new GenericLocation(48.68358, 9.00826);\n  static final GenericLocation herrenbergerStrasse = new GenericLocation(48.68497, 9.00909);\n  static final GenericLocation steinbeissWeg = new GenericLocation(48.68172, 9.00599);\n\n  @Test\n  void shouldTakeDeufringenTurnRestrictionsIntoAccount() {\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmAndGtfsGraph(\n      ConstantsForTests.DEUFRINGEN_OSM,\n      ConstantsForTests.VVS_BUS_764_ONLY\n    );\n    Graph graph = model.graph();\n    // https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/10264251 has a turn restriction so when leaving Hardtheimer Weg\n    // you must either turn right and take the long way to Steinhaldenweg or go past the intersection with the\n    // turn restriction and turn around.\n    // on top of this, it has a bus stop so this test also makes sure that the turn restrictions work\n    // even when the streets are split.\n    var noRightTurnPermitted = computeCarPolyline(graph, hardtheimerWeg, steinhaldenWeg);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      noRightTurnPermitted,\n      \"ijbhHuycu@g@Uq@[e@|BENGVYxA]xAYz@Yp@Yj@^n@JDN_@?Wa@i@Xq@X{@\\\\yAXyACGAIB]j@_DPaA@e@MDCB\"\n    );\n\n    // when to drive in reverse direction it's fine to go this way\n    var leftTurnOk = computeCarPolyline(graph, steinhaldenWeg, hardtheimerWeg);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(leftTurnOk, \"kmbhHo_du@BCLEAd@Q`Ak@~CC\\\\@HBFFWDOd@}Bp@Zf@T\");\n\n    // make sure that going straight on a straight-only turn direction also works\n    var straightAhead = computeCarPolyline(graph, hardtheimerWeg, k1022);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(straightAhead, \"ijbhHuycu@g@Uq@[e@|BENGVYxA]xAXn@Hd@\");\n\n    var straightAheadBack = computeCarPolyline(graph, k1022, hardtheimerWeg);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(straightAheadBack, \"kobhHwmcu@Ie@Yo@\\\\yAXyAFWDOd@}Bp@Zf@T\");\n\n    // make sure that turning left onto the minor road works even when the opposite direction has a straight-only\n    // restriction\n    var leftTurnAllowed = computeCarPolyline(graph, k1022, steinhaldenWeg);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(leftTurnAllowed, \"kobhHwmcu@Ie@Yo@\\\\yAXyACGAIB]j@_DPaA@e@MDCB\");\n\n    var rightTurnAllowed = computeCarPolyline(graph, steinhaldenWeg, k1022);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(rightTurnAllowed, \"kmbhHo_du@BCLEAd@Q`Ak@~CC\\\\@HBFYxA]xAXn@Hd@\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void shouldTakeBoeblingenTurnRestrictionsIntoAccount() {\n    // this tests that the following turn restriction is transferred correctly to the split edges\n    // https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/299171\n    TestOtpModel model = ConstantsForTests.buildOsmAndGtfsGraph(\n      ConstantsForTests.BOEBLINGEN_OSM,\n      ConstantsForTests.VVS_BUS_751_ONLY\n    );\n    var graph = model.graph();\n\n    // turning left from the main road onto a residential one\n    var turnLeft = computeCarPolyline(graph, parkStrasse, paulGerhardtWegEast);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      turnLeft,\n      \"kochHsl~u@HQL]N_@v@mBDKN]KKM\\\\{@~BKXWj@KRKPCFYj@DP^lAJX\"\n    );\n\n    // right hand turn out of the the residential road onto the main road, only right turn allowed plus there\n    // is a bus station along the way, splitting the edge\n    var noLeftTurnPermitted = computeCarPolyline(graph, paulGerhardtWegEast, parkStrasse);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(noLeftTurnPermitted, \"sochHof~u@KY_@mAVi@Te@DK\");\n\n    // right hand turn out of the the residential road onto the main road, only right turn allowed plus there\n    // is a bus station along the way, splitting the edge\n    var longWay = computeCarPolyline(graph, paulGerhardtWegEast, herrenbergerStrasse);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      longWay,\n      \"sochHof~u@KY_@mAVi@Te@N]L]N_@v@mBDKN]KKM\\\\{@~BKXWj@KRKPCFa@`@_@XWPSHQDMCEAQMKKSgAa@qCMe@\"\n    );\n\n    var longWayBack = computeCarPolyline(graph, herrenbergerStrasse, paulGerhardtWegEast);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      longWayBack,\n      \"axchHwq~u@G_@Qc@@UCMAK@Q@WTUh@eA@Cb@gANg@Nu@Lq@Fe@Da@Bo@Bq@BUD[Je@Li@DWFBHJt@bAFFTZLN@@d@j@|@lA`@r@\\\\r@z@tBLZ]TYX]`@e@z@Yp@GJM\\\\{@~BKXWj@KRKPCFYj@DP^lAJX\"\n    );\n\n    // test that you can correctly turn right here https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/415123 when approaching\n    // from south\n    var fromSouth = computeCarPolyline(graph, steinbeissWeg, paulGerhardtWegWest);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(fromSouth, \"wcchHk~}u@Fd@Hj@o@\\\\{@b@KFyBlAWmA\");\n    var toSouth = computeCarPolyline(graph, paulGerhardtWegWest, steinbeissWeg);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(toSouth, \"okchHoy}u@VlAxBmAJGz@c@n@]Ik@Ge@\");\n\n    // test that you cannot turn left here https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/415123 when approaching\n    // from north\n    var fromNorth = computeCarPolyline(graph, paulGerhardtWegWest, herrenbergerStrasse);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      fromNorth,\n      \"okchHoy}u@VlA{BlAIBOLCBIDc@{AYiAM_@Kc@K_@I_@Ia@Ga@Gc@Gc@Ei@EYAIKaAEe@CQCSIm@SgAa@qCMe@\"\n    );\n\n    // when you approach you cannot turn left so you have to take a long way\n    var toNorth = computeCarPolyline(graph, herrenbergerStrasse, paulGerhardtWegWest);\n    assertThatPolylinesAreEqual(\n      toNorth,\n      \"axchHwq~u@G_@Qc@@UCMAK@Q@WTUh@eA@Cb@gANg@Nu@Lq@Fe@Da@Bo@Bq@BUD[Je@Li@DWFBHJt@bAFFTZLN@@d@j@|@lA`@r@\\\\r@z@tBLZ]TYX]`@e@z@Yp@GJJJBDDDBBBFvAhCXv@Rp@`@lC@Ff@`D@HRpAJt@Hj@o@\\\\{@b@KFyBlAWmA\"\n    );\n  }\n\n  private static String computeCarPolyline(Graph graph, GenericLocation from, GenericLocation to) {\n    RouteRequest request = new RouteRequest();\n    request.setDateTime(dateTime);\n    request.setFrom(from);\n    request.setTo(to);\n\n    request.streetSubRequestModes = new TraverseModeSet(TraverseMode.CAR);\n\n    var temporaryVertices = new TemporaryVerticesContainer(graph, request);\n    RoutingContext routingContext = new RoutingContext(request, graph, temporaryVertices);\n\n    var gpf = new GraphPathFinder(null, Duration.ofSeconds(5));\n    var paths = gpf.graphPathFinderEntryPoint(routingContext);\n\n    GraphPathToItineraryMapper graphPathToItineraryMapper = new GraphPathToItineraryMapper(\n      ZoneId.of(\"Europe/Berlin\"),\n      graph.streetNotesService,\n      graph.ellipsoidToGeoidDifference\n    );\n\n    var itineraries = graphPathToItineraryMapper.mapItineraries(paths);\n    temporaryVertices.close();\n\n    // make sure that we only get CAR legs\n    itineraries.forEach(i ->\n      i.getLegs().forEach(l -> Assertions.assertEquals(l.getMode(), TraverseMode.CAR))\n    );\n    Geometry geometry = itineraries.get(0).getLegs().get(0).getLegGeometry();\n    return PolylineEncoder.encodeGeometry(geometry).points();\n  }\n}\n"

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test;\n\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.api.request.RaptorProfile.MIN_TRAVEL_DURATION;\nimport static org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.api.request.RaptorProfile.MIN_TRAVEL_DURATION_BEST_TIME;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneId;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RoutingTag;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test.model.SpeedTestProfile;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test.model.testcase.TestCase;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test.options.SpeedTestCmdLineOpts;\nimport org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test.options.SpeedTestConfig;\n\npublic class SpeedTestRequest {\n\n  private final TestCase testCase;\n  private final SpeedTestCmdLineOpts opts;\n  private final SpeedTestConfig config;\n  private final SpeedTestProfile profile;\n  private final ZoneId timeZoneId;\n\n  SpeedTestRequest(\n    TestCase testCase,\n    SpeedTestCmdLineOpts opts,\n    SpeedTestConfig config,\n    SpeedTestProfile profile,\n    ZoneId timeZoneId\n  ) {\n    this.testCase = testCase;\n    this.opts = opts;\n    this.config = config;\n    this.profile = profile;\n    this.timeZoneId = timeZoneId;\n  }\n\n  public TestCase tc() {\n    return testCase;\n  }\n\n  RouteRequest toRoutingRequest() {\n    var request = config.request.clone();\n    var input = testCase.definition();\n\n    if (input.departureTime() != TestCase.NOT_SET) {\n      request.setDateTime(time(input.departureTime()));\n      request.setArriveBy(false);\n      if (input.arrivalTime() != TestCase.NOT_SET) {\n        request.raptorOptions.withTimeLimit(time(input.arrivalTime()));\n      }\n    } else if (input.arrivalTime() != TestCase.NOT_SET) {\n      request.setDateTime(time(input.arrivalTime()));\n      request.setArriveBy(true);\n    }\n\n    if (input.window() != TestCase.NOT_SET) {\n      request.setSearchWindow(Duration.ofSeconds(input.window()));\n    }\n\n    request.setFrom(input.fromPlace());\n    request.setTo(input.toPlace());\n    request.setNumItineraries(opts.numOfItineraries());\n    request.modes = input.modes();\n\n    request.raptorOptions\n      .withProfile(profile.raptorProfile())\n      .withOptimizations(profile.optimizations())\n      .withSearchDirection(profile.direction());\n\n    if (profile.raptorProfile().isOneOf(MIN_TRAVEL_DURATION, MIN_TRAVEL_DURATION_BEST_TIME)) {\n      request.setSearchWindow(Duration.ZERO);\n    }\n\n    addDebugOptions(request, opts);\n    request.tags =\n      Set.of(\n        RoutingTag.testCaseSample(input.idAndDescription()),\n        RoutingTag.testCaseCategory(input.category())\n      );\n\n    return request;\n  }\n\n  private static void addDebugOptions(RouteRequest request, SpeedTestCmdLineOpts opts) {\n    request.raptorDebugging.withStops(opts.debugStops()).withPath(opts.debugPath());\n  }\n\n  private Instant time(int time) {\n    // Note time may be negative and exceed 24 hours\n    return config.testDate.atStartOfDay(timeZoneId).plusSeconds(time).toInstant();\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package org.opentripplanner.transit.raptor.speed_test.options;\n\nimport static org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.RoutingRequestMapper.mapRoutingRequest;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.net.URI;\nimport java.time.LocalDate;\nimport org.opentripplanner.routing.api.request.RouteRequest;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.ConfigLoader;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.NodeAdapter;\nimport org.opentripplanner.standalone.config.TransitRoutingConfig;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\npublic class SpeedTestConfig {\n\n  private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(SpeedTestConfig.class);\n  public static final String FILE_NAME = \"speed-test-config.json\";\n\n  private final JsonNode rawNode;\n\n  /**\n   * The test date is the date used for all test cases. The default value is today.\n   */\n  public final LocalDate testDate;\n\n  /** The speed test run all its test on an existing pre-build graph. */\n  public final URI graph;\n\n  public final String feedId;\n\n  public final TransitRoutingConfig transitRoutingParams;\n  public final RouteRequest request;\n\n  public SpeedTestConfig(JsonNode node) {\n    NodeAdapter adapter = new NodeAdapter(node, FILE_NAME);\n    this.rawNode = node;\n    testDate = adapter.asDateOrRelativePeriod(\"testDate\", \"PT0D\");\n    graph = adapter.asUri(\"graph\", null);\n    feedId = adapter.asText(\"feedId\", null);\n    transitRoutingParams = new TransitRoutingConfig(adapter.path(\"tuningParameters\"));\n    request = mapRoutingRequest(adapter.path(\"routingDefaults\"));\n  }\n\n  public static SpeedTestConfig config(File dir) {\n    var json = new ConfigLoader(dir).loadJsonByFilename(FILE_NAME);\n    SpeedTestConfig config = new SpeedTestConfig(json);\n    LOG.info(\"SpeedTest config loaded: \" + config);\n    return config;\n  }\n\n  @Override\n  public String toString() {\n    return rawNode.toPrettyString();\n  }\n}\n"
    }
